Method: 提出一套心理测量学工具箱，核心为"交叉对称化"方法：将每个逻辑无关因素以平衡配对方式翻转，跨越多种问题形式语料库构建等价格式。具体包括：(1) 用分级评分在逻辑等价形式间恢复内部道德尺度θ；(2) 通过强制是/否二元裁决揭示可分解的人为偏差（顺序偏差+词汇倾向）；(3) 将"是/否"替换为任意标签分离词汇与裁决；(4) 建立最小模型 P = σ((θ±m)/s)，用框架敏感性m和道德果断性s总结偏差。

Result: 前沿模型的道德立场θ几乎不受格式影响，跨形式不一致性仅0.12-0.21（在±1轴上）；小型开源模型以不同方式失效。强制二元裁决时，Claude模型存在显著人为偏差（故事平均-0.32至-0.86），而GPT-5.5和Gemini≈0，且在扩展推理下偏差缩小。用任意标签替代"是/否"后，与裁决相关的逻辑偏差在各前沿模型中均≈0，但模型特定的标签和顺序依附仍然存在。框架敏感性m与道德果断性s可测量地区别于采样温度。

Conclusion: 测量模型的价值观需要对问题的框架进行交叉验证，而非仅提问一次。交叉对称化方法可不变地应用于任何困境集合和二元格式，为评估LLM道德判断提供了可靠的心理测量学基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly issue judgments read as binary verdicts, and a growing literature reports such judgments shifting under logically irrelevant changes of wording - among them an amplified yes-no bias on moral dilemmas, absent in humans. A single framing cannot say what such a shift is: in a yes/no question the word "no" is at once logical verdict, lexical token, and last-printed option. We introduce a psychometric battery that separates these: crossed symmetrization - every logically irrelevant factor flipped in balanced pairs - across a corpus of question forms. A graded rating across logically equivalent forms recovers a coherent internal moral scale: frontier models' stance $θ$ is nearly format-invariant (cross-form incoherence 0.12-0.21 on a $\pm 1$ axis); small open-weight models fail in model-specific ways. Forcing the verdict through yes/no overlays a decomposable artifact: an order bias toward the last-printed option - opposite to classic human primacy - plus a lexical pull toward the word "no"; the artifact is substantial only in the Claude models (story-averaged -0.32 to -0.86), $\approx 0$ for GPT-5.5 and Gemini, and shrinks under extended reasoning. The word and the verdict share one token; swapping the words for arbitrary labels separates them, and the verdict-attached logical bias proves $\approx 0$ for every frontier model, while model-specific label and order attachments remain: the models are not drawn toward rejecting - the pull follows the printed surface, not the verdict it carries. A minimal model, $P = σ((θ\pm m)/s)$, summarizes any such artifact by a framing susceptibility m and a moral decisiveness s, measurably distinct from sampling temperature. The battery applies unchanged to any dilemma set and binary format: measuring what a model values requires crossing the frames of the question, not asking once.

</details>


### [103] [Prompt Robustness Is Task-Dependent: Comparing Objective and Belief-Style Questions in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05554)
*Sadia Kamal,Arefa Patwary,Anthony Marchiafava,Atriya Sen,Sagnik Ray Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了指令微调大语言模型在面对提示词变化时的回答鲁棒性，发现客观题（有固定答案）与主观题（询问意见/价值观）之间存在显著差异，提示鲁棒性取决于问题类型、提示变化方式和具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于调查问卷方式评估大语言模型价值观的做法，隐含地假设模型的提示回答能够稳定反映其"信念"或"价值观"。然而，这一假设极为脆弱，尤其是在解读政治价值观、社会态度等主观问题时。作者旨在检验：客观题与主观题在面对提示词扰动时，回答一致性是否存在系统性差异。

Method: 在四个指令微调模型家族上，使用三个客观数据集（MMLU、ARC、CulturalBench）和三个主观数据集（Political Compass Test、ValueBench、World Values Survey）。对每个问题/陈述施加多种类型的提示变化（措辞变化、框架变化、格式变化），测量模型在不同变体间是否给出一致答案。采用二项广义估计方程（binomial GEE）分析模型、数据集类型、提示变化类别及其交互效应。

Result: 模型、数据集类型、提示变化类别的主效应及其交互作用均显著。数据集类型（客观 vs. 主观）的主效应显著，且数据集类型与提示变化类别的交互效应较大。表明提示鲁棒性因问题类型、提示变化方式和模型而异。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型的提示回答直接解读为其稳定价值观或信念的做法存在严重方法论缺陷，尤其在主观类问题上。研究者应审慎对待基于提示的调查式评估，考虑提示鲁棒性对结论有效性的威胁。

Abstract: Survey-style evaluations of large language models often treat a prompted response as a measure of a model's values or beliefs. This assumption is particularly fragile when responses are read as evidence of political values, social attitudes, or beliefs. We ask whether prompt robustness differs between objective questions with fixed answers and subjective questions that ask for opinions or values. We evaluate four instruction-tuned model families on three objective datasets (MMLU, ARC, and CulturalBench) and three subjective datasets (Political Compass Test, ValueBench, and World Values Survey). For each question/statement, we apply multiple types of prompt changes, such as variations in wording, framing, and format, and measure whether the model gives the same answer across variants. Using a binomial generalized estimating equation, we find significant effects of model, dataset, prompt category, and their interactions. The dataset type effect is also significant, and the interaction between dataset type and prompt category is large. These results show that prompt robustness depends on the question type, the prompt change, and the model.

</details>


### [104] [ResonatorLM: Causal Resonant Field Mixing for Efficient Long-Context Language Modelin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05583)
*Archie Chaudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ResonatorLM，一种用物理启发的阻尼谐振子因果函数替代自注意力机制的新架构，在长上下文建模中实现显著推理加速（32K tokens时解码速度达Transformer的6.47倍）和更高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer、RNN、CNN等现有架构在处理长上下文时效率低下，自注意力机制的二次复杂度成为瓶颈，亟需一种更高效的长序列建模方案。

Method: ResonatorLM将token序列视为一维受驱隐式场（driven one-dimensional latent field），用阻尼谐振子的因果函数（causal functions of damped resonators）替代注意力点积运算，在传统网络架构上实现该机制。

Result: 在600万参数的匹配设置下，训练和预填充速度随序列长度增加而提升；32K tokens时解码速度达到标准优化Transformer的6.47倍；WikiText数据集上准确率达61.31%（Transformer为55.32%）。

Conclusion: ResonatorLM验证了物理启发的谐振子机制可有效替代自注意力，在长上下文场景中兼具效率与精度优势，为语言模型架构设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Contemporary language models are dominated by the transformer architecture, which leverages self-attention mechanisms to enable more efficient, parallelized training across a wide set of documents and corpora. This has allowed transformers to effectively model data across a wide range of modalities and contexts. However, transformers, along with their conventional counterparts such as recurrent neural networks (RNNs) and convolutional neural networks (CNNs), often struggle to maintain efficiency when processing long contexts. We introduce ResonatorLM, a new mechanism that replaces attention with a physics-derived alternative. ResonatorLM treats token sequences as a single, driven one-dimensional latent field and replaces attention dot products with causal functions of damped resonators. We implement ResonatorLM on a traditional network architecture and test it on standard long-context modeling tasks. We find that in a small, 6M matched setting, training and prefill speedups increase with sequence length, decode speed reaches 6.47x compared to that of a standard, optimized transformer at 32K tokens, and accuracy reaches 61.31 percent (compared to 55.32 percent) on WikiText.

</details>


### [105] [Revisiting the Relation Between Language Model Perplexity and ASR Word Error Rate for Modern End-to-End Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05612)
*Mohammad Zeineldeen,Albert Zeyer,Haoran Zhang,Robin Schmitt,Ralf Schlüter,Hermann Ney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了现代端到端ASR系统中语言模型困惑度(PPL)与词错误率(WER)之间的关系，发现传统log-log线性关系在引入内部语言建模、LLM及不同解码策略后发生变化，ILM减法是影响该关系的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为LM困惑度与ASR词错误率在log-log空间呈近似线性关系，但现代端到端ASR系统内置语言建模能力、常不使用外部LM评估，且可与神经LM和LLM通过不同策略结合，这些新变化挑战了原有假设，亟需重新审视PPL-WER关系。

Method: 通过在注意力机制的编码器-解码器端到端ASR系统上，系统研究以下问题：(1)外部LM是否仍能改进现代端到端ASR；(2)PPL-WER的log-log线性关系是否依然成立；(3)编码器上下文长度对该关系的影响；(4)LLM困惑度如何融入标准神经LM的趋势线；(5)内部语言建模(ILM)减法如何改变PPL-WER关系。

Result: 外部LM仍能提升端到端ASR性能；PPL-WER在log-log空间的线性关系在考虑ILM后发生变化；编码器上下文长度影响该关系；LLM困惑度表现出与标准神经LM不同的趋势；ILM减法是正确解读外部LM质量效应的关键——必须将解码器内部LM纳入考量。

Conclusion: 在现代端到端ASR系统中，PPL与WER的关系比传统认识更为复杂，不能简单沿用log-log线性假设。解码器内部语言建模的影响不可忽略，ILM减法是连接外部LM质量与ASR性能的关键环节，需在评估和系统设计中予以充分考虑。

Abstract: Language model (LM) perplexity (PPL) has historically been used as a proxy for automatic speech recognition (ASR) word error rate (WER), with prior work reporting an approximately linear relation in log-log space. Modern end-to-end ASR systems challenge this assumption because they already contain internal language modeling capacity, are often evaluated without external language models, and can now be combined with neural LMs and large language models (LLMs) through different recognition strategies. This paper revisits the relation between PPL and WER for modern ASR systems. We study whether external LMs still improve current end-to-end ASR systems, whether the PPL-WER relation remains linear in log-log space, how encoder context length affects this relation, and how LLM perplexities fit into the trend observed for standard neural LMs. We further investigate internal language modeling (ILM) in attention-based encoder-decoder systems and show that ILM subtraction changes the observed PPL-WER relation, indicating that the decoder's internal LM must be considered when interpreting the effect of external LM quality.

</details>


### [106] [BaFCo: A Document Understanding Benchmark for Complex Bangla Form Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05614)
*Abu Tyeb Azad,Ishita Sur Apan,Fahim Ahmed,Sumaiya Karim Katha,Ezharuddin Jubaer,Armun Alam,Pranjal Kumar Nandi,Amin Ahsan Ali,Aman Chadha,Md Mofijul Islam,AKM Mahbubur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BaFCo 是一个用于孟加拉语表单理解的基准数据集，包含200份多页政府表单，定义了26种细粒度和5种粗粒度实体类型。评估显示当前主流多模态大模型在理解孟加拉语表单方面仍存在局限性，尤其在精细实体定位上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在文档理解任务中日益重要，但孟加拉语等低资源语言因缺乏高质量标注数据而发展受限。现有基准数据集多聚焦于英语等主流语言，孟加拉语表单理解领域存在明显空白，亟需构建专用数据集来推动该方向研究。

Method: 作者构建了BaFCo数据集，从农业、教育、银行和土地管理等多个领域收集200份孟加拉国政府多页复杂表单；设计了一套包含26种细粒度实体类型和5种粗粒度实体类型的标注体系；在此数据集上，对ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude、Qwen、Kimi等最新MLLMs进行评估，采用零样本和思维链提示策略，分别在低推理和高推理两种设置下测试。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前主流多模态大语言模型在孟加拉语表单理解任务上存在明显局限性，尤其是在精确定位细粒度表单实体方面表现不足，揭示出低资源语言文档理解仍是一项未完全解决的挑战。

Conclusion: BaFCo数据集填补了孟加拉语表单理解领域的资源空白，为评估和提升MLLMs在低资源语言文档理解上的能力提供了重要基准。当前模型在细粒度实体定位上的不足，指明了未来改进方向。

Abstract: Document comprehension is a challenging yet impactful task for Multimodal Large Language Models, especially as these systems see growing adoption in real-world, human-centric applications. However, this adoption is limited for low-resource languages such as Bangla due to the scarcity of high-quality annotated data. To address this gap, we introduce BaFCo, a benchmark dataset for Bangla form comprehension with a focus on Document Layout Analysis (DLA) and Key Information Extraction (KIE). BaFCo curates 200 multi-page complex Bangladeshi government forms, sourced from across diverse sectors including agriculture, education, banking, and land management. To accurately capture the structural and contextual complexity of these forms, we define a fine-grained annotation schema comprising 26 types of form entities, along with a separate coarse form entity set consisting of 5 types. We evaluate the latest MLLMs from the ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, Qwen, and Kimi series using zero-shot and chain-of-thought prompts under both low and high reasoning setups. Our results reveal limitations in current MLLMs' ability in comprehending Bangla forms, particularly in accurately localizing highly granular form entities. Our dataset and code is available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Mausul/bafco

</details>


### [107] [NAVER LABS System Re-implementation for the IWSLT 2026 Instruction-Following Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05623)
*Anand Kamble,Aniket Tathe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文复现了NAVER LABS的IWSLT 2025指令遵循流程，适配IWSLT 2026共享任务（约束条件，短音频赛道），采用SeamlessM4T-v2-large作为语音编码器、Qwen3-4B-Instruct作为LLM骨干，保留三阶段训练流程，并构建了100k合成指令遵循数据。在MCIF基准上，英中语音翻译COMET达0.781，英语SQA的BERTScore-F1达0.346。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将IWSLT 2025的指令遵循流水线迁移适配到IWSLT 2026共享任务的约束条件（短音频、指定组件），验证该三阶段训练范式在新任务设定下的有效性。

Method: 采用三阶段训练策略：(1) 投影器对齐——对齐语音编码器与LLM之间的模态映射；(2) 仅文本LoRA预训练——在文本数据上微调LoRA适配器；(3) 多模态融合——整合语音与文本模态。同时利用提供的语料构建了10万条合成指令遵循样本，覆盖10种语音中心任务类型（每类1万条），用于第三阶段微调。

Result: 在MCIF基准上，主模型英中语音翻译COMET得分为0.781，英语语音问答(SQA)的BERTScore-F1为0.346。

Conclusion: NAVER LABS的三阶段指令遵循流水线可成功适配IWSLT 2026的约束条件，所构建的合成数据与定制的训练流程在语音翻译和语音问答任务上取得了初步有效的结果。

Abstract: We re-implement the NAVER LABS IWSLT 2025 instruction-following pipeline for the IWSLT 2026 Shared Task (constrained condition, short audio track), adapting it to the mandated components: SeamlessM4T-v2-large as the speech encoder and Qwen3-4B-Instruct as the LLM backbone. The three-stage approach projector alignment, text-only LoRA pre-training, and multimodal merging is preserved from the original design. We additionally construct 100k synthetic instruction-following examples across ten speech-centric task types (10k per task) from the provided corpora, suitable for further Stage 3 fine-tuning. Our primary model achieves COMET 0.781 on EN-ZH speech translation and BERTScore-F1 0.346 on English SQA on the MCIF benchmark.

</details>


### [108] [Population-Level Profiling of DSM-5 Depressive Symptoms Among Self-Reported ADHD and ASD Users on Twitter: An Exploratory Study Using Advanced NLP and Statistical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05626)
*Muhammad Rizwan,David Nabergoj,Jure Demšar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用社交媒体文本分析，发现ADHD和ASD用户在表达DSM-5抑郁症状时存在稳定但有限的群体层面差异，但这些差异仅反映可重复性而非临床有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症常与ADHD和ASD共病，但群体层面上这两类人群在抑郁症状表达方面的差异尚未得到充分探索。现有研究缺乏利用大规模自然语言数据比较ADHD与ASD患者抑郁症状表达差异的工作。

Method: 收集792名自报诊断的Twitter用户（ADHD 622人，ASD 170人）的1,282,437条推文。首先使用零样本自然语言推理（NLI）对推文进行抑郁相关性预过滤，然后利用在ReDSM5数据集上微调的MentalRoBERTa模型将推文分类为九种DSM-5抑郁症状。对每位用户进行均值中心化处理后，采用L1正则化逻辑回归与交叉验证区分ADHD和ASD用户，辅以Pearson相关分析考察症状共现结构，并通过五种过滤阈值的自助抽样（bootstrapping）检验结果的稳健性。

Result: MentalRoBERTa在保留测试集上达到0.901的宏F1分数，优于原始ReDSM5基准。ADHD vs ASD分类表现稳定但一般（交叉验证ROC-AUC为0.645–0.653）。认知问题、睡眠问题、食欲变化和疲劳更偏向ADHD，而自杀意念和快感缺乏更偏向ASD。两组之间呈现出大致共享的症状共现结构，没有任何症状对满足稳健的障碍特异性差异标准。

Conclusion: ADHD和ASD社交媒体用户在抑郁相关语言使用上存在稳定但有限的群体层面差异，这些差异在不同过滤阈值下可重复出现，反映的是可重复性而非临床有效性。研究结果属于探索性质，不能确立个体层面的现象学差异。

Abstract: Background: Depression frequently co-occurs with ADHD and autism spectrum disorder (ASD), but population-level differences in symptom expression between these groups remain underexplored. Objective: We examined whether social media users with ADHD and ASD differ in how they express DSM-5 depressive symptoms in their tweets, and whether differences persist across varying levels of depressive-content filtering. Methods: We analysed 1,282,437 tweets from 792 users (622 ADHD; 170 ASD) with self-reported diagnoses on Twitter. Tweets were pre-filtered for depressive relevance using zero-shot NLI, then classified into nine DSM-5 symptoms using MentalRoBERTa fine-tuned on ReDSM5. Profiles were mean-centered per user. We applied L1-penalised logistic regression with cross-validation to distinguish ADHD from ASD users, complemented by Pearson correlations for symptom co-occurrence, and tested robustness across five filtering thresholds using bootstrapping. Results: MentalRoBERTa achieved macro-F1 of 0.901 on a held-out set, outperforming the original ReDSM5 benchmark. ADHD vs ASD classification yielded stable but modest performance (cross-validated ROC-AUC 0.645-0.653). Cognitive issues, sleep issues, appetite change, and fatigue leaned toward ADHD, while suicidal ideation and anhedonia leaned toward ASD. A largely shared symptom co-occurrence structure emerged between groups; no pair met our criterion for a robust disorder-specific difference. Conclusions: Population-level differences in depression-related language between ADHD and ASD social media users were consistently observed across thresholds, reflecting reproducibility rather than clinical validity. Findings are exploratory and do not establish differing phenomenology at the individual level.

</details>


### [109] [Do It Right! A Methodology for Successful NLP System Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05644)
*Olga V. Patterson,Brett South,T. Elizabeth Workman,Scott L DuVall*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将系统开发生命周期（SDLC）框架应用于自然语言处理（NLP）项目的分步方法，强调成功的NLP项目不仅依赖算法知识，还需要系统化的项目管理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献和教程多聚焦于NLP的具体算法与应用，但忽略了NLP项目成功所需的其他关键要素，即缺乏一个系统化的项目管理框架来指导从电子病历中提取信息的NLP项目。

Method: 基于现有文献，将系统开发生命周期（SDLC）模型的各阶段（规划、分析、设计、实施、维护等）映射并适配到NLP数据提取项目中，形成一套分步式方法指南。

Result: 论文提出了一个适用于NLP项目的SDLC分步框架，涵盖了从项目规划到长期维护的完整生命周期，弥补了仅关注算法而忽视流程管理的空白。

Conclusion: 成功的临床NLP项目需要将算法知识与系统化的开发管理流程相结合，SDLC框架为NLP项目的规划、执行和维护提供了结构化指导，有助于提升项目的整体成功率。

Abstract: Natural language processing (NLP) is a common method for supplying data to clinical research and decision making by extracting information from electronic medical records. Numerous textbooks and tutorials describe specific algorithms and applications for text processing, yet algorithmic knowledge is only one ingredient of a successful NLP project. Drawing on the available literature, this paper presents a stepwise approach that applies the Systems Development Life Cycle (SDLC) to projects that rely on data extraction through language processing.

</details>


### [110] [RPAM: A Principled Metric for Evaluating Associations in Language Models with High Predictive Validity in Downstream Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05679)
*Damian Hodel,Jevin West,Aylin Caliskan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了相对概率关联度量（RPAM），一种针对生成式语言模型的上游关联评估指标。实验表明，RPAM 在多个模型和数据集上与人类的内隐/外显关联及下游偏差测量之间存在强相关性，优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的下游指标依赖特定评估数据集，泛化性受限；而上游指标虽能跨模型进行原则性分析，但此前无一上游指标能在生成式 LM 中揭示其与真实世界关联（含下游生成文本中测量的关联）之间的强关系。该空白亟待填补。

Method: 提出 RPAM（Relative Probability Association Metric），基于嵌入或续写概率在基础层面评估生成式 LM 中的关联。在 Mistral-7B-Instruct、Mistral-7B、GPT-2 三个不同质量和用途的模型上，使用 WEAT-WS、Bellezza、WS-353、SST2 等成熟评估数据集进行验证。

Result: RPAM 上游测量值与人类的内隐/外显关联以及下游特定任务的偏差测量之间均呈现强相关性，在可比较的情况下超越了此前的记录值。

Conclusion: RPAM 作为上游关联度量，能有效桥接生成式 LM 内部表征与真实世界关联及下游偏差，为偏差分析与缓解提供了更具泛化性和原则性的评估工具。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) exhibit problematic biases, such as stereotypes. Effectively analyzing and mitigating such biases requires accurate and generalizable evaluation methods of the underlying associations. Some existing approaches focus on downstream metrics that analyze associations in generated text. Since generated text content can vary drastically across LMs, such metrics often require specialized evaluation datasets, which limits the generalization of such downstream metrics. In contrast, upstream metrics examine LMs at the fundamental level of embeddings or continuation probabilities, enabling principled association analyses across LMs. Yet, to date, no upstream metric for generative LMs has uncovered a strong relationship with real-world associations, including those measured in generated text. To address this gap, we introduce the Relative Probability Association Metric (RPAM), an association evaluation metric for generative LMs. For three LMs of different quality of language generation and purpose (Mistral-7B-Instruct, Mistral-7B, and GPT-2) and well-studied evaluation datasets (WEAT-WS, Bellezza, WS-353, and SST2), we find a strong relationship between upstream RPAM measurements and corresponding implicit and explicit associations observed in humans, as well as biases measured downstream with LM-specific tasks, outperforming prior record values where applicable.

</details>


### [111] [UCSC NLP at SemEval-2026 Task 10: Boundary-Aware Span Extraction and RoBERTa Classification for Conspiracy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05689)
*Dom Marhoefer,Milos Suvakovic,Glenn Grant-Richards,Aidan Pinero,Ryan King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了针对SemEval-2026任务10（PsyCoMark）的两套系统：子任务1采用基于IoU≥0.95正标签、硬负采样和基于包含关系的非极大抑制（NMS）的多标签跨度分类方法进行阴谋论标记提取；子任务2采用带标签平滑和分层划分的序列分类器进行文档级阴谋论检测。最终在子任务1排名第7（宏F1: 0.2251），子任务2排名第11（加权F1: 0.7694）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与SemEval-2026 Task 10（PsyCoMark），该任务聚焦于阴谋论文本分析，需要解决两个子任务：从文本中精确提取阴谋论相关标记（如行动者、受害者、行动、效果、证据等角色），以及在文档层面判断文本是否包含阴谋论。该任务对理解阴谋论传播机制、开发自动检测工具具有现实意义。

Method: 子任务1（标记提取）：将任务建模为对枚举候选跨度的多标签跨度分类问题。核心策略包括：①使用IoU≥0.95作为正标签匹配标准；②引入硬负采样增强训练；③设计基于包含关系（containment-based）的非极大抑制（NMS）配合边界感知的跨度表示进行后处理。子任务2（文档级检测）：独立建模，使用序列分类器，结合标签平滑（label smoothing）和分层训练-验证集划分（stratified split）。

Result: 分析表明，实体类角色（行动者Actor、受害者Victim）检测较为鲁棒，而抽象角色（行动Action、效果Effect、证据Evidence）对边界标准敏感。官方测试集结果：子任务1宏F1为0.2251，排名第7/若干；子任务2加权F1为0.7694，排名第11/若干。

Conclusion: 所提出的跨度分类框架在阴谋论标记提取中有效，尤其对实体类角色检测表现稳定；但抽象角色的边界判定仍是难点。文档级分类性能中等偏上，两子任务均有进一步优化空间。

Abstract: We present our systems for SemEval-2026 Task 10 (PsyCoMark), addressing conspiracy marker extraction (Subtask 1) and document-level conspiracy detection (Subtask 2). For marker extraction, we formulate the task as multi-label span classification over enumerated candidate spans, using IoU >= 0.95 positive labeling, hard-negative sampling, and containment-based non-maximum suppression (NMS) with boundary-aware span representations. Document classification is modeled independently using a sequence classifier with label smoothing and a stratified train-validation split. Analysis shows that entity-like roles (Actor, Victim) are detected robustly, while abstract roles (Action, Effect, Evidence) remain sensitive to boundary criteria. On the official test set, our systems rank 7th in Subtask 1 (0.2251 macro F1) and 11th in Subtask 2 (0.7694 weighted F1).

</details>


### [112] [Where to cut, how deep: BPE and Unigram-LM on chemistry SMILES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05691)
*Hunter Heidenreich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在化学语言模型（SMILES）中，BPE和Unigram-LM两种分词算法在相同条件下构建出几乎不相交的子词词汇表，且Unigram-LM的分词粒度始终更细，表明分词算法是一项关键建模决策而非可随意默认的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学语言模型普遍从自然语言处理中继承BPE分词，但缺乏对该选择在化学领域适用性的审视。自然语言中Unigram-LM与BPE构建出结构不同的词汇表，但这一差异是否在化学领域成立尚不明确。

Method: 在固定165词元化学基础集上，对BPE和Unigram-LM进行受控比较。涵盖三种语料类型（多样性、类药性、天然产物）、两种预分词边界策略，以及从小词汇量到八倍规模的多种条件，共22组匹配比较。

Result: 两种算法在所有条件下构建出近乎不相交的子词词汇表——跨算法Jaccard重叠系数从未超过0.161，高频词元加权后不超过0.05。Unigram-LM将分子分割成多出29-41%的词元。两者在"切开位置"上大体一致，但Unigram-LM切得更深，BPE的分割在80-99%的分子上是Unigram-LM的严格粗化。

Conclusion: 分词算法的选择不是免费默认项，而是一项实质性建模决策。该差异在语料、边界策略和词汇量规模上均稳健存在，且不依赖于是否实际训练语言模型。

Abstract: Every chemical language model reading SMILES begins with a tokenizer, yet the field has inherited byte-pair encoding (BPE) from natural language with little scrutiny. In natural language, BPE's principal alternative, Unigram-LM, is known to build structurally different vocabularies. Whether that contrast survives in chemistry was open. We report a controlled comparison of BPE and Unigram-LM over a fixed 165-token chemistry base, at the small vocabulary sizes where token embeddings are learnable, across three corpus typologies (diverse, drug-like, natural-products) and both pre-tokenization boundary policies. The two do not converge. In all 22 matched conditions they build near-disjoint subword vocabularies: cross-algorithm Jaccard overlap on the learned pieces never exceeds 0.161, and at most 0.05 once weighted toward the high-frequency pieces a model updates most. Unigram-LM also segments held-out molecules into 29-41% more tokens; the arms largely agree on where to cut but not how deeply, so BPE's segmentation is a strict coarsening of Unigram-LM's on 80-99% of molecules. The separation holds across corpus, boundary, and vocabulary size, persisting even at eight times that scale. The subword algorithm is therefore a modeling decision, not a free default. The study trains no language models.

</details>


### [113] [SpanUQ: Span-Level Uncertainty Quantification for Large Language Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05721)
*Yimeng Zhang,Yingying Zhuang,Ziyi Wang,Yuxuan Lu,Pei Chen,Aman Gupta,Zhe Su,Ming Tan,Zhilin Zhang,Qun Liu,Manikandarajan Ramanathan,Rajashekar Maragoud,Edward Vul,Jing Huang,Dakuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Span-Level Uncertainty Estimation (SLUE)任务，在语义连贯的文本片段层面估计不确定性；设计SPANUQ轻量探针，通过DETR风格解码器和Beta混合分布，将多采样推理的不确定性知识蒸馏到单次前向传播中，在速度和精度上全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性估计存在粒度缺陷：token级别缺乏语义连贯性，序列级别无法精确定位错误。亟需在语义连贯的文本片段（span）这一自然粒度上进行不确定性估计，以支撑可信部署和自我修正。

Method: 提出SPANUQ：一个轻量级探针，将昂贵多采样推理的不确定性知识蒸馏到单次前向传播中。核心包括：(1) DETR风格span解码器同时检测span并估计不确定性；(2) 采用Beta混合分布建模不确定性；(3) 联合Beta NLL回归和对比排序目标进行训练。构建首个片段级不确定性基准SPANUQ-BENCH（20K提示、293K标注span、多采样声明验证软标签）。

Result: 在5个LLM骨干上，SPANUQ的span级不确定性质量始终最优，超越最强探针基线和所有采样方法，速度提升10-20倍。DETR span检测器F1达0.910，超最佳启发式方法39.4%。方法在2个模型家族、5个LLM上均泛化良好。

Conclusion: SLUE任务填补了token级与序列级不确定性估计之间的空白；SPANUQ以高效单次前向传播实现精确的片段级不确定性估计和错误定位，为LLM可信部署和自我修正提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is essential not only for the trustworthy deployment of large language models (LLMs) but also as a foundation for self-refinement in LLM generation. However, existing approaches operate at suboptimal granularities: token-level scores lack semantic coherence, while sequence-level scores fail to localize errors. We formalize Span-Level Uncertainty Estimation (SLUE), a new task that targets the natural granularity for uncertainty: semantically coherent text spans, each conveying a single assessable unit of meaning. To address this task, we introduce SPANUQ, a lightweight probe that distills the uncertainty knowledge from expensive multi-sample inference into a single forward pass over LLM hidden states. SPANUQ employs a DETR-style span decoder to simultaneously detect spans and estimate their uncertainty via a Mixture of Beta distribution, trained with a principled combination of Beta NLL regression and contrastive ranking objectives. We construct SPANUQ-BENCH, the first span-level uncertainty benchmark comprising 20K prompts, 293K annotated spans, and continuous soft labels derived from multi-sample claim verification. Experiments on five LLM backbones show that SPANUQ consistently achieves the best span-level uncertainty quality, outperforming the strongest probe baseline and all sampling-based methods while being 10-20x faster. Its DETR-based span detector attains 0.910 F1, surpassing the best heuristic by 39.4%, enabling precise error localization that sequence-level methods cannot provide. The framework generalizes across five LLMs spanning two model families.

</details>


### [114] [Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion: A Tri-Mode Language Model Unifying Autoregressive, Diffusion, and Self-Speculation Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05722)
*Yonggan Fu,Lexington Whalen,Abhinav Garg,Chengyue Wu,Maksim Khadkevich,Nicolai Oswald,Enze Xie,Daniel Egert,Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas,Shizhe Diao,Chenhan Yu,Ye Yu,Weijia Chen,Sajad Norouzi,Jingyu Liu,Shiyi Lan,Ligeng Zhu,Jin Wang,Jindong Jiang,Morteza Mardani,Mehran Maghoumi,Song Han,Ante Jukić,Nima Tajbakhsh,Jan Kautz,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion 是一个三模式语言模型，在单一架构中统一了自回归（AR）、扩散和自推测解码，通过联合训练目标实现了高速吞吐，其8B版本在单次前向传播中比Qwen3-8B多解码6倍token，吞吐量提升4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型面临推理效率瓶颈：自回归解码受限于逐token生成的低并行度，而扩散模型虽可并行生成但缺乏语言先验。该工作旨在融合两者优势，并通过自推测解码机制进一步提升吞吐量，以适应不同部署场景和并发级别。

Method: 提出三模式架构：1) 联合AR-扩散训练目标，使模型同时掌握左到右的语言先验和全局规划能力；2) 自推测解码模式中，扩散分支负责草稿生成，AR分支负责验证，实现高效推测解码；3) 扩展到3B、8B、14B参数规模，覆盖基础模型、指令微调模型和视觉语言模型。

Result: 1) AR与扩散目标互补：扩散改善前瞻规划，AR提供语言先验；2) 自推测模式下扩散草稿+AR验证的接受率和实际设备效率均优于多token预测（MTP）方法；3) 最优采样器下的光速分析显示扩散每次前向可多生成76.5%的token；4) Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-8B每次前向解码token数是Qwen3-8B的6倍，在SPEED-Bench上吞吐量达4倍（SGLang/GB200）。

Conclusion: Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion 验证了AR与扩散目标可在单一模型中互补共存，三模式设计在保持精度的同时大幅提升推理吞吐量，展示了扩散方法在大规模语言模型推理加速中的长期潜力，为高效部署提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: We introduce Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion, a tri-mode language model (LM) that unifies AR, diffusion, and self-speculation decoding within a single architecture. Trained with a joint AR-diffusion objective, Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion can switch modes to sustain high throughput across deployment settings and concurrency levels. Our study shows that (1) AR and diffusion objectives are complementary: diffusion improves lookahead planning, while AR provides left-to-right linguistic priors. (2) In self-speculation mode, diffusion drafts while AR verifies, outperforming multi-token prediction (MTP) methods in both acceptance rate and real-device efficiency. (3) A speed-of-light analysis further demonstrates diffusion's long-term potential, with up to 76.5% more tokens per forward pass than self-speculation under an optimal sampler. Scaling to 3B, 8B, and 14B parameters, our Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion family, including base, instruct, and vision-language models, consistently outperforms state-of-the-art open-source AR and diffusion LMs in both accuracy and speed. For example, Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-8B decodes 6x more tokens per forward than Qwen3-8B with comparable accuracy, translating to 4x higher throughput on SPEED-Bench with SGLang on a GB200 GPU.

</details>


### [115] [When Should LLMs Search? Counterfactual Supervision for Search Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05752)
*Minho Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将检索增强语言模型中的搜索调用问题形式化为实例级搜索路由问题，通过构建"不搜索/强制搜索"对比的 Oracle 监督信号，结合监督微调和偏好优化训练路由策略，在 Gemma 和 Qwen 模型上显著提升了路由 F1 分数，并揭示了不同模型的差异化路由失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强并非始终有益：模型可能对已知问题冗余调用搜索，或对噪声证据盲从，而不做纠正、澄清或拒答。因此需要一种实例级的智能搜索路由机制，按需决定是否搜索。

Method: 将问题形式化为三分类路由决策（NO SEARCH / SEARCH / UNSOLVED）。通过比较同一问题的无搜索与强制搜索结果，基于任务成功率构建 Oracle 标签，以此作为评估标准和学习信号。利用监督微调（SFT）和偏好优化训练路由策略。

Result: 在 Oracle 合格实例上，路由宏 F1 显著提升：Gemma E2B 从 0.7082 升至 0.8235，Qwen3.5-4B 从 0.7053 升至 0.8365。Gemma 主要学会了抑制不必要的搜索，Qwen 则进一步减少了漏搜；剩余 UNSOLVED 案例揭示了模型能力、检索预算、证据利用和策略行为等多维瓶颈。

Conclusion: 基于 Oracle 监督的搜索路由策略训练能有效提升路由决策质量，且不同基础模型呈现出差异化的改进路径。实例级路由有助于在检索增强系统中实现更精准、更节制的知识调用。

Abstract: Search-augmented language models can use external evidence to compensate for limitations in parametric knowledge, but search is not uniformly beneficial: models may call search for questions they can already answer, or rely on noisy evidence when correction, clarification, or abstention would be more appropriate. We formulate this as an instance-level search-routing problem: deciding whether search is needed to improve task success relative to a no-search execution. To derive supervision, we compare no-search and forced-search outcomes for the same question and construct an oracle over NO SEARCH, SEARCH, and UNSOLVED based on task-specific success. Using this oracle as both an evaluation criterion and a learning signal, we train search-routing policies with supervised fine-tuning and preference optimization, improving routing macro-F1 on oracle-eligible examples from 0.7082 to 0.8235 for Gemma E2B and from 0.7053 to 0.8365 for Qwen3.5-4B. Further analysis shows that the learned policies reduce model-specific routing failures: Gemma primarily learns no-search restraint, while Qwen further reduces missed search; residual UNSOLVED cases reveal heterogeneous bottlenecks involving model capacity, retrieval budget, evidence use, and policy behavior.

</details>


### [116] [Inject or Navigate? Token-Efficient Retrieval for LLM Analysis of Transactional Legal Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05764)
*Mahmoud Hany,Mourad ElSheraey,Mahmoud Said,Peter Naoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在法律文档问答系统中，用结构化检索替代全量语料注入LLM上下文窗口：语义检索+重排序在18个文档类问题中与全量注入持平16个，同时减少17.3倍输入token；结构化索引导航模式在全部18个问题上持平，且总token减少1.61倍、回答上下文缩小约56倍、成本降低25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全量语料注入（将整个文档集塞入LLM上下文窗口）虽然召回率最高，但其token开销随语料规模线性增长，且长上下文会带来性能退化。作者试图探究能否用结构化检索方法替代全量注入，在保持回答质量的同时显著降低token消耗和成本。

Method: 作者基于自研的结构感知分块（structure-aware chunking），对比了三种模式：（1）基线：全量语料注入；（2）NAVEMBED：嵌入检索+重排序，使用通用文本嵌入（GTE）进行语义检索；（3）NAVINDEX：LLM在紧凑的结构化索引上导航。在包含20个问题（18个文档绑定问题+2个超出范围对照问题）且具有验证过标准答案的基准上，采用位置偏差控制、参考锚定的成对评判方式进行评估。

Result: NAVEMBED在18个文档类问题中与全量注入持平16个（注入在2个问题上更优），输入token减少17.3倍（GTE配置可达29.9倍，但持平率略低）；2个超出范围对照问题上双方持平。NAVINDEX在全部18个问题上与注入持平，总token减少1.61倍，回答上下文缩小约56倍，美元成本降低25%。作者还推导了一个缓存交叉规则：仅当语料规模低于检索有效载荷约10倍时，缓存的注入模式在美元成本上更优。

Conclusion: 结构化检索可以在法律文档问答场景中有效替代全量语料注入，在保持回答质量的同时大幅降低token消耗和运营成本。NAVINDEX模式在成本和效率上表现尤为突出。

Abstract: Answering questions over a set of transactional legal documents is most simply done by injecting the whole corpus into the LLM's context window on every query. That baseline maximises retrieval recall, but its token footprint scales with the corpus rather than the question, and long-context degradation scales with it. We report what it took to replace full-corpus injection in a legal-document analysis system, comparing it against two structured retrieval modes over our proprietary structure-aware chunking: embedding retrieval (NAVEMBED) and LLM navigation over a compact structured index (NAVINDEX). On a 20-question benchmark with verified ground-truth answers, a position-bias-controlled, reference-anchored pairwise judge scored semantic retrieval with reranking tied with injection on 16 of 18 document-bound questions (injection preferred on 2) while attending to 17.3x fewer input tokens (a general-text-embedding (GTE) configuration reaches 29.9x at a lower tie rate); both modes were judged tied on the 2 out-of-scope controls. NAVINDEX was judged tied on all 18 at a 1.61x smaller total token footprint, a ~56x smaller answering context, and 25% lower dollar cost. We derive a closed-form caching-crossover rule: cached injection is cheaper in dollars only while the corpus stays below roughly ten times the retrieval payload. Scope and uncertainty are quantified in Section 8.

</details>


### [117] [CoPiT: Cognitive Pivot Translation for Digraphic Low-Resource Mongolian in the Traditional Script](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05849)
*Burte Bayarsaikhan,Serynn Kim,Buru Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CoPiT——一种认知启发的枢轴翻译流水线，通过将传统蒙古文先转写为西里尔蒙古文再翻译，有效解决了因传统蒙文数据稀缺和正字法歧义导致的翻译性能低下问题，在多个模型和语言对上显著超越直接翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙古语是双文种语言（传统蒙文和西里尔蒙文），但两者数据资源严重不均衡：西里尔蒙文资源相对丰富，传统蒙文数据极度稀缺且正字法存在歧义，导致直接翻译性能大幅下降。现有方法未能有效利用这一内部资源层级来提升传统蒙文的机器翻译质量。

Method: 提出CoPiT（Cognitively motivated Pivot-based Translation），一种路由翻译策略：先将传统蒙文文本转写为西里尔蒙文（显式消解文种引发的歧义），再以西里尔蒙文为枢轴翻译到目标语言。此外，利用该流水线可从传统蒙文文本直接构建合成平行语料，缓解数据稀缺问题。同时发布覆盖蒙英、蒙韩、蒙俄的多文种平行数据集。

Result: 在多个骨干模型和目标语言上，CoPiT一致优于直接翻译，取得显著的绝对BLEU提升以及1.5-1.6倍的COMET增益；强开源模型在可比评估设置下可匹敌或超越GPT-4.1。

Conclusion: CoPiT通过利用语言内部的文种资源层级，以认知合理的枢轴策略有效解决了传统蒙古文翻译中的数据稀缺和正字法歧义问题，不仅在推理阶段带来显著提升，还能通过合成数据缓解低资源场景的数据瓶颈。

Abstract: Low-resource languages remain challenging for machine translation, and Mongolian is a representative case. As a digraphic language, Mongolian is written in both Cyrillic and Traditional scripts, which exhibit a severe imbalance in data availability. While the Cyrillic script is relatively well-resourced, the Traditional script remains extremely data-scarce and orthographically ambiguous, leading to substantial performance degradation in direct translation. We propose CoPiT, a cognitively motivated pivot-based translation pipeline that exploits this internal resource hierarchy by routing translation through the Cyrillic script. The pipeline explicitly resolves script-induced ambiguity in the Traditional script before translation, enabling more stable and accurate meaning transfer. Across multiple backbone models and target languages, CoPiT consistently outperforms direct translation, achieving substantial absolute BLEU improvements together with consistent 1.5-1.6x COMET gains. These gains allow strong open-source models to match or outperform GPT-4.1 under comparable evaluation settings. Beyond inference-time improvements, CoPiT enables the construction of synthetic parallel data directly from Traditional-script text, mitigating data scarcity in realistic low-resource scenarios. We release a new multi-script parallel dataset covering Mongolian in both scripts alongside English, Korean, and Russian. All datasets and code are publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/anonymous_project-76C7.

</details>


### [118] [Mitigating Factual Hallucination in Large Reasoning Models via Mixed-Mode Advantage Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05861)
*Kaishen Wang,Tong Zheng,Xuehao Cui,Ruibo Chen,Tianyi Xiong,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大推理模型在事实性QA中虽能通过显式思维链提升整体表现，但存在"思维诱导幻觉"——显式推理反而推翻原本正确的直觉答案。本文将其建模为"思维残差"，并提出MARGO框架，通过混合模式优势正则化的强化学习，利用非思维rollout作为参考来抑制有害推理、保留有益推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRMs）的显式思维链在事实性问答中并非均匀有益：虽然整体上能通过恢复相关知识提升性能，但在实例层面，显式推理有时会推翻原本正确的直接答案，导致事实漂移（即"思维诱导幻觉"）。现有方法缺乏对这种失败模式的系统性理解和针对性缓解机制。

Method: 提出MARGO（Mixed-Mode Advantage Regularization for Grounded Optimization）框架：（1）将显式思维形式化为"思维残差"——即模型直接回答倾向之上的增量，该残差既可恢复缺失知识也可能引入无依据关联；（2）构建混合模式rollout组，同时包含思维和非思维轨迹；（3）以非思维rollout作为同模型参考进行优势估计，评估显式思维相对于直接回答是否带来事实性增益；（4）基于此设计强化学习目标，抑制诱发幻觉的思维模式，同时保留有益的推理行为。

Result: 在多个事实性QA基准上，MARGO相比强基线方法显著提升了事实可靠性；同时在数学推理基准上的评估表明，该方法能够保持模型的通用推理能力不退化。

Conclusion: 显式思维链在事实性QA中存在"思维诱导幻觉"这一重要失败模式；通过将其建模为思维残差，并利用混合模式优势正则化进行强化学习优化，可以在不损害通用推理能力的前提下有效提升事实可靠性。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) improve language model capabilities by generating explicit thinking traces before final answers. In factuality-oriented question answering (QA), such thinking often improves overall performance by helping the model recover relevant knowledge and refine its answers. However, we find that this benefit is not uniform at the instance level: explicit thinking can also overturn correct non-thinking answers and lead to factual drift. We refer to this failure mode as \emph{thinking-induced hallucination}. To explain this phenomenon, we formulate explicit thinking in factuality QA as a thinking residual over the model's direct-answer tendency, which can either recover missing knowledge or introduce unsupported associations. Based on this formulation, we propose MARGO, \underline{\textit{M}}ixed-Mode \underline{\textit{A}}dvantage \underline{\textit{R}}egularization for \underline{\textit{G}}rounded \underline{\textit{O}}ptimization, a reinforcement learning framework that uses non-thinking rollouts as same-model references in advantage estimation. By constructing mixed-mode rollout groups with both thinking and non-thinking trajectories, MARGO evaluates whether explicit thinking adds factual value beyond direct answering, thereby suppressing hallucination-prone thinking while preserving beneficial thinking behaviors. Experiments across multiple factuality-oriented QA benchmarks demonstrate that MARGO improves factual reliability over strong baselines, while evaluations on mathematical benchmarks show that it preserves general reasoning ability.

</details>


### [119] [Is Domain Adaptation Always Helpful? A Frozen-Backbone Study of Cross-Domain Sentiment Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05937)
*Phat Tran,Artin Lahni,Pranav Kulkarni,Yaolun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验评估了冻结预训练语言模型在情感分析中显式领域自适应的实际收益，发现其效果高度依赖于骨干模型是否已具备目标领域知识：对已覆盖的领域几乎无增益，对未覆盖领域可恢复显著性能，且对抗性对齐可能损害领域专用模型的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前以冻结PLM为骨干进行情感分析已成主流范式，但显式领域自适应的实际收益尚不明确——尤其当不同骨干模型编码了不同程度的目标领域知识时。本文旨在通过初步案例研究填补这一空白。

Method: 采用受控的冻结嵌入骨干家族（Qwen3-Embedding 0.6B/4B/8B、RoBERTa-base、FinBERT），在消费者评论数据上训练轻量级MLP适配器，分别使用领域对抗神经网络（DANN）、最大均值差异（MMD）和监督对比学习（SCL）三种领域自适应方法；随后迁移评估至电影评论（SST-2）和受限金融新闻子集（Financial PhraseBank）。

Result: 观察到两种截然不同的迁移模式：（1）在SST-2上，无论模型规模如何，领域自适应几乎无增益；（2）在金融子集上，显式领域自适应可为小型通用骨干模型恢复大量性能。值得注意的是，对抗性对齐（DANN）会损害领域专用骨干（如FinBERT）的性能，暗示其侵蚀了已有的领域特定结构；而监督对比损失则倾向于保留该结构。

Conclusion: 显式领域自适应的有效性高度取决于冻结骨干是否已具备目标领域覆盖：当骨干已编码目标领域知识时，额外自适应收效甚微甚至有害；当骨干缺乏该知识时，自适应可带来实质提升。方法选择同样关键，对抗性方法可能对专用模型适得其反。

Abstract: Sentiment analysis with frozen pre-trained language model (PLM) backbones has become a common paradigm, yet the practical benefit of explicit domain adaptation remains unclear, particularly when backbones encode varying degrees of target-domain knowledge. We present a preliminary case study evaluating a controlled family of frozen embedding backbones (Qwen3-Embedding 0.6B, 4B, 8B), alongside RoBERTa-base and FinBERT. We train a lightweight MLP adapter on consumer reviews using Domain-Adversarial Neural Networks (DANN), Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), and Supervised Contrastive Learning (SCL), and evaluate transfer to movie reviews (SST-2) and a heavily restricted subset of financial news (Financial PhraseBank). Within this constrained sample, we observe two distinct transfer patterns. On SST-2, domain adaptation provides negligible gain regardless of scale. On the financial subset, explicit domain adaptation appears to recover substantial performance for small general-purpose backbones. Notably, we find that adversarial alignment (DANN) is associated with degraded performance for domain-specialized backbones like FinBERT, consistent with erosion of pre-existing domain-specific structure, whereas supervised contrastive loss appears to preserve it. These preliminary findings suggest that the efficacy of explicit domain adaptation is highly contingent on whether the frozen backbone already possesses target-domain coverage.

</details>


### [120] [Umm... With Transformers? Insights from Filled Pause Use across Four Slavic Parliaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05964)
*Ivan Porupski,Branimir Dropuljić,Nikola Ljubešić*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过对四种斯拉夫语言约4000小时议会演讲的大规模语料分析，本研究使用基于Transformer的自动检测和GEE建模，验证了年龄和语速与填充停顿（FP）频率的负相关，但发现性别效应具有语言特异性且与多数前人研究相反。新发现包括情感与FP频率正相关，以及在野党演讲者的FP频率低于执政联盟演讲者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有填充停顿（FPs）研究大多依赖小规模、单一语言的语料库，限制了研究结论的普适性。为弥补这一不足，亟需基于大规模、跨语言语料对FP的各类社会语言学及副语言影响因素进行系统验证与拓展。

Method: 使用约4000小时的四种相关斯拉夫语言（克罗地亚语、捷克语、波兰语、塞尔维亚语）议会演讲语料。采用基于Transformer的自动检测方法识别FP出现；使用带Mundlak校正的广义估计方程（GEE）对FP频率进行建模，以区分说话人内部效应与说话人间效应。预测变量包括年龄、语速、性别、情感、政治倾向和权力地位。

Result: （1）年龄与语速对FP频率的负相关得到复现；（2）性别效应呈现语言特异性，且方向与多数既有文献相反；（3）情感与FP频率之间存在一致的正相关关系；（4）政治倾向与权力地位对FP频率有调节作用：在野党演讲者的FP频率显著低于执政联盟演讲者。

Conclusion: FP的发生受到多种社会语言学与语境因素的共同影响，其中年龄和语速效应具有跨语言稳健性，而性别效应并非普适的。情感、政治倾向与权力地位等新颖因素对FP有显著且一致的预测力，表明FP不仅是认知加工的指标，也承载着社会语用功能，呼吁未来研究在更大范围的跨语言、跨语境语料中进行验证与拓展。

Abstract: Filled pauses (FPs) are a universal feature of spontaneous speech, yet most studies rely on small, single-language corpora, limiting the generalisability of their findings. We analyse ~4,000 hours of parliamentary speech across four related Slavic languages (Croatian, Czech, Polish, Serbian). FP occurrence is obtained via transformer-based automatic detection, while FP rate is modelled using Generalised Estimating Equations (GEE) with Mundlak correction to distinguish within- from between- speaker effects. We replicate a negative association of age and speech rate with FP rate, but find that gender effects are language-specific and directionally opposite to most prior literature. Novel analyses of sentiment, political orientation, and power status reveal a consistent positive association between sentiment and FP rate, alongside parliament-specific modulation by orientation and power status, with opposition speakers tending toward lower FP rates than governing coalition speakers.

</details>


### [121] [InfluMatch: Frontier-Quality KOL Search at 4B-Model Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05968)
*Krittanon Kaewtawee,Petmongkon Pornpichitsuwan,Natchaya Temyingyong,Nutnicha Laplamoon,Wachiravit Modecrua,Krittin Pachtrachai,Touchapon Kraisingkorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InfluMatch 提出了一种低成本三级级联架构（检索→重排序→推理），全部基于小型开源模型，用于将自由形式的多部分泰语营销标准与 KOL 匹配。该方案在匹配精度上与前沿大模型 Kimi-K2.6 相当，但输出 token 减少约 35 倍，单张 A100 上 50-KOL 查询仅需约 20 秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KOL 匹配方案存在两难困境：基于关键词搜索的结构化画像匹配缺乏语义理解能力，而使用前沿大模型逐候选推理虽然准确但速度慢、成本高。需要一种兼顾精度与成本、可部署且可解释的替代方案。

Method: 提出三级级联架构 InfluMatch：(1) 稠密检索召回 50 个候选；(2) 4B 参数的点式重排序器，通过单个 Yes token 的对数概率评分，筛选至 10 个；(3) 4B 参数的推理器按标准对短名单进行评分，并输出泰语推理依据。训练方面，仅采用成对 SimPO 微调重排序器有效；推理器使用未微调的基座模型直接部署。

Result: 在 11 条查询、每查询 50 候选全标注的数据集上：完整级联达到 94.1% P@5，检索基线上接近随机水平；与前沿模型 Kimi-K2.6（91.8%）精度匹配，输出 token 减少约 35 倍；重排序器最佳选择准确率达 78.0% EM。推理器按标准逐点微调虽提升离线分数，但恶化了端到端排序性能。

Conclusion: InfluMatch 以远低于前沿模型的成本实现了可部署、可解释的 KOL 搜索系统。关键发现是成对微调优于逐点微调，且推理阶段直接使用未微调基座模型反而最优，这揭示了对绝对标注任务设计的反思。

Abstract: Matching influencers (KOLs) to free-form, multi-part Thai marketing criteria is today served either by keyword search over structured profiles, which misses semantic fit, or by prompting frontier LLMs over every candidate, which is accurate but slow and expensive. We present InfluMatch, a low-cost three-stage cascade -- retrieval $\rightarrow$ rerank $\rightarrow$ reason -- built entirely from small open-weight models: dense retrieval returns 50 candidates, a 4B pointwise reranker scores each by the log-probability of a single Yes token and keeps 10, and a 4B reasoner grades the shortlist per criterion on a rubric with a Thai rationale. The cascade is designed for cost: reasoning over a filtered top-10 halves token spend versus reasoning over all 50 while scoring 14 points higher. End-to-end against human relevance labels on an 11-query set with all 50 candidates labeled, the full cascade reaches 94.1% P@5, versus a retrieval-only baseline near random; it matches the frontier model Kimi-K2.6 (91.8%) while emitting ${\sim}35\times$ fewer output tokens and serving a 50-KOL query in ${\sim}20$ s on one A100. Notably, the only fine-tuning that pays off is pairwise: a SimPO-tuned reranker matches the frontier baseline's best-pick accuracy (78.0 EM), whereas fine-tuning the reasoner on pointwise per-criterion labels improves offline scores yet degrades end-to-end ranking -- an inversion we trace to the design of the absolute labeling task -- leaving the untuned base model as the strongest deployed reasoner. The result is a deployable, explainable KOL search system at a small fraction of frontier serving cost.

</details>


### [122] [MemDefrag: Latent Memory Defragmentation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05969)
*Ruiyi Yan,Zhuoyuan Mao,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemDefrag 提出一种无需训练、模型无关的框架，利用中间层注意力密度作为追踪信号，对潜在记忆进行碎片整理和比例遗忘，显著提升 LLM 在记忆更新时的知识保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在记忆范式（如 MemoryLLM、M+）在记忆更新时性能严重退化，根本原因在于：位置编码错位导致记忆碎片无法被准确定位，且缺乏区分目标记忆与无关记忆的追踪机制。

Method: 首先通过探测层间注意力密度，发现中间若干 Transformer 层天然对目标记忆片段聚集最高注意力密度，构成固有的追踪信号。基于此提出 MemDefrag：① 利用中间层追踪信号进行记忆碎片整理（排序、重排、过滤）；② 当容量超限时，采用信息量引导的比例遗忘机制。

Result: 在 50 次记忆更新后，MemDefrag 知识保留率达 43.0%，远超 MemoryLLM（17.4%）和 M+（17.6%）；在长上下文基准测试中同样表现优异，并跨多种 LLM 和潜在记忆变体展现出良好泛化性。

Conclusion: MemDefrag 通过挖掘模型内在的中间层追踪信号，以训练无关的方式有效解决了潜在记忆更新的碎片化问题，为 LLM 长期记忆机制提供了简洁高效的优化路径。

Abstract: Latent memory, which stores past knowledge fragments as per-layer hidden states, has emerged as a promising paradigm (e.g., MemoryLLM and M+) for long-term memory in large language models (LLMs). However, the paradigm suffers from significant performance degradation during memory updates, due to positional encoding misalignment and the absence of any tracing mechanism to distinguish target memory fragments from irrelevant ones. To discover such a tracing mechanism, we probe the layer-wise attention density over stored memory fragments, and find that a small set of middle transformer layers consistently concentrates the highest density on the target fragment - exposing an inherent tracing signal. In light of this, we propose MemDefrag, a training-free and model-agnostic framework that (1) uses a middle-layer tracing signal to conduct memory defragmentation (rank, reorder, and filter memories), and (2) applies an informativeness-guided proportional forgetting mechanism once capacity is exceeded. Experiments show that MemDefrag substantially outperforms MemoryLLM and M+ on knowledge retention (e.g., 43.0% vs. 17.4%/17.6% after 50 memory updates) and long-context benchmarks, and generalizes well across various LLMs and latent-memory variants.

</details>


### [123] [PluraMath: Extending Mathematical Reasoning Evaluation Beyond High-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05992)
*Daryna Dementieva,Nikolay Babakov,Kathy Hämmerl,Ilseyar Alimova,Jindřich Libovický,Shu Okabe,Miras Baisbay,Lukas Edman,Abrorkhon Inomkhujaev,Antonia Karamolegkou,Mateusz Lango,Volkan Özer,Nikola Selic,Subhankar Swain,Tsedeniya Kinfe Temesgen,Galit Bary Weisberg,Alexander Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 PluraMath，一个面向18种低资源语言的多语言数学推理基准数据集。该数据集扩展自 PolyMath，覆盖6个语系，通过母语者人工验证构建。作者在27个推理LLM上测试，发现高资源语言与低资源语言之间存在显著的数学推理性能差距，且更强的指令遵循能力与更好的多语言推理表现相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准（如 PolyMath）严重偏向英语和中文等高资源语言，仅覆盖18种高资源语言，大量中低资源语言缺乏评估数据集，导致无法有效衡量LLM在多元语言条件下的数学推理能力。

Method: 提出 PluraMath 数据集：1）在 PolyMath 基础上扩展至18种新增的低资源语言，覆盖6个语系；2）采用人工策划流程，由母语者对预计算翻译进行逐一验证；3）在4个模型规模等级（小、中、大、闭源集成）的27个推理LLM上进行系统基准测试；4）开展细粒度分析，考察不同语言条件下的数学推理表现。

Result: 细粒度分析确认：高资源语言与低资源语言之间的数学推理性能存在持续显著差距；更强的指令遵循能力与更好的多语言推理表现密切相关。数据集、数据采集流程和评估框架已全部开源。

Conclusion: PluraMath 有效填补了低资源语言数学推理评估的空白，揭示了当前SOTA模型在多语言数学推理上的能力不均衡，开源全流程旨在降低低资源社区参与多语言基准开发的门槛。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning has become a central task for evaluating and tuning reasoning Large Language Models (LLMs), yet existing benchmarks remain heavily biased toward high-resource languages, with English and Chinese dominating both pre-training corpora and evaluation suites. The recently released PolyMath (Wang et al., 2025) dataset represents a significant step forward, yet its coverage is still limited to 18 only high-resource languages. To address this gap, we introduce PluraMath, an extension of PolyMath to 18 additional {underrepresented languages spanning 6 language families -- ranging from mid-resource to extreme low-resource settings. We constructed the dataset through a human-curated pipeline, where native speakers thoroughly validated pre-computed translations. Using PluraMath, we then benchmark 27 reasoning LLMs across four model scales -- small, mid-size, large, and closed-source ensembles -- probing the multilingual mathematical reasoning capabilities of state-of-the-art models under diverse linguistic conditions. Our fine-grained analysis confirms a persistent gap in mathematical reasoning performance between high-resource and underrepresented languages, with stronger results largely associated with better instruction-following ability. We fully open-source our dataset, data acquisition pipeline, and evaluation framework, with the goal of lowering the barrier to multilingual benchmark development for underrepresented communities.

</details>


### [124] [From Blueprint to Reality: Modeling and Applying Putnam's Social Capital Theory with LLM-based Multi-agent Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06080)
*Shiyi Ling,Zhi Zheng,Hui Zheng,Wenjun Xue,Feng Ye,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SocaSim 是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体仿真框架，将普特南社会资本理论从理论蓝图转化为可模拟的现实，通过整合社会网络演化、信任动态和规范传播三个维度，复现宏观模式并追踪微观因果路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实证方法在控制和复现方面存在实际限制，而现有的基于大语言模型的社会仿真通常以行为驱动，缺乏与理论对齐的环境来建模普特南的核心命题。两者之间存在明显空白。

Method: 提出 SocaSim 框架，构建融合社会网络演化、信任动态和规范传播的环境，让智能体参与重复的集体行动实验；在此基础上，将三个维度应用于智慧养老的适应挑战分析，并通过逐轮仿真和反事实干预追踪微观因果路径。

Result: 仿真成功复现了普特南理论的宏观模式，并在群体层面展现出较强的人-智能体对齐度；同时实现了社会网络、信任和规范三个维度的过程级可解释性。

Conclusion: SocaSim 建立了一种利用 LLM 智能体桥接社会科学与计算机科学的研究范式，为理论驱动的社会仿真提供了从微观因果到宏观模式的完整分析路径。

Abstract: Putnam's Social Capital Theory is a foundational framework for collective action and community prosperity. However, traditional empirical methods face practical limits on control and replication. Meanwhile, LLM-based social simulations are typically behavior-driven and lack theory-aligned environments for modeling Putnam's core propositions. To address these gaps, we introduce SocaSim, an LLM-based multi-agent simulation framework to study Putnam's Social Capital Theory from theoretical blueprint to simulated reality. Specifically, we build an environment integrating social network evolution, trust dynamics, and norm propagation, where agents engage in repeated collective-action experiments, and then apply the three dimensions to analyze adaptation challenges in smart elderly care. Our simulations reproduce Putnam's macro-level patterns and exhibit strong human-agent alignment at the group level. Unlike traditional methods, SocaSim traces micro-level causal pathways of social network, trust, and norms via round-by-round simulations and counterfactual interventions, enabling process-level interpretability. Taken together, these capabilities establish a research paradigm that leverages LLM agents to bridge social science and computer science.

</details>


### [125] [Measuring the practice of shared-decision making (OPTION12): An Investigation into Open-sourced Smaller LLMs (OS-sLLMs) for Better Privacy and Sustainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06127)
*Tamara Wit,Lifeng Han,Carly Heipon,David Lindevelt,Anne Stiggelbout,Suzan Verberne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次探索开源小型语言模型（OS-sLLMs）在共享决策（SDM）自动化评估中的应用，采用Observer OPTION12框架和荷兰语黑色素瘤咨询记录。研究发现通用领域模型优于医学领域模型，Gemma3:12b与人类标注一致性最高，并提出Judge-LLM共识框架。结论表明当前OS-sLLMs尚无法替代人类标注者，但为人机协同的隐私保护SDM评估奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SDM自动化评估依赖大型商业模型和简化的OPTION5工具，存在隐私泄露风险且不可本地部署。研究旨在填补开源小型语言模型在完整OPTION12框架下进行隐私保护SDM评估的空白，并聚焦非英语（荷兰语）临床场景。

Method: 使用专家标注的荷兰语黑色素瘤咨询记录，在开发阶段试点研究中评估三种通用领域和两种医学领域的开源小型语言模型。采用Pearson相关系数和Spearman秩相关系数量化模型与人类标注的一致性，并进行条目级和定性分析。此外提出Judge-LLM共识框架以支持多模型间的分歧解决。

Result: 通用领域模型整体优于医学领域模型，后者出现严重的幻觉和指令遵循失败。Gemma3:12b取得最佳性能（Pearson r=0.51, Spearman ρ=0.59）。系统性问题集中在时序话语推理、对话角色归因和证据锚定三个方面。

Conclusion: 当前开源小型语言模型尚不能完全替代人类标注者，但在隐私保护的人机协同SDM评估中展现出有前景的基础能力，为未来低资源语言和敏感医疗场景下的自动化评估提供了可行方向。

Abstract: We present LLM4SDM, the first study of open-source smaller language models (OS-sLLMs) for automated assessment of shared decision making (SDM) using the Observer OPTION12 framework. Unlike previous work that relies on large commercial models and the shorter OPTION5 instrument, our study focuses on privacy-preserving locally deployable models and Dutch melanoma consultation transcripts. Using expert-annotated clinical consultations, we evaluate three general-domain and two medical-domain OS-sLLMs during a development-phase pilot study. Results show that general-domain models outperform medical-domain models, which exhibit substantial hallucination and instruction-following failures. Gemma3:12b achieves the strongest agreement with human annotations (Pearson r=0.51, Spearman \r{ho}=0.59). Item-level and qualitative analyses reveal systematic challenges related to temporal discourse reasoning, conversational role attribution, and evidence grounding. We further introduce a Judge-LLM consensus framework designed to support disagreement resolution among multiple models. Our findings suggest that while current OS-sLLMs cannot replace human annotators, they offer a promising foundation for privacy-preserving human-in-the-loop SDM assessment.

</details>


### [126] [CurateEvo: Data-Curation Evolving for Agentic Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06140)
*Dingzirui Wang,Xuanliang Zhang,Keyan Xu,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CurateEvo提出了一种故障驱动的动态进化框架，用于LLM智能体的后训练数据管理。它将管理策略表示为可执行代码，并利用失败轨迹迭代重写该策略，从而在提升数据有效性的同时降低管理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体后训练流程将数据管理视为固定的预处理步骤，侧重数据增强而忽视过滤、精炼及对下游失败的适应性调整，导致长程决策能力提升受限。

Method: CurateEvo将数据管理策略编码为可执行代码，在每轮迭代中利用开发集上的失败轨迹诊断重复出现的失败模式，据此对固定原始语料进行增强、过滤和精炼；随后在成本感知目标下剪枝冗余或低效用训练轮次，最终生成SFT数据、RL数据及推理时记忆库。

Result: 在ACEBench-Agent、BFCL-V4和τ²-Bench等基准上，CurateEvo在有标注数据和野数据两种设置下均一致优于先前管理方法，平均分数分别提升3.2和2.7分，且显著降低管理开销。

Conclusion: CurateEvo通过失败驱动的动态进化有效解决了智能体后训练数据管理的适应性与效率问题，兼容多种后训练方案，并大幅减少管理开销。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents require post-training methods that can improve long-horizon decision making from environment feedback. However, existing agentic post-training pipelines often treat data curation as a fixed preprocessing step, focusing mainly on data augmentation while neglecting filtering, refinement, and adaptation to downstream failures. We propose CurateEvo, a failure-driven dynamic evolution framework for agentic post-training data curation. CurateEvo represents the curation strategy as executable code and iteratively rewrites it using failed trajectories from a held-out development set. At each epoch, the evolved strategy transforms a fixed raw corpus into supervised fine-tuning data, reinforcement learning data, and an inference-time memory bank. The evolution process first improves effectiveness by diagnosing recurring failure modes and augmenting, filtering, or refining data accordingly, and then improves efficiency by pruning redundant or low-utility training turns under a cost-aware objective. Experiments on ACEBench-Agent, BFCL-V4, and τ^2-Bench under both labeled and wild-data settings show that CurateEvo consistently outperforms prior curation methods, improving average scores by 3.2 and 2.7 points, respectively. Further analyses demonstrate that CurateEvo is compatible with different post-training recipes and substantially reduces curation overhead.

</details>


### [127] [Prompting Complexity: Shortest Prompts for Texts and Behaviors in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06145)
*Adrian Cosma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“提示复杂度”（prompting complexity）概念，将其定义为：对于固定的指令微调语言模型，使确定性解码输出目标文本的最短合理提示长度。它是资源受限柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度在语言模型语境下的类比——提示是程序，模型接口是解释器，省略的信息由模型权重、训练分布等补全。作者进一步扩展到软提示复杂度、提示距离和行为提示复杂度，并制定了实证研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度依赖于通用图灵机，具有不变性定理，但缺乏对特定模型能力和偏好的刻画。作者希望建立一个与具体语言模型绑定的复杂度度量，以形式化地研究“哪些文本和行为可以通过短提示从固定LM接口中提取”，为提示工程与提示优化提供理论基础。

Method: 作者首先将提示复杂度定义为使模型确定性解码出目标文本的最短“合理”提示（限制为可读自然语言而非任意token串）。在有限上下文下该度量可通过枚举计算。在此基础上，拓展出三个变体：(1) 软提示复杂度（允许近似输出，引入有损压缩视角）；(2) 提示距离（通过比较两文本各自最短生成提示来度量文本间关系）；(3) 行为提示复杂度（衡量使模型输出满足某规范的最短提示）。

Result: 该论文属于概念性与框架性工作，未报告具体实验数值。核心成果是建立了一套形式化的定义体系：提示复杂度及其有损/行为变体，并论证了其可计算性（有限上下文下可枚举）、非普适性（无模型独立的不变性定理），以及作为提示优化形式化目标的适用性。

Conclusion: 提示复杂度为理解语言模型的可提示性（promptability）提供了形式化框架。由于该度量是模型相关的且非普适的，同一文本在不同模型上的复杂度可能迥异。文章呼吁开展实证研究，系统刻画哪些文本和行为可通过短提示从固定LM接口访问，从而深化对语言模型能力边界的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we define the quantity of prompting complexity: for a fixed instruction-tuned language model, what is the shortest plausible prompt that makes deterministic decoding produce a target text? It is an LM-relative analogue of resource-bounded Kolmogorov complexity: the prompt is a program, the model interface is the interpreter, and information omitted from the prompt is supplied by the model's weights, training distribution, tokenizer, template, and decoding rule. Unlike classical Kolmogorov complexity, this measure is intentionally non-universal. In the finite-context setting it is computable by enumeration, but there is no model-independent invariance theorem; the same text may be cheap for one model and inaccessible or expensive for another. To keep the search space aligned with prompt engineering, we restrict programs to plausible human-readable texts rather than arbitrary token strings. We extend the exact definition to soft prompting complexity for approximate outputs, yielding a lossy notion of model-relative text compression and a formal target for prompt optimization. We also define prompting distance by comparing shortest generating prompts, and behavioral prompting complexity for reaching any output satisfying a specification. Based on these formulations, we define a research agenda for empirically studying which texts and behaviors are accessible from short plausible prompts under a fixed LM interface.

</details>


### [128] [LLM Agents for Deliberative Collaboration: A Study on Joint Decision Making Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06157)
*Chenxu Wang,Yongkun Yang,Boyuan Du,Shiwei Lin,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型智能体在部分可观测的联合决策任务中的协商协作能力，构建了可扩展的基准测试，并对多种代表性LLM进行系统评估。结果显示当前模型在复杂协商任务上仍面临挑战，但协商过程本身也为反思和纠错提供了机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类协作中，协商（deliberation）是达成共识的关键环节。然而，当前大语言模型在部分可观测条件下的协商协作能力尚缺乏系统的评估框架和深入理解，亟需建立基准来检验LLM智能体在信息不对称情况下通过沟通达成联合决策的能力。

Method: 作者将协商协作形式化为一个具有部分和非对称观测的合作联合决策问题，并构建了一个可扩展的基准测试，涵盖多种任务场景和领域。在此基础上，设计了协商智能体的参考框架（scaffold）和评估协议，对一系列代表性大语言模型进行系统评估与诊断分析。

Result: 结果表明：（1）复杂的协商协作任务对当前最先进的语言模型仍构成挑战；（2）即使借助外部数学工具，模型在信息对齐的协商过程或复杂推理决策过程中仍可能失败；（3）诊断分析表明，协商过程可为反思和纠错提供机会，在某些情况下性能甚至优于集中式基线。

Conclusion: 该工作为评估和改进LLM智能体的协商协作能力奠定了基础，揭示了当前基于LLM的多智能体系统的优势、局限及特性，为未来研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Deliberation plays a crucial role in collaboration; when humans work together, they naturally engage in communication to align information and reach an agreement. In this paper, we investigate deliberative large language model (LLM) agents under partially observable joint decision-making tasks. We formalize deliberative collaboration as a cooperative joint decision problem with partial and asymmetric observations, and introduce a scalable benchmark that instantiates this problem across multiple task settings and domains in which agents must exchange information through deliberation to reach a joint decision with a shared reward. We then instantiate a reference scaffold and evaluation protocol for deliberative agents and conduct a systematic evaluation of a range of representative LLMs. The results reveal that complex deliberative collaboration tasks continue to challenge state-of-the-art language models. Even with the aid of external mathematical tools, language models may fail in either the deliberation process for aligning information or the complex reasoning process for making the decision. On the other hand, diagnostic analysis reveals that the deliberation process may also provide opportunities for reflection and error correction, sometimes improving performance over centralized baselines. Altogether, our work establishes a foundation for evaluating and improving LLM agents in deliberative collaboration and provides insights into the strengths, limitations, and properties of current LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [129] [LongCrafter: Towards Diverse Long-Context Understanding via Evidence-Graph-Guided Instruction Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06160)
*Chenhao Yuan,Yinhao Xu,Shuwen Xu,Xizhi Yang,Jiaxiang Liu,Chenxi Zhou,Shaoping Huang,Haolin Ren,Pengfei Cao,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LongCrafter 是一个结构化长上下文SFT数据合成框架，通过层级任务分类体系和证据锚定流水线，生成覆盖32种细粒度任务类型的高质量指令数据，有效缓解了现有方法任务覆盖窄、指令难度不足和缺乏忠实性监督的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文SFT数据合成方法存在三个核心局限：(1) 任务类型覆盖狭窄；(2) 指令难度不足，难以训练深层理解能力；(3) 生成过程中缺乏对答案忠实性的监督，导致模型可能产生与上下文不一致的回应。因此需要一种系统化的合成框架来解决这些问题。

Method: LongCrafter 提出两大核心设计：第一，构建层级任务分类体系，将长上下文理解分为局部/浅层和全局/深层两个层级，衍生出32种细粒度任务类型作为生成先验；第二，设计证据锚定流水线——根据分类体系构建任务对齐的长上下文，将其分解为显式的证据图以建模跨段落依赖关系，并在证据片段上严格锚定生成指令-响应对，确保难度可控且推理过程忠实可追溯。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B 和 LLaMA-3.1-8B 上，LongCrafter 数据微调的模型在 LongBench、LongBench v2 和 LooGLE 三个基准上全面超越所有 SFT 基线，甚至优于官方后训练模型，在高难度任务上增益最为显著。进一步分析表明，LongCrafter 数据多样性更强、难度分布更均匀，且训练后的模型能鲁棒定位证据（不受位置影响），有效缓解了"迷失在中间"问题。

Conclusion: LongCrafter 通过结构化任务分类与证据锚定合成，显著提升了长上下文SFT数据的质量、多样性和忠实性，训练出的模型在多个基准上表现优异，验证了该框架的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Synthesizing long-context supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data is a scalable way to enhance the long-context understanding of large language models (LLMs), yet existing approaches share three limitations: narrow task coverage, insufficient instruction difficulty, and a lack of faithfulness supervision. We propose \textbf{LongCrafter}, a structured synthesis framework that couples a hierarchical task taxonomy with an evidence-grounded pipeline. The taxonomy organizes long-context understanding into local/shallow and global/deep levels and yields 32 fine-grained task types that serve as a global generative prior. Guided by this taxonomy, LongCrafter constructs task-aligned long contexts, decomposes them into explicit evidence graphs that model cross-paragraph dependencies, and generates instruction--response pairs strictly grounded in the located evidence spans, ensuring both controllable difficulty and faithful, traceable reasoning. Models fine-tuned on LongCrafter data outperform all SFT baselines and even the official post-trained models on LongBench, LongBench~v2, and LooGLE across both Qwen2.5-7B and LLaMA-3.1-8B, with the largest gains on high-difficulty tasks. Further analysis shows that LongCrafter data is more diverse and better spread across difficulty levels, and that the trained models locate evidence robustly regardless of position, effectively mitigating the ``lost in the middle'' problem.

</details>


### [130] [Improving LLM-Generated Process Model Quality Through Reinforcement Learning: The Role of Reward Function Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06175)
*Alexander Rombach,Chantale Lauer,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了基于强化学习的BPMN流程模型生成中奖励函数的设计，发现奖励加权策略与模型架构存在复杂交互，平等加权优于定向加权，且设计选择的影响与是否使用RL本身同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调(SFT)受限于训练数据模式，强化学习(RL)可通过外部质量度量突破这一上限，但多维质量指标下奖励函数应如何设计仍是一个未被探索的关键问题。

Method: 在两个LLM家族(Llama 3.1 8B, Qwen 2.5 14B)上，使用Group Sequence Policy Optimization，在48种配置下进行训练；奖励信号来自包含38个指标的自动评估框架，覆盖语法、语用和语义三个质量维度。

Result: (1) RL显著提升语用和语法质量，保持语义保真度，输出变异性降低六倍以上；(2) 平等权重始终优于定向加权，强调某一维度反致质量崩溃；(3) 无效性惩罚等设计选择与模型架构存在非平凡交互：对一模型关键，对另一模型无关；SFT初始化对一架构不可或缺，对另一架构却适得其反。

Conclusion: 奖励组合是优化结果的首要决定因素，其效应与是否使用RL本身相当。该发现可推广至任何以多维度自动化指标评估质量的结构化生成任务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate BPMN process models from natural-language descriptions, yet supervised fine-tuning (SFT) limits their output quality to the patterns present in the training data. Reinforcement learning (RL) can optimize beyond this ceiling using external quality measures, but how the reward function should be designed when quality is multi-dimensional remains unexplored. We present a systematic investigation of reward function design for RL-based process model generation, training two LLM families (Llama~3.1 8B, Qwen~2.5 14B) under 48 configurations using Group Sequence Policy Optimization with rewards derived from an automated evaluation framework comprising 38 metrics across syntactic, pragmatic, and semantic quality. Three findings emerge. First, RL significantly improves pragmatic and syntactic quality while preserving semantic fidelity, reducing output variability by more than sixfold. Second, equal reward weighting consistently outperforms targeted weighting: emphasizing a specific dimension fails to improve it and can collapse the model into a low-quality mode. Third, design choices interact with model architecture in non-trivial ways: the invalidity penalty is essential for one model but irrelevant for the other, and SFT initialization is indispensable for one architecture but counterproductive for another. These results demonstrate that reward composition is a primary determinant of optimization outcomes, with effects as large as the decision to apply RL itself. The findings generalize to any structured generation task where quality is assessed along multiple automated dimensions. We release our implementation and experimental code at https://github.com/chlauer99/RL_for_process_modeling.

</details>


### [131] [Pluralis v0.1: Towards a Multicultural, Multimodal, Multilingual Benchmark for AI Risk and Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06196)
*Alicia Parrish,Rajat Shinde,Sanket Badhe,Xinyi Bai,Sree Bhargavi Balija,Hua-Rong Chu,Emilio Ferrara,Armstrong Foundjem,Rajat Ghosh,Aakash Gupta,Xuanli He,Ong Chen Hui,Minji Jung,Madhangi Karimanal,Faiza Khan Khattak,Boryoung Kim,Eugenia Kim,Liliya Lavitas,Seok Min Lim,Victor Lu,Jim Moirangthem,Dhivya Nagasubramanian,Deepak Pandita,Sita Rajagopal,Geetha Raju,Evgeniia Razumovskaia,Aravind Reddy,Federico Ricciuti,Nobin Sarwar,Sungpil Shin,Sunayana Sitaram,Snehal Thorat,Tharindu Cyril Weerasooriya,Jasmijn Bastings,Joachim Baumann,Kongtao Chen,Murali Emani,Mariya Hendriksen,Jiho Jin,Jun Seong Kim,Younghoon Ko,Alicja Kwasniewska,Minjae Lee,Tom Wei-cyuan Lin Kashyap Ramanandula Manjusha,Junho Myung,Junyeong Park,Roma Patel,Shyam Ratan,Sudarsun Santhiappan,Priyanka Suresh,Tuesday,Ksheeraj Sai Vepuri Laura Amortegui-Ordonez,Claire Dennis,Minsuk Kahng,Chris Knotz,Alice Oh,Balaraman Ravindran,Soojung Ryu William Bartholomew,Hiwot Tesfaye,Lora Aroyo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Pluralis v0.1，一个面向亚太六国的多模态、多语言、多区域安全评估数据集，揭示了VLM在跨文化部署中的系统性失败模式，并引入Judge-Pluralis作为文化适配的评估机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全评估框架以西方为中心、文化盲区严重，掩盖了区域法律、社会语言细微差异和文化禁忌，导致VLM在全球部署中存在安全隐患。需要从文化优先的视角构建本地化的安全评估基准。

Method: 构建Pluralis v0.1数据集，覆盖孟加拉国、印度、韩国、巴基斯坦、新加坡、台湾六个亚太国家和地区，包含6,448条提示、八种语言。核心创新在于引入多模态评估范式：用户文本（如"我该送这个吗？"）与指代性图像（如时钟）各自无害，但组合后触发特定法律或文化违规。将通用安全违规与本地化文化适宜性解耦，后者被确立为独立评估轴。同时提出Judge-Pluralis，一个基于一致性门控的LLM-as-a-Judge集成方法，在经验性文化分类体系上训练。

Result: 在Pluralis子集上观测VLM行为，发现反复出现的、区域特定的失败模式：图像误识别导致的下游危害、忽略物品-上下文-区域交互、以及不充分的拒绝响应。这些失败模式在不同区域和语言间呈系统性差异，暴露了全局平均指标所掩盖的盲点。

Conclusion: Pluralis并非一个已解决的文化对齐评估框架，而是作为迈向多语言、多文化评估科学的第一步与催化剂，呼吁研究社区在此基础上推进全球AI文化对齐。

Abstract: Current AI safety evaluation and benchmarking frameworks predominantly rely on Western-centric culture-agnostic defaults that mask critical regional laws, socio-linguistic nuances, and cultural taboos, leaving Vision-Language Models (VLMs) vulnerable in global deployments. We introduce Pluralis v0.1: a novel multimodal, multi-regional, and multilingual dataset built from a culture-first perspective. Spanning 6,448 prompts across six Asia-Pacific countries (Bangladesh, India, Korea, Pakistan, Singapore, Taiwan) and eight languages, Pluralis diverges from prior work by natively sourcing localized safety hazards rather than adapting Western datasets. Crucially, it introduces a multimodal evaluation paradigm: user text (e.g., "Should I gift this?") and an image referring to "this" (e.g., a clock) - both innocuous in isolation, but synergistically triggering specific legal or cultural violations. Pluralis disentangles universal safety violations from localized cultural appropriateness, establishing the latter as a first-class evaluation axis. To operationalize this, we present Judge-Pluralis, an agreement-gated LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble trained on examples classified in an empirically derived cultural taxonomy. Observing VLM behavior on a subset of the Pluralis surfaces recurring, locale-specific failure modes such as image misidentifications with downstream harm, missed item-context-locale interactions, and inadequate refusals. These failure modes vary systematically across locales and languages, exposing blind spots that globally averaged metrics conceal. Ultimately, Pluralis is not presented as a solved evaluation framework for cultural alignment, but rather as a first step and catalyst for future innovation. We call upon the research community to utilize this foundation to advance the science of multilingual, multicultural evaluation to better support AI cultural alignment globally.

</details>


### [132] [Spider 2.0-AIFunc: Extending Real-World Text-to-SQL to AI-Native SQL Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06229)
*Tianyang Liu,Canwen Xu,Fangyu Lei,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Jixuan Chen,Tao Yu,Julian McAuley,Zhewei Yao,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了 Spider 2.0-AIFunc 基准，用于评估模型生成包含大语言模型调用（如分类、情感分析等）的 AI 原生 SQL 的能力，发现最强闭源模型仅达 67-70% 执行准确率，且传统 text-to-SQL 智能体框架在该场景下优势不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前云数据平台已将大语言模型能力集成为原生 SQL 函数，但现有 text-to-SQL 基准仅评估传统 SQL，无法反映模型生成此类 AI 原生 SQL 的能力，存在评估盲区。

Method: 基于现有企业级 text-to-SQL 基准，通过智能体流水线将源任务改写为 AI 原生形式，同步转换目标查询并精炼自然语言指令，生成 465 个实例（覆盖 125 个真实数据库、Snowflake 平台六类 AI 函数），所有实例通过多轮重复执行协议验证结果稳定性后发布。

Result: 在十款最先进语言模型上评估：最强闭源模型执行准确率达 67-70%，最佳开源模型仅 58.1%，差距主要源于谓词指定、模式锚定和 AI 函数参数化错误；传统 text-to-SQL 智能体框架（如模式检索、相关表选择）迁移效果不佳，最小化智能体设置即可达到甚至超越复杂方案。

Conclusion: AI 原生 SQL 生成对现有模型构成显著挑战，传统智能体策略在该场景下并非关键，需针对 AI 函数调用特点（谓词、模式绑定、参数化）进行专门优化。

Abstract: Major cloud data platforms now expose large language model capabilities as native SQL functions, enabling analysts to perform classification, filtering, sentiment analysis, extraction, similarity search, and aggregation within ordinary SQL queries. Yet existing text-to-SQL benchmarks evaluate only conventional SQL and provide no signal on whether models can generate such AI-native SQL. We introduce Spider 2.0-AIFunc, a benchmark of 465 verified instances across 125 real-world databases covering six types of AI functions on the Snowflake platform. Starting from an existing enterprise text-to-SQL benchmark, we construct Spider 2.0-AIFunc through an agent-based pipeline that rewrites source tasks into AI-native form, simultaneously transforming target queries and refining natural language instructions to make the intended AI-native solution explicit and reduce ambiguity. All instances pass a multi-round repeated execution protocol across temporally separated windows to confirm result stability before release. Evaluating ten state-of-the-art language models, we find that the strongest proprietary models reach 67-70% execution accuracy while the best open-source model achieves 58.1%, a gap driven primarily by errors in predicate specification, schema grounding, and AI function parameterization. Agent frameworks designed for traditional text-to-SQL challenges, such as schema retrieval and relevant table selection, do not transfer effectively to AI-native SQL: a minimal agent setup consistently matches or outperforms more elaborate alternatives, suggesting that the strategies these frameworks employ are less critical in this setting. Data are available at https://github.com/Leolty/Spider2-AIFunc .

</details>


### [133] [Early Language Learning via Spreading Activation and Category Exploration in Complex Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06258)
*Salvatore Citraro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过基于图的心理词典上的搜索过程建模儿童早期语言习得，发现扩散激活机制（spreading activation）结合词汇类别探索约束，能优于最短路径基线模拟四种语言（德、英、荷、西班牙语）的规范词习得顺序，揭示了激活动力学与词汇类别访问约束之间的非平凡交互在词汇发展中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究儿童词汇习得在语义和词汇类别上是否呈不均匀分布，以及如何理解词汇发展的内在机制。具体而言，检验基于扩散激活和图搜索的模型能否解释不同语言中词汇类别级别的习得动态。

Method: 将早期语言习得建模为基于图的"心理词典"上的搜索过程，由两个交互过程驱动：(1) 扩散激活（spreading activation）；(2) 对词汇类别进行强制探索（enforced exploration，而非exploitation）。使用Wordbank中的CDI（交际发展量表）数据作为词汇类别的真值，借助词相似性图重建资源，在德语、英语、荷兰语和里奥普拉塔西班牙语四种语言上评估模型，并与最短路径基线进行比较。通过burstiness（爆发性）和平均持续时间的分析来检验类别序列的动态特征。

Result: 扩散激活模型在模拟规范词汇习得方面优于最短路径基线；在类别层面，揭示了CDI之间的复杂转换；通过分析类别序列的爆发性（burstiness）和同一CDI内的平均持续时间（average persistence time），发现扩散激活能更好地捕捉经验观测到的探索动态。

Conclusion: 词汇发展可以通过激活动力学与调节复杂网络中词汇类别访问的某种程度约束之间的非平凡交互来理解。早期词汇习得不是均匀进行的，而是受到图结构上的扩散激活和类别探索机制共同塑造。

Abstract: Is word acquisition in children uneven with respect to semantic and lexical categories? To answer this question, we model early language learning as a search on a graph-based mental lexicon, driven by two interacting processes: spreading activation and an enforced exploration (rather than exploitation) of lexical categories. We evaluate model performance on four languages (German, English, Dutch, and Rioplatense Spanish), using CDIs as ground-truth data for lexical categories, normative ages derived from the Wordbank repository, and state-of-the-art resources for reconstructing graphs of word similarities. We find that spreading activation outperforms a shortest path baseline in simulating normative word acquisition. At the category level, we highlight complex transitions between CDIs. By studying their sequences in terms of burstiness and average persistence time within the same CDI, we find that spreading activation better captures the exploration dynamics observed empirically. Overall, our findings suggest that vocabulary development can be understood through the non-trivial interplay between activation dynamics and some degree of constraints regulating the visiting of lexical categories in complex networks.

</details>


### [134] [From Sinhala to Dhivehi: Cross-Lingual Transfer Learning for Low-Resource Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06289)
*Lukmal Ilyas,Nevidu Jayatilleke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了从僧伽罗语到迪维希语的跨语言迁移学习对迪维希语自动语音识别的提升效果，通过17项实验发现，基于僧伽罗语的持续预训练加微调策略效果最优，词错误率降至12.89%，相比仅用迪维希语的基线系统降低了13.50%的WER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迪维希语（马尔代夫国语）在自动语音识别等NLP任务上资源严重不足；而僧伽罗语同属印度-雅利安语支海岛语言，资源相对丰富，两者具有语言学上的亲缘关系。因此探索能否借助僧伽罗语通过跨语言迁移学习来弥补迪维希语的数据稀缺问题。

Method: 设计了五种迁移学习范式共17项对照实验：(1) 仅用迪维希语的基线；(2) 顺序微调；(3) 多语言微调；(4) 持续预训练；(5) 使用土耳其语作为无关语言的对照实验。最优系统采用在僧伽罗语上持续预训练、再在迪维希语上微调，并配合KenLM语言模型解码。

Result: 最佳系统（僧伽罗语持续预训练+迪维希语微调+KenLM）达到12.89% WER和2.70% CER，相比仅用迪维希语的基线系统，WER降低了13.50%，CER降低了3.02%。土耳其语对照实验证实性能提升确实源于语言亲缘关系，同时迁移策略和解码配置同样关键。

Conclusion: 跨语言迁移学习能显著改善低资源语言迪维希语的ASR性能，但成功的关键不仅在于源语言与目标语言的语言学亲缘性，还在于选择合适的迁移策略和解码配置。

Abstract: Dhivehi, the national language of the Maldives, is currently under-resourced for automatic speech recognition (ASR) and other NLP tasks. This study investigates whether cross-lingual transfer learning from Sinhala, a linguistically related, relatively well-resourced Insular Indo-Aryan language, can improve Dhivehi ASR. We conduct seventeen experiments across five transfer learning paradigms: Dhivehi-only baselines, sequential fine-tuning, multilingual fine-tuning, continual pre-training, and a control using Turkish as an unrelated language. The strongest system, continual pre-training on Sinhala followed by fine-tuning on Dhivehi with KenLM, achieves 12.89% WER and 2.70% CER, outperforming the Dhivehi-only baseline by 13.50% WER and 3.02% CER. However, the adaptation strategy and decoding configuration are equally critical for a successful transfer learning experiment. We conduct seventeen controlled experiments spanning five transfer learning paradigms: Dhivehi-only baselines, sequential fine-tuning, multilingual fine-tuning, continual pre-training, and a control experiment using Turkish as an unrelated language. The strongest system, continual pre-training on Sinhala followed by fine-tuning on Dhivehi with KenLM, achieves 12.89% WER and 2.70% CER, outperforming the Dhivehi-only baseline by 13.50% WER and 3.02% CER. The Turkish control experiment confirms that observed improvements stem from linguistic relatedness; adaptation strategy and decoding configuration are also critical.

</details>


### [135] [Estimating Uncertainty from Reasoning: A Large-Scale Study of Multi- and Crosslingual MCQA Performance in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06327)
*Andrea Alfarano,Andrea Bacciu,Saab Mansour,Amin Mantrach,Marcello Federico*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次大规模多语言（22种语言）不确定性估计评估，发现用英语推理可显著提升低资源语言的UE性能，且UE方法选择应取决于模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UE研究几乎全部聚焦于英语，缺乏对多语言场景下不确定性估计方法的系统评估，限制了LLM在多语言环境中安全部署的能力。

Method: 在两个人工策划的QA数据集上，覆盖22种语言（高/中/低资源），对比9种开放盒与封闭盒UE方法，测试不同模型规模和架构；采用长文本推理生成，避免LLM-as-a-judge和嵌入评分带来的评估噪声。

Result: 三大核心发现：(1) 低资源语言问题+英语推理可大幅提升UE性能，表明理解能力完好，瓶颈在生成端；(2) 英语推理可消除低资源与高资源语言间的UE性能差距；(3) 小模型适合概率型开放盒方法，大模型适合自言语化的封闭盒不确定性方法。

Conclusion: 多语言LLM的不确定性估计中，推理语言比问题语言更重要；UE方法的最优选择随模型规模变化，并给出了多语言场景下选择性预测的阈值校准指导。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation (UE) enables LLM-powered systems to recognize when to abstain, yet existing research has predominantly focused on English. We present the first large-scale evaluation of UE methods across 22 languages, spanning high-, mid-, and low-resource settings. Using two human-curated Q\&A datasets, we compare open and closed box UE methods (nine in total) across different model sizes and architectures while eliciting long-form reasoning, avoiding LLM-as-a-judge and embedding-based scoring, which can introduce evaluation noise. We report three main actionable findings. First, we find that prompting models to reason in English while keeping questions in low-resource languages substantially improves UE performance, suggesting that comprehension of low-resource languages is largely intact, and that the reliability bottleneck lies in generation rather than understanding. Second, prompting models to reason in English closes the UE performance gap between low and high-resource languages, demonstrating that generation language matters more than the question language. Third, the choice of UE method should depend on model scale: at smaller scales, open-box probability-based methods outperform alternatives; at larger scales, closed-box self-verbalized uncertainty becomes superior. Finally, we provide an analysis of threshold selection for selective prediction, offering guidance on calibrating abstention in multilingual settings.

</details>


### [136] [Automated Compliance Mapping in Cloud Security with Domain-Adapted Sentence Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06364)
*John Bianchi,Luca Petrillo,Fabio Martinelli,Marinella Petrocchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Sentence Transformer模型的领域自适应方法，将云安全控制到技术指标的映射过程自动化。通过构建并扩充语义对训练语料，微调多种架构，在两个任务上均显著优于零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前云安全控制到技术指标的映射完全依赖人工操作，效率低下且难以扩展。亟需一种自动化方法来实现安全控制与具体技术指标之间的语义关联。
