Method: 首先从五个欧洲安全标准与技术指标中构建3,499对语义对训练语料；再通过回译（back-translation）和基于LLM的复述（paraphrasing）将数据扩充至最多13,996条样本，形成四种场景；最后在五种Sentence Transformer架构上进行微调，并在控制-指标映射和跨标准控制关联两个独立任务上评估。

Result: 所有微调模型均优于零样本基线。在控制-指标映射任务上，最佳模型提升高达23个nDCG@10点；在跨标准控制关联任务上，回译增强下的multi-qa-mpnet-dot-v1模型达到0.870 nDCG@10。结果表明，领域内训练数据是性能提升的主要驱动力。

Conclusion: 领域自适应微调显著提升了Sentence Transformer在云安全控制语义映射任务上的表现，证明了构建高质量领域内训练数据对于自动化安全合规映射的关键作用。

Abstract: Mapping cloud security controls to technical metrics is currently a manual process. This paper proposes domain adaptation of Sentence Transformer models to automate it. We build a training corpus of 3,499 semantic pairs from five European security standards and a set of technical metrics, then expand it via back-translation and LLM-based paraphrasing to up to 13,996 samples across four scenarios. We fine-tune five architectures and evaluate their performance on two independent tasks: control-to-metric and cross-standard controls association. All fine-tuned models outperform their zero-shot baselines. On the control-to-metric task, the best model gains up to 23 nDCG@10 points, while on the cross-standard control task, \textit{multi-qa-mpnet-dot-v1} under back-translation reaches 0.870 nDCG@10. The results show that in-domain training data is a primary driver of performance for the considered case studies.

</details>


### [137] [From Voting to Agent Collaboration: Answer-Type-Aware LLM Pipelines for BioASQ 14b](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06452)
*Taeyun Roh,Eunha Lee,Wonjune Jang,Sohyun Chung,Junha Jung,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向生物医学问答的问题类型特定型大语言模型框架，针对BioASQ 14b Task B中的yes/no、factoid和list三类问题分别设计不同的推理策略，在官方评测中取得竞争性表现，并在Batch 4的factoid子任务中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学问答不仅需要从科学文献中精确抽取信息，还需可靠地整合多文档证据。传统单一提示策略难以同时满足不同类型问题（yes/no、factoid、list）在推理与评估上的差异化需求，导致答案鲁棒性和证据可靠性不足。

Method: 提出问题类型特定的LLM框架：(1) yes/no问题采用片段打乱与自反思机制，降低对证据顺序的敏感性，提升决策稳定性；(2) factoid问题采用全文段输入结合基于思维链的上下文学习，增强生物医学实体识别精度；(3) list问题采用多智能体架构，协同完成证据抽取、候选生成、答案验证与最终聚合。框架先在BioASQ 13b上进行策略筛选，再提交至BioASQ 14b Task B官方评测。

Result: 在BioASQ 14b Task B官方评测中，框架在多个批次中表现竞争性，并在Batch 4的factoid子任务中获得第一名。

Conclusion: 问题类型特定的推理策略、集成预测与智能体验证的结合，是实现可靠生物医学问答的有效路径。

Abstract: Biomedical question answering requires not only accurate extraction of information from scientific literature but also reliable integration of evidence across multiple documents. This study presents a question-type-specific large language model (LLM) framework for BioASQ 14b Task B, designed to improve answer robustness and evidence grounding in biomedical question answering. Rather than applying a single prompting strategy to all questions, the framework selects different inference procedures for yes/no, factoid, and list questions according to their distinct reasoning and evaluation requirements. For yes/no questions, snippet shuffling and self-reflection are used to reduce sensitivity to evidence ordering and improve decision stability. For factoid questions, full-snippet input is combined with chain-of-thought-based in-context learning to support accurate biomedical entity identification. For list questions, a multi-agent architecture is employed, in which evidence extraction, candidate generation, answer verification, and final aggregation are handled collaboratively. Preliminary experiments on BioASQ 13b were used to identify effective inference strategies for each question type, and the resulting framework was subsequently evaluated in the official BioASQ 14b Task B challenge. In the official evaluation, our framework showed competitive performance across multiple batches and achieved first place in the factoid subtask of Batch 4. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining question-type-specific inference, ensemble prediction, and agent-based verification for reliable biomedical question answering.

</details>


### [138] [Data Analysis in the Wild: Benchmarking Large Language Models Against Real-World Data Complexities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06482)
*So Hasegawa,Shailaja Keyur Sampat,Lei Liu,Wei-Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DataGovBench——一个基于政府开放数据的LLM评测基准，包含复杂Table QA和Table Insight两个任务，实验表明当前最先进的LLM在真实数据分析场景下表现不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM数据分析和评估基准脱离真实场景——聚焦小表格的事实检索，忽略多表格数据集、外部知识整合和探索性洞察发现等实际挑战。

Method: 提出DataGovBench基准，源自政府开放数据，包含两个任务：(1) Table QA——需解决可分解的复杂问题并生成文本答案或可视化；(2) Table Insight——评估模型通过探索性数据分析生成专家级洞察的能力。在多种SOTA LLM上开展综合实验，涵盖有无agent框架的设定。

Result: 实验揭示当前LLM在两个任务上均存在显著性能差距，无论是否使用agent框架，现有LLM系统仍远未满足真实数据分析的需求。

Conclusion: DataGovBench为推进LLM在分析性查询回答和数据洞察发现方面的研究提供了一个具有挑战性的新基准。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) in data analysis often fail to reflect real-world settings. They typically focus on fact retrieval from small tables and overlook the challenges of large multi-tabular datasets, external knowledge integration, and exploratory insight discovery. We introduce DataGovBench, a benchmark derived from governmental open data designed to evaluate LLMs in practical scenarios. The benchmark includes two tasks: Table QA that requires solving complex decomposable questions and producing textual answers or visualizations, and Table Insight that evaluates the ability of models to generate expert-level findings through exploratory data analysis. Comprehensive experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs, both with and without agentic frameworks, reveal significant performance gaps across both tasks. These results suggest that current LLM-based systems remain far from satisfying the demands of real-world data analytics. DataGovBench provides a challenging benchmark for advancing research on LLMs capable of both answering analytical queries and discovering insights from data. Code and sample data are available at https://github.com/SoHasegawa/datagovbench.

</details>


### [139] [Pitwall: Faithful Natural-Language Race-Strategy Briefings from a Calibrated Real-Time Monte Carlo Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06495)
*Juan S. Santillana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Pitwall is a production system that generates live, multilingual Formula 1 strategy briefings with architectural faithfulness guarantees — every factual claim in every sentence is verified against a calibrated Monte Carlo simulation engine before publication.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Live sports commentary presents a unique grounded generation challenge: real-time statements about named athletes with rapidly changing state, no reference text, and a hard deadline. Existing NLG systems treat faithfulness as an aspiration rather than a verifiable property; the authors aim to make it an architectural guarantee.

Method: The system has two core components: (1) A vectorized Monte Carlo race simulation engine (N=2,000 per-lap continuations) calibrated on 126 races (2018–2024), producing probabilistic race states. (2) A generation pipeline where every output sentence is decomposed into typed factual claims (positions, gaps, tyres, pace, overtakes, race control), each verified against the grounding state. Fine-tuning data is gated: only 81.9% of model-written targets where every claim is state-supported are retained; the rest fall back to a provably faithful template. A sparse-context auditing mechanism removes hallucination-prone outputs.

Result: The simulation engine achieves 90.3% winner-in-top-3 accuracy over 155 backtests and a held-out Brier score of 0.0745 on 2025–2026 seasons. In generation, fine-tuning on richer targets improves vividness but collapses into hallucination under sparse grounding — a failure replicated across four base models and attributed to instruction adherence, not scale. The system was successfully deployed end-to-end at two live 2026 Grands Prix (Austria and Britain); at Silverstone, the probability trace correctly locked onto the eventual winner ten laps before the finish.

Conclusion: A recurring architectural insight is that virtues trade off and must be gated separately: calibration-optimal ≠ decision-optimal in simulation, and generation richness must be explicitly gated against hallucination. Faithfulness can be achieved as an architectural property through claim-level verification against a probabilistic grounding substrate.

Abstract: Live sports commentary is grounded generation under a deadline: statements concern real, named athletes, the grounding state changes every few seconds, and no reference text exists at generation time. We present Pitwall, a production system that generates natural-language Formula 1 strategy briefings in English, Spanish, and Portuguese, treating faithfulness as an architectural property rather than an aspiration: every published sentence is decomposed into typed factual claims (positions, gaps, tyres, pace, overtakes, race control) and each claim is verified against the probabilistic race state that prompted it. The same verifier gates the fine-tuning data: of 3,045 model-written targets, only the 81.9% whose every claim is state-supported are retained, the rest falling back to a provably faithful template, so the generator never sees an ungrounded target. Verification is meaningful because of the grounding substrate: a vectorized Monte Carlo engine (N=2,000 per-lap race continuations) calibrated on 126 races (2018-2024) and validated on fully held-out 2025-2026 seasons (winner-in-top-3 90.3% over 155 backtests; held-out Brier 0.0745). A recurring finding spans both halves of the system: virtues trade off and must be gated separately. In simulation, calibration-optimal is not decision-optimal; in generation, fine-tuning on richer targets buys vividness that collapses into hallucination when the grounding state is sparse -- a failure a four-base replication traces to base-model instruction adherence, not scale, and that sparse-context auditing removes from the production model. End-to-end operation -- live timing to verified trilingual briefings -- was confirmed at two consecutive live Grands Prix (Austria and Britain, 2026); at Silverstone a timestamped probability trace, committed to disk before the outcome was known, locked onto the eventual winner ten laps before the flag.

</details>


### [140] [DynaKRAG: A Unified Framework for Learnable Evidence Control in Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06507)
*Yaqi Wu,Xiaolei Guo,Chenyu Zhou,Jiaqi Huang,Xianfa Zhang,Junxu Zhang,Zhuo Yu,Zhubo Shi,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DynaKRAG将多跳证据获取形式化为基于状态条件的原子操作控制，通过学习一个共享策略在每步动态选择最优操作（检索、查询改写、证据评判、充分性判断），在三个多跳QA基准上超越最强基线，并证明学习控制器和充分性反馈的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳RAG方法虽提供迭代检索、查询改写、证据评判等操作，但通常将其组织在特定于方法的流水线或预定义控制拓扑中，缺乏一个统一的、可学习的共享策略来根据当前证据状态动态选择最优操作。该工作旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出DynaKRAG框架，将多跳证据获取建模为状态条件下的原子证据操作控制。每步由有效性层（validity layer）构建当前可执行的动作集合，再由一个基于Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct学习的控制器选择下一步操作，执行后更新证据状态并可能解锁新操作。原子操作涵盖检索、查询改写、证据评判和充分性判断等。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2Wiki和MuSiQue上分别取得0.5998、0.5340和0.3061的F1分数，全面超越最强受控基线。消融实验中，将学习控制器替换为均匀有效策略导致F1下降3.96-5.78个点；移除充分性反馈对三个数据集均有损害；受控检索容量实验表明额外检索并非总是有益的。

Conclusion: 在动态演化的证据状态下，协调检索、诊断与缺口导向获取具有显著收益。学习到的状态条件策略能够有效判断何时检索、何时诊断、何时停止，而非盲目增加检索量。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) acquires evidence sequentially, with each new document potentially revealing missing facts, bridge entities, query defects, or sufficient support for answering. Existing methods provide useful operations such as iterative retrieval, query reformulation, evidence critique, and sufficiency judging, but typically organize them within method-specific pipelines or predefined control topologies. This leaves underexplored how to learn a shared state-conditioned policy that chooses among currently valid evidence operations. We introduce DynaKRAG, which formulates multi-hop evidence acquisition as state-conditioned control over atomic evidence operations. At each step, a validity layer constructs the executable action set, and a learned controller selects the next operation. The resulting transition updates the evidence state and may enable new operations at subsequent steps. With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, DynaKRAG achieves F1 scores of 0.5998 on HotpotQA, 0.5340 on 2Wiki, and 0.3061 on MuSiQue, outperforming the strongest controlled baseline on all three benchmarks. Replacing the learned controller with a uniform-valid policy reduces F1 by 3.96--5.78 points, while removing sufficiency feedback hurts all three datasets. Controlled retrieval-cap experiments further show that additional retrieval is not uniformly beneficial. Together, these results demonstrate the benefit of coordinating retrieval, diagnosis, and gap-directed acquisition under an evolving evidence state.

</details>


### [141] [RSF-GLLM: Bridging the Semantic Gap in Multi-Hop Knowledge Graph QA via Recurrent Soft-Flow and Decoupled LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06527)
*Sambaran Bandyopadhyay,Ananth Muppidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 RSF-GLLM 框架，通过可微分的循环软流模块（RSF）解耦图推理与答案生成，利用 GRU 引导的查询更新器和动态门控机制跨越语义鸿沟，并结合流稀疏正则化保证收敛到离散推理路径，最终在知识图谱多跳问答上取得竞争性能与更高推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 retrieve-then-read 流水线破坏了可微性，使得检索器无法学习跨越语义鸿沟——即中间节点与查询之间缺乏词汇重叠时难以有效检索。因此需要一种可端到端学习的图推理方法来解决该问题。

Method: 提出 RSF-GLLM 框架：（1）RSF 模块使用 GRU 引导的查询更新器传播连续相关性分数，采用动态门控机制利用结构线索穿越语义不相似的桥节点；（2）引入流稀疏正则化，从理论上保证从软概率到离散推理路径的收敛；（3）提取并文本化推理路径，用于微调大语言模型（LLM），确保生成以事实拓扑为基础。

Result: 在 WebQSP 和 CWQ 数据集上的实验表明，RSF-GLLM 取得了竞争性的性能，并且相比于基于 LLM 的高计算成本方法，具有更优的推理效率。

Conclusion: RSF-GLLM 通过解耦可微图推理与 LLM 生成，有效解决了知识图谱多跳问答中的语义鸿沟问题，在保持竞争性精度的同时大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: Multi-hop Question Answering over Knowledge Graphs faces a critical challenge: traditional retrieve-then-read pipelines break differentiability, preventing the retriever from learning to bridge the semantic gap where intermediate nodes lack lexical overlap with the query. To address this, we propose RSF-GLLM, a framework decoupling differentiable graph reasoning from answer generation. Our Recurrent Soft-Flow (RSF) module employs a GRU-guided query updater to propagate continuous relevance scores, utilizing a dynamic gating mechanism to traverse semantically dissimilar bridge nodes via structural cues. We introduce flow sparsity regularization to theoretically guarantee convergence from soft probabilities to discrete reasoning paths. These paths are extracted and textualized to fine-tune a Large Language Model (LLM), ensuring generation is grounded in factual topology. Experiments on WebQSP and CWQ demonstrate that RSF-GLLM achieves competitive performance with superior inference efficiency compared to LLM based computationally expensive approaches.

</details>


### [142] [Life Style Levels: Neighborhood Delineation using Geospatial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06529)
*Srivatsa Kulkarni,Debarag Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于开源卫星影像建筑形态的可扩展网格化城市划分框架，用于绘制印度城市内部的富裕与贫困差异，并通过街景验证、非正规住区识别及消费贷款违约分析验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度等发展中国家快速城市化地区普遍缺乏精细尺度的社会经济信息，难以刻画城市内部的贫富差异，因此需要一种低成本、可扩展的方法来填补这一数据空白。

Method: 利用开源卫星影像提取建筑形态指标，将59个印度城市划分为高分辨率空间网格；构建基于规则的可解释评分框架，对网格进行富裕度分级；采用Google街景进行实地验证；在孟买通过建筑足迹密度聚类识别密集住区并与已知非正规住区对比；最后进行消费贷款违约率的探索性分析。

Result: 分类结果与Google街景观察的生活方式富裕指标高度一致，网格类别之间呈现显著对比；孟买的密度聚类结果与已知非正规住区存在大量空间重叠；消费贷款违约率在衍生富裕等级之间呈现明显差异。

Conclusion: 完全基于公开可获取的地理空间数据，该框架为印度城市提供了一种可扩展、可解释且成本效益高的精细城市富裕度制图方法。

Abstract: Fine-scale socioeconomic information is often unavailable across rapidly ur-banizing regions of the developing world, like India, limiting the ability to delineate intra-urban variations in affluence and deprivation. This study pro-poses a scalable, grid-based urban delineation framework using building morphology derived from open-source satellite imagery. Urban areas across 59 Indian cities and towns are partitioned into high-resolution spatial grids and characterized using interpretable morphological indicators, which are combined into a transparent, rule-based scoring framework to delineate areas with contrasting levels of urban affluence. The resulting classifications are validated through ground-level Google Street View observations, revealing a sharp contrast between the grid classes which are consistent with the ex-pected effects of the lifestyle affluence indicators. We further investigate density-based clustering of building footprints in Mumbai to identify dense urban settlements, demonstrating that the resulting clusters exhibit substan-tial spatial overlap with known informal settlements across the city. Finally, we conduct an exploratory analysis mapping consumer loan delinquency across the derived affluence classes. By relying entirely on publicly available geospatial data, the proposed framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and cost-effective approach for granular urban affluence mapping across In-dian cities.

</details>


### [143] [Hierarchical Acoustic-Semantic Modeling: Modality Separation and Semantic Coherence for Full-Duplex SLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06540)
*Zhenyu Liu,Yunxin Li,Xuanyu Zhang,Qixun Teng,Shenyuan Jiang,Haolan Chen,Minjun Zhao,Fanbo Meng,Yu Xu,Yancheng He,Baotian Hu,Haizhou Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了全双工口语语言模型中模态干扰的根源——声学与语义建模在共享深层参数空间时产生的梯度冲突，并提出Lychee-FD框架，通过层级参数分离策略解耦冲突模态，同时保持跨模态一致性，在全双工基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全双工口语语言模型（SLM）面临严重的模态干扰问题，导致知识退化和语义完整性受损，使模型显得不自然、不智能。现有工作未能从根本上解决这一问题，亟需深入理解模态干扰的成因并提出有效解决方案。

Method: 作者首先通过细粒度模型优化动力学分析，揭示模态干扰源于声学与语义建模共享深层参数空间时的梯度冲突。基于此洞察，提出Lychee-FD端到端全双工框架，核心是层级参数分离策略：在深层解耦冲突模态，同时通过专用语义对齐通道保持跨模态一致性。

Result: 在多个全双工基准上，Lychee-FD显著超越现有最优水平：口语问答（Spoken QA）提升7.4%，FullDuplexBench 1.5上交互流畅度提升28.5%，且不影响推理效率。

Conclusion: 该工作首次揭示了全双工SLM中模态干扰的根本原因，并设计了优雅的层级模型及实用解决方案，为实现无缝、高性能、原生智能的全双工SLM奠定了基础。

Abstract: Developing seamless, high-performance, native intelligent full-duplex Spoken Language Models (SLMs) remains a critical challenge and long-standing goal for the speech and NLP community. Despite notable progress, recent endeavors are fundamentally constrained by severe modality interference, which causes substantial knowledge degradation and compromises semantic integrity -- ultimately making full-duplex SLMs feel unnatural and unintelligent. In this paper, through an exhaustive fine-grained analysis of model optimization dynamics, we uncover the root cause of such performance degradation, revealing that modality interference arises from inherent gradient conflicts between acoustic and semantic modeling when the two modalities are forced to share a deep parameter space. Guided by this key insight, we introduce Lychee-FD, a native end-to-end full-duplex framework designed to mitigate modality interference. Importantly, we propose a hierarchical parameter separation strategy that decouples conflicting modalities in deep layers while preserving cross-modality coherence via a dedicated semantic alignment channel. Extensive experiments on multiple full-duplex benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly advances the state of the art, yielding substantial improvements in both speech intelligence (+7.4% on Spoken QA) and full-duplex interaction fluidity (+28.5% on FullDuplexBench 1.5) without compromising inference efficiency. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to achieve two key advances: 1) uncovering and elucidating the root cause of modality interference in full-duplex SLMs, and 2) designing an elegant hierarchical model together with a practical solution for seamless, high-performance, native intelligent full-duplex SLMs.

</details>


### [144] [On the feasibility of dependency parsing of non-human sequences without a gold standard. Is evaluation possible in other species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06542)
*Ramon Ferrer-i-Cancho,Catherine Hobaiter,Thore Bergman,Morgan Gustison*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证了在非人灵长类动物的发声/手势序列中，由于序列长度分布快速衰减的网络科学特性，无监督依存句法分析的评估即使没有金标准也可行；而人类语言缺乏该特性，因此无金标准评估在人类语言中反而更困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督依存句法分析通常依赖金标准进行评估，但对于非人类物种，金标准不可知，这似乎使得跨物种的句法分析研究不可行。本文旨在挑战这一结论，探索在没有金标准的情况下评估非人类物种无监督解析器准确性的可能性。

Method: 利用网络科学的最新进展，分析序列长度分布的衰减特性。核心论证：非人灵长类动物的发声/手势序列长度分布呈现快速衰减，这一统计特性可以约束解析器正确边比例的下界，从而使无金标准评估成为可能。

Result: 对于非人灵长类动物的序列，解析器检索到的正确边比例必须很高（由序列长度分布的快速衰减所保证）；而人类语言序列缺乏这种快速衰减特性，因此无金标准评估在人类语言中仍是难题。

Conclusion: 在非人灵长类动物中，无金标准的依存句法分析评估是可行的，而在人类语言中则是一个困难问题。这一发现为跨物种的句法结构比较研究开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Dependency parsing consists of finding a tree representation for a sequence. Unsupervised dependency parsing aims to develop parsing methods without a gold standard during model training. In human languages, an unsupervised parser can be evaluated because some gold standard is usually available or can be created. For other species, a gold standard is unknown. Thus one may conclude that it is impossible to determine the accuracy of an unsupervised parser and, consequently, dependency parsing is unfeasible in other species. However, here we apply recent advances in network science to demonstrate that the proportion of correct edges retrieved by a parser must be high for the sequences of vocalizations or gestures that non-human primates produce due to the fast decay of the sequence length distribution. In contrast, human language sequences lack that property. Therefore, evaluation without a gold standard is feasible in non-human primates but a hard problem in humans.

</details>


### [145] [Diffusion Language Model Parallel Decoding via Product-of-Experts Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08048)
*Juntong Shi,Brian L. Trippe,Jure Leskovec,Stefano Ermon,Minkai Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PoE-Bridge 提出一种新型解码框架，通过在扩散语言模型（DLM）和自回归（AR）模型之间构建一个 Product-of-Experts 中间分布，结合并行草稿生成、拒绝采样和重要性采样，以 5 倍加速实现至少 95% 的 AR 模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽然可以通过并行解码实现高速生成，但由于缺乏 token 间的依赖关系，生成质量远低于自回归模型。现有方法尝试用重要性采样弥合差距（DLM 为提议分布，AR 为目标分布），但两者分布差距巨大，需要大量粒子才能有效采样，计算开销过大。

Method: 提出 PoE-Bridge 解码框架：1）构建 DLM 提议分布与 AR 目标分布的 Product-of-Experts 作为中间桥接分布；2）先用 DLM 并行草稿多条续写；3）通过拒绝采样验证草稿 token，将候选结果推向 PoE 分布；4）再用重要性采样将 PoE 对齐的候选进一步校正到 AR 目标分布。此外引入混合温度采样以增强多样性，以及弹性拒绝窗口以减少无效验证开销。

Result: PoE-Bridge 在标准 DLM 解码方法上实现 5 倍加速，同时恢复至少 95% 目标 AR 模型性能，在数学推理和编程任务上有效弥合了大部分质量差距。

Conclusion: 通过构建中间桥接分布，PoE-Bridge 成功在扩散语言模型的并行加速优势与自回归模型的生成质量之间取得高效平衡，为扩散语言模型的实用化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer substantial speed advantages through parallel decoding, but the lack of token dependencies limits generation quality compared to autoregressive (AR) models. Recent progress attempts to bridge the gap via importance sampling, with DLM being the proposal and AR being the target. However, due to the huge gap between their distributions, the sampling requires a large number of particles and is thus expensive to compute. In this paper, we introduce PoE-Bridge, a novel decoding framework that drastically improves generation speed and accuracy by introducing an intermediate distribution to bridge the gap. The distribution is constructed as a Product-of-Experts (PoE) of the DLM proposal and the AR target. With the intermediate distribution, we first use the DLM to draft multiple continuations in parallel, then apply rejection sampling to verify the drafted tokens and move the resulting candidates toward the PoE. We then use importance sampling to further correct the PoE-aligned candidates toward the AR target. We further propose several improved techniques, including mixed-temperature sampling for enhanced diversity and elastic rejection windows for reducing wasted verification. Empirically, PoE-Bridge achieves significantly improved accuracy with $5\times$ speedup over the standard DLM decoding approach, and recovers at least 95% of the target AR model's performance, efficiently advancing most of the quality gap on challenging mathematical reasoning and coding tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/juntongshi48/poe-bridge.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Statistically Meaningful Geometry and Gauge Symmetry Breaking: A Geometric Foundation for Scientific Discovery and Intelligence Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05436)
*Bing Cheng,Yi-Shuai Niu,Howell Tong,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统计有意义几何（SMG），将过度参数化学习系统建模为无限维非参数 Orlicz 纤维丛，证明持续分布外刺激下几何结构会发生焦点边界崩溃并触发规范对称性破缺（GSB），从而产生可观测的结构 G-熵的离散跃迁，以此区分真正的智能发现与统计插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模过度参数化模型（如 LLMs）面临根本性危机：无法区分模型是在做纯粹的统计模式匹配，还是真正自主发现了新的因果规律。经典欧氏统计学无法辨别连续插值与新因果律的发现，因此需要一个新的数学框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出 SMG 框架，将过度参数化学习系统建模为无限维非参数 Orlicz 纤维丛。在此框架下：(1) 证明持续 OOD 刺激下，未建模方差泄漏至不可观测的垂直纤维空间，积累“主动反事实张力”；(2) 该张力在统计流形非线性曲率驱动下到达共轭焦点边界（T_crit = π²/K_max），引发局部体积坍缩与矩阵奇异性；(3) 该几何崩溃作为非平衡触发条件，引发规范对称性破缺（GSB），系统从规范冗余中清除隐藏张力，自发结晶出新的独立水平坐标轴；(4) 通过解耦参数卡、最小能量路径准则和因果不变性过滤器，区分真正发现与有害幻觉。

Result: SMG 框架下的非参数相变表现为可观测结构 G-熵的离散 +1.0 整数阶跃。该框架提供了一种无参数、可证伪的仪表盘，用以数学化地认证真正智能，并将 AI for Science 转化为自主范式转换的引擎。

Conclusion: SMG 提供了一种从几何与拓扑角度严格区分统计插值（模式匹配）与真正因果发现（智能）的数学机制，核心在于规范对称性破缺触发的结构熵离散跃迁，为判定大模型是否具备真正智能提供了可操作的数学标准。

Abstract: The rapid scaling of over-parameterized machine learning architectures, particularly LLMs, raises a profound crisis: do these systems exhibit genuine intelligence, or are they merely sophisticated statistical pattern matchers? Classical flat Euclidean statistics cannot differentiate continuous interpolation from the autonomous discovery of novel causal laws. To resolve this, we introduce Statistically Meaningful Geometry (SMG), a framework modeling over-parameterized learning systems as infinite-dimensional non-parametric Orlicz fiber bundles. We prove that under persistent out-of-distribution (OOD) stimuli governed by unmodeled causal mechanisms, continuous optimization fails. Unmodeled variance is rejected by the visible horizontal base manifold, leaking into the unobservable vertical fiber space and generating an accumulation of Active Acausal Tension. Driven by the statistical manifold's non-linear curvature, this tension inevitably strikes a conjugate focal boundary ($T_{\text{crit}} = π^2 / K_{\text{max}}$), triggering localized volumetric collapse and a catastrophic matrix singularity ($[G_f]^{-1} \to \infty$). We demonstrate this geometric breakdown acts as the strict non-equilibrium trigger for a Gauge Symmetry Break (GSB). The system purges hidden tension from unobservable gauge redundancies, spontaneously crystallizing a new, mathematically independent horizontal coordinate axis. This non-parametric phase transition registers as a discrete $+1.0$ integer step-jump in observable Structural G-Entropy. By decoupling parameter charts and subjecting emergent axes to a Minimal Energy Path Criterion and a Causal Invariance Filter, we distinguish genuine discovery from malignant hallucinations. Ultimately, SMG provides a parameter-free, falsifiable dashboard to mathematically certify true intelligence, transforming AI for Science into an engine of autonomous paradigm shifts.

</details>


### [147] [Design-CP: Context Parallelism for Design of Protein Nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05439)
*Lorenzo Tarricone,Helen E. Eisenach,Aiko Muraishi,Charlotte M. Deane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Design-CP 提出两种上下文并行推理策略（1D 行分片和 2D 网格分片配合环注意力），将 RFdiffusion 3 的二次激活分布到多 GPU 上，实现超单 GPU 显存限制的大规模多聚体蛋白质全原子设计，并验证了其在二十面体及八面体纳米颗粒设计中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全原子生成式蛋白质模型理论上可联合建模多条链以设计大型多聚体复合物，但其 token 和原子对的二次方表示随链数和残基数增长迅速超出单 GPU 显存，严重限制了可设计的装配体规模。

Method: 以 RFdiffusion 3 为基础，引入两种上下文并行推理策略：1D 行分片（沿序列维度切分）和 2D 网格分片（结合环注意力机制，在序列和原子对两个维度上分布计算）。两种策略均保持预训练权重不变，将二次方的激活矩阵分布到多 GPU 网格上协同计算。

Result: 在采样二十面体装配体时，最大可行的非对称单元（ASU）尺寸随 GPU 数量呈预期的平方根趋势增长；2D 分片在墙钟时间扩展性上优于 1D 分片。利用强点群对称约束，该方法可开箱即用地进行端到端全原子二十面体纳米颗粒设计，获得良好的 in silico 结构与界面指标。进一步在 16GB 工作站级 GPU 小集群上成功设计八面体纳米颗粒。

Conclusion: Design-CP 通过上下文并行策略突破了单 GPU 显存瓶颈，使得大规模蛋白质装配体的全原子设计可在多 GPU 甚至消费级 GPU 集群上实现，为大型蛋白质设计的民主化提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Many all-atom generative protein models can in principle design large multimeric complexes by jointly modelling all chains, but their quadratic token- and atom-pair representations quickly exceed single-GPU memory as the number of chains and residues modelled grows. We introduce Design-CP, two context-parallel (CP) inference strategies for RFdiffusion 3 (1D row-sharding and 2D grid sharding with ring attention) that distribute the quadratic activations across a multi-GPU mesh while preserving pretrained weights. We characterise their scaling when sampling icosahedral assemblies, showing that the maximum feasible asymmetric subunit (ASU) size grows with the expected square-root trend in GPU count and that 2D sharding achieves better wall-clock scaling. Moreover, we show how strong point-group symmetry constraints make CP usable out of the box for end-to-end, all-atom design of icosahedral nanoparticles, yielding favourable in silico structural and interface metrics. Finally, we demonstrate octahedral nanoparticle design on a small cluster of workstation-grade 16GB GPUs, illustrating how Design-CP can be a practical path towards democratising large-assembly protein design.

</details>


### [148] [Geometry-Aware Infrastructure-Anchored Denoiser for UWB Sensing and Work-Zone Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05449)
*Weizhe Tang,Jiaxi Liu,Junwei you,Steven T. Parker,Pei Li,Sikai Chen,Meng Ran,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAIA是一个几何感知的、基础设施锚定的学习框架，通过对UWB测距数据进行时间序列建模、潜在锚点布局估计和确定性距离投影，实现面向边界一致性重建的距离去噪，在真实室外数据集上相比基线方法MSE降低18.4%、多边形IoU提升15.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室外UWB测距常受非视距传播、突发噪声和长尾误差影响，导致下游空间重建失真。现有方法缺乏对工作区几何结构的显式建模，亟需一种能将测距去噪与空间重建一致性联合优化的方法。

Method: GAIA框架包含三个核心组件：(1)时间序列范围建模，捕获UWB测距的时序依赖；(2)潜在锚点布局估计，从测距数据中隐式推断基础设施锚点的空间配置；(3)确定性距离投影，将去噪后的距离约束投影到满足工作区边界一致性的几何空间中，使距离去噪与多边形重建目标对齐。

Result: 在真实室外UWB数据集（同步UWB/GNSS/IMU）及基于真实数据标定的压力测试模拟器上，GAIA实现了最低的整体测距MSE和最高的多边形IoU，相比PoseMLP基线，MSE降低18.4%，多边形IoU提升15.5%。

Conclusion: 几何感知的距离去噪是通向空间一致的工作区重建的有效路径，GAIA框架验证了将测距层面的学习与几何重建目标耦合的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Accurate work-zone geometry perception is critical for intelligent transportation systems, and ultra-wideband sensing offers a low-cost approach for infrastructure-aided reconstruction. However, outdoor UWB ranging is often degraded by non-line-of-sight propagation, burst noise, and long-tail errors, which can distort downstream spatial reconstruction. We present GAIA, a geometry-aware, infrastructure-anchored learning framework that couples temporal range modeling with latent anchor-layout estimation and deterministic distance projection. GAIA preserves range denoising as the supervised task while orienting the learned distances toward boundary-consistent reconstruction. We evaluate GAIA on a real-world outdoor UWB dataset with synchronized UWB, GNSS, and IMU measurements, and further test robustness using a real-data-calibrated stress-test simulator. GAIA achieves the lowest overall range MSE and highest polygon IoU among evaluated filtering-based and learning-based baselines, reducing MSE by 18.4% and improving polygon IoU by 15.5% over PoseMLP. These results show that geometry-aware range denoising provides an effective path toward spatially coherent work-zone reconstruction.

</details>


### [149] [The Granularity Paradox: How Temporal Disaggregation Inflates In-Sample Fit and Compounds Out-of-Sample Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05450)
*Hugo Moreira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了时间序列预测中的"粒度悖论"：更细的时间粒度虽能改善样本内诊断指标并扩大数据量，但因递归误差复合效应反而降低样本外精度。通过对13年采购数据在六种粒度上测试10种模型，发现误差呈非单调阈值结构——LSTM呈U型曲线，而线性回归保持稳定，表明悖论源于递归反馈拓扑而非模型复杂度。此外，标准逐点指标会系统性掩盖累积误差传播，需引入累积指标进行目标导向评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中，更细的时间粒度（如从月到周/日）通常会改善样本内拟合指标并增加数据量(N)，但在实际应用中却常导致样本外预测精度下降。现有评估体系依赖逐点指标（RMSE、MAE），可能系统性掩盖递归误差累积效应。作者旨在形式化这一"粒度悖论"的权衡关系，揭示其驱动机制，并提出更可靠的评估框架。

Method: 作者形式化了粒度与预测误差之间的权衡关系（细粒度增加N但放大多步递归误差，粗粒度消除误差传播但减少数据）。使用13年公共采购数据集，在年度、季度、月度、双周、周度、日度六种时间粒度上，对10种模型（涵盖naïve、统计、机器学习和深度学习架构）进行基准测试。引入共识-分歧诊断方法，对比逐点指标与累积TPFE在不同粒度上的方向性行为，并采用累积误差指标（TPFE）揭示被标准指标掩盖的误差传播模式。

Result: 实证结果揭示了非单调阈值结构：(1) 递归自回归和季节性模型在高频预测下严重退化，如Holt-Winters在日粒度上测试R²达-151，TPFE达425.85%；(2) LSTM呈U型误差曲线，从月度(19.66%)恶化至双周(35.94%)，随后在日粒度克服误差传播惩罚(TPFE 4.35%，R² 0.66)；(3) 线性回归在所有粒度上保持稳定(TPFE 16.3-17.0%)，证明悖论由递归反馈拓扑驱动而非模型复杂度；(4) 标准逐点指标系统性掩盖累积误差传播，在不使用目标依赖的累积指标时会产生误导性评估。

Conclusion: 粒度悖论是时间序列预测中的结构性问题，其根源在于递归反馈拓扑而非模型复杂度。评估预测模型时必须引入目标导向的累积误差指标（如TPFE），否则标准逐点指标将系统性产生误导。共识-分歧诊断方法可有效识别那些被标准诊断掩盖系统性误差传播的模型，为实践者选择合适的时间粒度和模型架构提供了实用框架。

Abstract: This paper explores the "Granularity Paradox" in time-series forecasting, wherein finer temporal disaggregation (e.g., Monthly to Weekly/Daily) improves in-sample diagnostics and dataset size (N), but degrades out-of-sample accuracy due to recursive error compounding over longer horizons (H). Conversely, coarse aggregation (Annual) eliminates recursive error propagation but reduces data available to estimators. We formalize this trade-off and benchmark 10 models - spanning naïve, statistical, machine learning, and deep learning architectures - across six granularities using a 13-year public procurement dataset. The empirical results reveal a non-monotonic threshold structure: recursive autoregressive and seasonal models degrade substantially under high-frequency forecasting (e.g., Holt-Winters reaches a Test R-squared of -151 and TPFE of 425.85% at the Daily grain), while the LSTM traces a U-shaped error curve, worsening from Monthly (19.66%) through Bi-Weekly (35.94%) before overcoming the error propagation penalty at Daily (TPFE of 4.35%, R-squared of 0.66). Linear Regression remains stable across all granularities (16.3-17.0% TPFE), confirming that the paradox is driven by recursive feedback topology, not model complexity. The results demonstrate that standard pointwise metrics (RMSE, MAE) systematically mask cumulative error propagation, and that evaluating forecasts without goal-dependent cumulative metrics produces misleading assessments of model adequacy. We introduce a consensus-dissensus diagnostic comparing the directional behaviour of pointwise metrics against cumulative TPFE across granularities, enabling the identification of models whose standard diagnostics mask systematic error propagation.

</details>


### [150] [Exogenous Dropout: A Simple, Strong Baseline for Corruption-Robust Time Series Forecasting with Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05452)
*Hao Hu,Xue-shan Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Exogenous Dropout——一种模型无关的训练干预方法，通过在训练时随机将整个外生协变量通道置零，显著提升时间序列预测模型对协变量噪声、时间错位和缺失的鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法在保持干净数据精度的同时，甚至优于专门设计的显式有界架构 BoundEx。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用外生协变量的时间序列预测模型在部署时极为脆弱：当协变量出现噪声、时间错位或完全缺失时，强外生融合模型的性能会急剧下降，甚至远低于纯内生基线。现有解决方案多依赖专门的模型架构设计，作者希望探究能否通过简单的训练干预来获得同等甚至更优的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 Exogenous Dropout（外生退出），一种在训练过程中随机将整个外生协变量通道（逐通道）完全置零的模型无关正则化方法。同时设计了 BoundEx 作为显式架构有界性的对照方法，其结合了可学习门控、回退到内生主干的残差连接、以及逐通道 FiLM 调制。通过跨领域实验、dropout-架构消融、门控行为诊断和表征层面边界分析进行系统对比。

Result: 在电价预测、水库水文学和气象学三个领域，Exogenous Dropout 在保持干净数据精度的同时，显著提升了高斯噪声、时间错位和通道完全缺失三种扰动下的鲁棒性。应用于双相关网络（Dual-Correlation Network）时获得了全实验中最鲁棒的模型。消融实验表明，无界的普通模型加上 Exogenous Dropout 训练后，在所有领域都比显式有界架构 BoundEx 更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对外生协变量扰动的鲁棒性不需要显式的架构有界性设计，简单的 Exogenous Dropout 训练干预即可实现更优效果。作者开源了扰动鲁棒性基准，并推荐将 Exogenous Dropout 作为未来带协变量时间序列预测研究的简单强基线。

Abstract: Time series forecasters that use exogenous covariates are fragile in deployment: when those covariates are noised, temporally misaligned, or missing, strong exogenous-fusion and exogenous-adapted models can degrade far above the endogenous-only floor. We study whether such robustness requires specialized architectures, or whether it can be obtained through a simple training intervention. We propose exogenous dropout, a model-agnostic method that randomly zeros whole exogenous channels during training. Across electricity-price forecasting, reservoir hydrology, and meteorology, exogenous dropout substantially improves robustness under Gaussian noise, temporal misalignment, and fully missing channels, while preserving clean accuracy. Applied to a dual-correlation network, it yields the most robust model in our experiments, outperforming a deliberately strong bounded architectural foil, BoundEx, which combines a learnable gate, a fallback residual to the endogenous backbone, and per-channel exogenous FiLM modulation. Architecture-by-dropout ablations, gate-behavior diagnostics, and a representation-level bound show that explicit architectural boundedness is not necessary for this robustness: an unbounded model trained with exogenous dropout is more robust than the bounded model in every domain. We release a corruption-robustness benchmark and recommend exogenous dropout as a simple, strong baseline for future work on time series forecasting with covariates.

</details>


### [151] [Empirical Minimal-Realisation Compression of Deep Neural Networks via Controllability-Observability Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05457)
*Anis Hamadouche,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可控性-可观测性框架的深度神经网络状态阶数约简方法。通过将训练后的网络视为深度索引非线性动力系统，利用数据驱动的方式构建可控性格拉姆矩阵、可观测性格拉姆矩阵和平衡格拉姆矩阵，估计逐层可达秩、可观测秩及平衡秩，并以此作为压缩后的层宽度，实现网络压缩。在MNIST和CIFAR-10上验证，分别实现约73%和约83%的参数压缩，精度损失极小，且在CIFAR-10上推理延迟降低约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络内部隐藏状态存在大量冗余，但现有压缩方法（剪枝、量化、低秩分解等）大多直接操作权重或神经元，未能从动力学角度显式刻画内部状态的功能角色。本文旨在提供一个有理论依据的"最小实现"准则，从可控性与可观测性角度诊断并消除隐藏状态冗余。

Method: 将训练好的DNN建模为深度索引的非线性动力系统，从前向传播的隐藏状态快照和输出雅可比矩阵出发，数据驱动地构造可达性格拉姆矩阵、可观测性格拉姆矩阵和平衡格拉姆矩阵。通过A/B/C测试估计逐层的可达秩、可观测秩以及联合可达-可观测秩（平衡秩），将这些秩直接用作压缩后网络的层宽度，实现约简网络的构建。

Result: 在MNIST上，四层SiLU DNN从状态阶数1024压缩至277（状态压缩72.95%，参数压缩73.48%），精度从96.60%降至95.45%；在CIFAR-10上，更大规模的SiLU DNN从状态阶数4608压缩至1339（状态压缩70.94%，参数压缩83.09%），精度从54.45%降至54.44%，几乎无损，CUDA推理延迟约降低3倍。方法优于投影降维、非结构化剪枝、结构化剪枝、低秩SVD、INT8动态量化及线性基线。

Conclusion: 平衡可达-可观测秩为设计紧凑神经网络架构提供了一个有理论依据的经验最小实现准则，能够在几乎无精度损失的前提下实现显著的参数压缩和推理加速。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often contain substantial hidden-state redundancy, but most compression methods operate directly on weights, neurons, or quantised representations without explicitly characterising the dynamical role of internal states. This paper proposes a controllability-observability framework for empirical state-order reduction of deep neural networks. By viewing a trained network as a depth-indexed nonlinear dynamical system, we construct data-driven reachability, observability, and balanced Gramians from hidden-state snapshots and output Jacobians. The resulting A/B/C tests estimate layer-wise reachable, observable, and jointly reachable--observable ranks. These ranks are then used not only as diagnostic measures of hidden-state redundancy, but also as actual compressed layer widths for realised reduced networks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 compare the proposed balanced realisation against projection-based reduction, unstructured pruning, structured pruning, low-rank SVD, dynamic INT8 quantisation, and linear baselines. On MNIST, a four-layer SiLU DNN is reduced from state order 1024 to 277, giving 72.95% state compression and 73.48% parameter compression, while maintaining 95.45% accuracy compared with 96.60% for the full model. On CIFAR-10, a larger SiLU DNN is reduced from state order 4608 to 1339, giving 70.94% state compression and 83.09% parameter compression, while preserving accuracy from 54.45% to 54.44% and reducing CUDA inference latency by approximately 3X. The results show that balanced reachable-observable ranks provide a principled empirical minimal-realisation criterion for designing compact neural architectures with little or no loss in accuracy.

</details>


### [152] [Learning to Control LLM Agent Harnesses with Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05458)
*Haiwen Yi,Xinyuan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM智能体的执行框架（harness）视为一个可学习的控制层，将其形式化为有限视界Harness MDP，通过离线优势加权回归训练轻量控制器来选择结构化执行动作。实验表明，在冻结LLM执行器的前提下，学习到的控制器能一致改善验证行为，并选择性提升最终任务质量，揭示了离线支持对过程控制转化为最终答案质量的制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体的改进通常聚焦于提示词、模型本身或手工编写的工作流，而围绕模型的执行框架（execution harness）被视为固定基础设施。作者认为这种执行框架本身应该是一个可学习的控制层，其学习潜力尚未被挖掘。核心动机在于探索：在冻结LLM执行器的条件下，能否通过训练一个轻量控制器来优化执行过程的结构化决策，从而提升智能体的验证行为和最终任务质量。

Method: 1. 将harness操作形式化为有限视界的Harness MDP，其中轻量控制器负责选择结构化执行动作（如是否执行验证、重试等），LLM执行器保持冻结。2. 使用离线rollout数据，仅以终端任务评分（task-rubric rewards）作为奖励信号，通过优势加权回归（advantage-weighted regression, AWR）训练控制器。3. 引入Harness Maturity Score（框架成熟度评分），将最终任务质量与过程执行模式的可靠性解耦评估——前者衡量答案正确性，后者衡量执行模式是否遵循可靠范式。4. 该分离提供了有限缓冲区视角：最终质量提升需要离线缓冲区中存在高回报支持，而过程行为可在与优势加权动作对齐时即发生转变。

Result: 在六个受控领域和两个公开基准适配器上，学习到的控制器一致改善了验证行为，并选择性提升了最终任务质量。最大收益出现在：适配的tau-bench零售任务、适配的AgentBench DB-Bench、以及配备校准结构化验证器的编码任务。消融实验表明，这些收益不能由行为克隆（BC）或简单添加CHECK（Forced CHECK）来解释，排除了模仿学习或机械性增加检查的替代解释。

Conclusion: 执行框架控制是冻结LLM智能体中一个可学习的层级，离线数据的回报支持范围限定了更优过程控制能否转化为更优最终答案。该工作为LLM智能体的系统化优化开辟了新的维度——不再仅优化模型或提示，而是在模型外围引入可学习的执行策略，同时警示了离线学习固有数据覆盖范围的限制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are usually improved by changing prompts, models, or hand-written workflows, while the execution harness around the model is treated as fixed infrastructure. We argue that this harness is itself a learnable control layer. We formalize harness operation as a finite-horizon Harness MDP, where a lightweight controller selects structural execution actions while the LLM executor remains frozen. The controller is trained from offline rollouts using advantage-weighted regression with only terminal task-rubric rewards. We also separate final task quality from a post-hoc Harness Maturity Score, which measures whether the harness follows reliable execution patterns rather than only whether the final answer is correct. This separation gives a finite-buffer view of harness learning: final-quality gains require high-return support in the offline buffer, while process behavior can shift whenever it aligns with advantage-weighted actions. Across six controlled domains and two public-benchmark adapters, the learned controller consistently improves verification behavior and selectively improves final task quality, with the largest gains on adapted tau-bench retail, adapted AgentBench DB-Bench, and coding with a calibrated structural verifier. Ablations against behavior cloning and Forced CHECK show that the gains are not explained by imitation or by simply adding checks. These results identify harness control as a learnable layer for frozen LLM agents, while showing that offline support limits when better process control becomes better final answers.

</details>


### [153] [AdaStop: Cost-Aware Early Stopping for DNN Test Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05461)
*Bonan Shen,Wei-Jung Huang,Xin Liu,Jiazhou Gao,Tao Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaStop框架，将DNN测试建模为成本-收益决策过程，通过估计边际故障发现率，在测试过程中动态决定何时停止标注，从而在显著降低标注预算的同时保留大部分故障发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN测试方法在固定标注预算下优先选择可能暴露故障的输入，但实践中难以确定合适的预算：预算过少会遗漏故障，过多则造成标注浪费。因此需要一个自适应停止机制来平衡测试成本与收益。

Method: 将DNN测试形式化为成本-收益决策过程，标注代价为c，发现故障收益为v。提出AdaStop框架，在测试过程中实时估计边际故障发现率，当该率低于阈值τ=c/v时停止标注。

Result: 在多个数据集、模型架构和输入选择策略上的实验表明，仅使用9%–31%的标注预算即可发现65%–84%的故障。

Conclusion: AdaStop能有效解决DNN测试中的自适应停止问题，在大幅减少标注成本的同时保持较高的故障发现率，具有显著的实用价值。

Abstract: Existing methods for testing deep neural networks (DNNs) primarily prioritize test inputs likely to reveal model faults under a fixed labeling budget. In practice, choosing that budget is difficult: too little testing misses failures, while too much incurs unnecessary labeling costs. This work studies the stopping problem in DNN testing. We formulate testing as a cost--benefit decision process in which labeling an input incurs cost $c$ and discovering a fault yields value $v$. Based on this formulation, we introduce \textit{AdaStop}, a framework that estimates the marginal fault discovery rate during testing and stops labeling when the estimated rate falls below the threshold $τ= c/v$. Experiments across multiple datasets, architectures, and selection strategies show that $65$--$84\%$ of faults can be discovered using only $9$--$31\%$ of the labeling budget.

</details>


### [154] [No Subspace to Track: Non-Identifiability and Optimizer State in Low-Rank Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05872)
*Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明GaLore等低秩优化器所依赖的"梯度可追踪的低秩子空间"假设不成立——跨minibatch估计的top-r子空间一致性极低，仅有约39/128个方向可复现。作者提出LDAdam，通过将子空间刷新视为坐标变换并正确传输Adam状态，显著改善了低秩优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaLore等内存高效优化器的核心假设是：梯度投影所在的低秩子空间是一个缓慢漂移、可追踪的对象。本文质疑这一假设，旨在检验该子空间是否真的具有跨minibatch的可复现性，以及刷新时的表观旋转究竟源于真实漂移还是估计噪声。

Method: (1) 子空间可复现性分析：在同一步内用不相交minibatch估计两个top-r子空间，计算chordal distance，与T步间隔的子空间距离对比；(2) 谱衰减分析：通过N-fold平均研究梯度谱尾部的衰减速率（N^(-1/4) vs 理论纯噪声的N^(-1/2)）；(3) 提出LDAdam：将每次子空间刷新视为Adam状态的坐标变换，一阶矩精确传输，二阶矩采用最优rotation-blind估计器；(4) 在Pythia-160M到6.9B参数规模、三个架构类别的四个模型家族上验证。

Result: 同一步不同minibatch的子空间距离(0.73)与T步间隔的距离(0.74)几乎相同（最大chordal distance为√(2r)），表明表观旋转由估计噪声主导。r=128时仅约39个方向可复现。N-fold平均下谱尾以N^(-1/4)衰减，远慢于纯噪声的N^(-1/2)。LDAdam在1B参数、40k步、beta2=0.999时达到18.7 perplexity，优于GaLore最优配置的19.3。缩短二阶矩记忆(beta2=0.99)对刷新类优化器有益，但对标准GaLore效果微小。

Conclusion: 梯度低秩子空间不具有跨minibatch的可识别性，"可复现秩k*"远小于预设r。子空间刷新应被视为坐标变换而非追踪同一对象。LDAdam通过正确处理该变换显著提升性能。这一发现解释了GaLore为何仍能工作，并提供了在使用低秩假设前需检验的准则。

Abstract: Memory-efficient optimizers such as GaLore train large language models by projecting gradients onto a rank-r subspace recomputed every T steps, assuming this subspace is a slowly drifting object that can be tracked. We show that beyond a small reproducible core, there is no such object. Two estimates of the top-r subspace computed at the same step from disjoint minibatches disagree as much as estimates computed T steps apart (0.73 vs 0.74 of the maximal chordal distance sqrt(2r), at Pythia-160M with r=128): the apparent rotation at each refresh is dominated by estimator noise. This holds across four model families in three architecture classes from 70M to 6.9B parameters, strengthening with scale, and more weakly in a vision transformer. Only ~39 of 128 directions are reproducible across minibatches, and averaging cannot recover the rest: under N-fold averaging the gradient's spectral tail shrinks as N^(-1/4) rather than the N^(-1/2) of pure noise, so no averaging budget makes the subspace well defined. What helps instead follows from treating each refresh as a change of coordinates for Adam's state. Carrying the second moment blindly is provably about (r-k*)/2 worse than the best rotation-blind estimator, while the first moment transports exactly through the rotation, the optimal linear map under isotropic gradients and the rule LDAdam uses. At 1B over 40k steps (3 seeds), full LDAdam reaches 18.7 perplexity at beta2=0.999, beating untransported GaLore after its best beta2 fix (19.3); shortening the second-moment memory to beta2=0.99 helps the refreshing optimizers, though for canonical GaLore the effect is small and a full-rank control reverses it. One measurable fact, subspace non-identifiability, clarifies why GaLore works, which patches work, and what to check before trusting a low-rank assumption: the reproducible rank k*.

</details>


### [155] [Learnable Weighting of Intra-Attribute Distances for Categorical Data Clustering with Nominal and Ordinal Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05464)
*Yiqun Zhang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图视角的统一距离度量，能够同时处理标称属性和有序属性，并保留有序值的顺序关系；进而将属性内距离权重学习与数据划分整合到统一学习框架中，避免次优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类数据聚类方法大多对标称属性和有序属性采用相同的距离计算方式，忽略了有序属性值的顺序信息；此外，标称属性与有序属性之间存在相互依赖关系，现有方法未能充分挖掘这种关系来度量不相似性。

Method: 从图的视角研究标称与有序属性值的内在差异与联系，提出统一的距离度量以计算属性内距离，并保持有序值之间的顺序关系；在此基础上，提出一种新的聚类算法，将属性内距离权重学习与数据对象划分整合到单一学习范式中。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提算法相比现有方法具有更好的聚类效果。

Conclusion: 通过统一建模标称与有序属性、保留顺序信息并进行联合学习，能够有效提升分类数据聚类的性能。

Abstract: The success of categorical data clustering generally much relies on the distance metric that measures the dissimilarity degree between two objects. However, most of the existing clustering methods treat the two categorical subtypes, i.e. nominal and ordinal attributes, in the same way when calculating the dissimilarity without considering the relative order information of the ordinal values. Moreover, there would exist interdependence among the nominal and ordinal attributes, which is worth exploring for indicating the dissimilarity. This paper will therefore study the intrinsic difference and connection of nominal and ordinal attribute values from a perspective akin to the graph. Accordingly, we propose a novel distance metric to measure the intra-attribute distances of nominal and ordinal attributes in a unified way, meanwhile preserving the order relationship among ordinal values. Subsequently, we propose a new clustering algorithm to make the learning of intra-attribute distance weights and partitions of data objects into a single learning paradigm rather than two separate steps, whereby circumventing a suboptimal solution. Experiments show the efficacy of the proposed algorithm in comparison with the existing counterparts.

</details>


### [156] [Quantitative Gaussian-Process limits of Tensor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06290)
*Andrea Agazzi,Eloy Mosig García,Dario Trevisan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过张量程序（tensor programs）视角研究随机神经网络在无限宽度下的高斯过程极限，并给出了Wasserstein距离下的定量收敛理论，提供了显式的有限宽度误差界（宽度平方根的倒数阶），该框架具有架构无关性，覆盖前馈模型及递归和Transformer类架构的权重共享方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无限宽度神经网络的高斯过程极限研究缺乏定量的、显式的有限宽度收敛速率分析，且大多局限于特定架构。本文旨在建立一个统一的、架构无关的框架，为各类神经网络架构（包括前馈、递归、Transformer等）提供精确的有限宽度误差界。

Method: 采用张量程序（tensor programs）框架，在Wasserstein距离度量下，对随机神经网络在有限宽度下的执行与其无限宽度高斯过程极限之间的差异进行定量分析，推导出宽度平方根倒数阶的显式误差界。该框架具有架构无关性，可统一处理前馈网络及带有权重共享机制的递归和Transformer类架构。

Result: 成功给出了显式的有限宽度误差界，误差界为宽度平方根的倒数阶（O(1/√n)量级），适用于有限网络执行与高斯过程极限之间的Wasserstein距离。该结果架构无关，覆盖前馈模型以及递归和Transformer类架构。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的张量程序框架，首次为随机神经网络的高斯过程极限提供了显式的、定量的Wasserstein距离收敛速率，填补了该领域的理论空白，且结果适用于多种主流架构。

Abstract: We study the infinite-width Gaussian-process limit of random neural networks
  through the lens of tensor programs, and we provide a quantitative convergence
  theory in Wasserstein distance.
  Our main result gives explicit finite-width error bounds, of order inverse square-root of the widths
  between finite-network executions and their
  Gaussian-process limits. The framework is architecture-agnostic and covers feed-forward models together
  with weight-sharing schemes relevant for recurrent and transformer-type
  architectures.

</details>


### [157] [Breaking Structural Isolation: Scalable Graph Clustering via Community-Aware Sampling and Structural Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05469)
*Jingyun Zhang,Hao Peng,Jianxin Li,Angsheng Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCISE 是一个可扩展的无监督图聚类框架，通过社区感知采样和约束结构熵来保持结构完整性，解决了现有图对比学习方法在 mini-batch 训练中的"结构隔离"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图对比学习的无监督图聚类方法在 mini-batch 训练时存在"结构隔离"问题，难以捕获反映全局拓扑分布的凝聚社区结构，导致社区碎片化和全局信息丢失。

Method: 提出 SCISE 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) SECC（结构熵社区约束算子），在约束解空间中优化结构信息以缓解社区碎片化、增强划分凝聚力；(2) CSampE（社区感知采样扩展机制），将目标节点的社区上下文融入采样批次，打破结构壁垒、保持拓扑完整性；(3) StructCL（结构对比学习模块），基于批次内结构相似性精炼边权重，引导编码器在高阶结构空间中学习表征。

Result: 在六个主流基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，SCISE 显著优于现有最优算法，消融实验和鲁棒性分析进一步验证了其在真实大规模图上的有效性和可靠性。

Conclusion: SCISE 通过社区感知采样与约束结构熵的协同机制，有效克服了 mini-batch 训练中的结构隔离问题，为大规模图的无监督聚类提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised graph clustering is a fundamental technique for uncovering underlying semantic patterns in large-scale networks. Although Graph Contrastive Learning has demonstrated promising performance, existing methods often suffer from the "structural isolation" issue during mini-batch training, making it challenging to capture cohesive community structures that characterize the global topological distribution. To address these challenges, we propose SCISE, a Scalable unsupervised graph Clustering framework that preserves structural Integrity by synergizing community-aware sampling with constrained Structural Entropy. Specifically, we first introduce the Structural Entropy Community Constraint operator (SECC), which optimizes structural information within a constrained solution space to mitigate community fragmentation and enhance partition cohesion. Second, to prevent global information loss during batch training, we design a Community-Aware Sampling Expansion (CSampE) mechanism that incorporates the community context of target nodes into sampling batches, effectively breaking structural barriers and preserving topological integrity. Finally, we devise a Structural Contrastive Learning (StructCL) module that refines edge weights based on intra-batch structural similarity, guiding the encoder to learn representations in a higher-order structural space. Extensive experiments on six mainstream benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCISE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms, with ablation studies and robustness analyses further validating its effectiveness and reliability for real-world large-scale graphs.

</details>


### [158] [EntroPath: Maximum Entropy Path Ensemble Embedding for Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06497)
*Przemysław Rola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EntroPath 是一种基于最大熵随机游走（MERW）的流形学习方法，通过聚合 k 步路径的完整系综构建相异性度量，恢复数据图的测地线几何结构。该自由能相异性在短时极限下收敛于平方测地距离，并在非均匀采样和分支轨迹场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的嵌入方法存在两类缺陷：局部归一化随机游走容易在高密度区域集中扩散，而最短路径距离对图中的虚假捷径边高度敏感。EntroPath 旨在克服这两类问题，通过路径系综聚合而非依赖单条轨迹来更忠实地恢复底层测地线几何。

Method: 采用最大熵随机游走（MERW）构建相异性度量，该度量聚合节点间所有 k 步路径的完整系综，形成自由能相异性。理论上通过 Varadhan 热核公式证明其在短时极限下收敛于平方测地距离。扩散深度 k 可在局部邻域结构与全局流形几何之间平滑插值，对称化核具有精确的 Gram 分解，可与核方法衔接。进一步提出地标投影和扩散势伪时间作为可扩展扩展。

Result: 在合成流形和单细胞基准数据集上，EntroPath 一致地匹配或优于基于扩散和最短路径的方法，同时在与局部结构保持嵌入方法（UMAP、t-SNE）对比时保持竞争力。在非均匀采样密度和分离良好的分支轨迹流形上，优势最为显著。

Conclusion: EntroPath 通过路径系综扩散机制，在保留底层测地线几何方面比现有的局部归一化随机游走和最短路径方法更为忠实，尤其适用于非均匀密度和分支结构数据，为基于图的流形学习提供了一种更稳健的范式。

Abstract: We introduce EntroPath, a manifold learning method that recovers geodesic geometry from data graphs through ensembles of diffusion paths. Many existing graph-based embeddings rely either on locally normalised random walks or on shortest-path distances. The former can concentrate diffusion in densely sampled regions, while the latter are sensitive to spurious shortcut edges in the graph. EntroPath instead builds its dissimilarities from the maximum entropy random walk (MERW), which aggregates the full ensemble of k-step paths between points rather than relying on any single trajectory. We show that the resulting free-energy dissimilarity converges to squared geodesic distance in the short-time limit, via Varadhan's heat-kernel formula. The diffusion depth k interpolates smoothly between local neighbourhood structure and global manifold geometry, and the symmetrised kernel admits an exact Gram factorisation connecting EntroPath to kernel methods. We further provide scalable extensions via landmark projection and diffusion-potential pseudotime. Across synthetic manifolds and single-cell benchmarks, EntroPath consistently matches or outperforms diffusion- and shortest-path-based methods, while remaining competitive with neighbourhood-preserving embeddings (UMAP, t-SNE) on local-structure metrics. Its gains are most pronounced on manifolds with non-uniform sampling density and well-separated branching trajectories, where path-ensemble diffusion more faithfully preserves the underlying geodesic geometry.

</details>


### [159] [Parameter-Free Encoders Remain Viable for RDB Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05476)
*Linjie Xu,David Wipf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了关系数据库预测任务中编码器设计的争议：参数化预训练编码器 vs 无参数子图编码器。作者从理论上证明了当标签作为输入时，可训练编码器参数的潜在效用存在局限性，并通过实验验证了更简单的无参数编码器在多个基准任务上仍能取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关系数据库的缺失值预测任务中，每当出现新的预测目标时重新训练模型成本高昂。基于RDB特定编码器的冻结基础模型是可行的解决方案，但理想设计存在争议：一方认为无参数子图编码器配合单表基础模型即可接近SOTA；另一方主张使用参数化编码器并通过预训练利用可观测标签学习任务特定表示。作者旨在澄清这一模糊性。

Method: 作者对RDB编码器在标签作为输入时的性质进行了理论分析，证明了可训练编码器参数在潜在效能上的局限性。随后通过实证验证，在多个相关基准任务上评估了更简单的无参数编码器的性能表现。

Result: 理论分析表明，当标签作为输入时，可训练编码器参数的作用受限于特定条件。实验结果表明，更简单的无参数编码器在多个基准任务上仍能取得有竞争力的强性能，验证了理论分析的结论。

Conclusion: 在关系数据库预测任务中，复杂的参数化预训练编码器并非总是必要；当标签信息可作为输入时，简单的无参数编码器足以实现强性能。这为RDB基础模型的编码器设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Given a relational database (RDB) storing heterogeneous tabular information, how can we predict missing (or future) values in some target column of interest? As the space of potential targets is vast across enterprise settings, it is preferable to avoid learning a new model from scratch each time there is a new prediction task. Frozen foundation models based on RDB-specific encoders provide a viable solution, but ideal design remains an open question. On the one hand, it has recently been argued that certain parameter-free subgraph encoders combined with single-table foundation models can achieve near SOTA performance, with no RDB-specific pre-training required. Meanwhile, other contemporary studies advocate for parameterized encoders pre-trained to exploit observable labels for learning task-specific representations. To address this ambiguity, we analyze RDB encoder properties specifically when labels are present as inputs, proving limitations on the potential efficacy of trainable encoder parameters. As empirical validation, we demonstrate that considerably simpler parameter-free encoders are still capable of strong performance across many relevant benchmarking tasks.

</details>


### [160] [InvWeaver: Deductive Feedback for Invariant Synthesis in Interacting-Loop Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05478)
*Guangyuan Wu,Weining Cao,Zehui Tan,Yuan Yao,Hengfeng Wei,Taolue Chen,Xiaoxing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InvWeaver 是一个神经符号框架，通过捕捉循环间依赖关系、结合循环级抽象、义务引导推理和最弱前置条件精化，有效解决了多循环程序的循环不变式综合问题，在82个多循环基准中解决了72个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的猜测-验证方法在单循环程序上表现良好，但难以处理包含多个交互循环的程序，核心挑战在于未能有效捕捉和利用循环间的依赖关系。

Method: 提出InvWeaver神经符号框架，核心思路是通过三个关键机制暴露循环间依赖并传播证明义务：(1) 循环级抽象（loop-level abstraction），(2) 义务引导推理（obligation-guided inference），(3) 基于最弱前置条件的精化（weakest-precondition-based refinement）。

Result: 在包含经典算法的新构建数据集在内的综合基准上评估，InvWeaver在82个多循环基准问题中解决了72个，显著优于现有方法，同时在单循环任务上也保持较强性能。

Conclusion: InvWeaver通过显式建模循环间依赖关系，有效突破了现有多循环不变式推理的瓶颈，证明了神经符号方法在复杂程序验证任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Loop invariant inference is a fundamental yet challenging problem in program verification. Recent LLM-aided guess-and-check techniques have shown strong performance on single-loop programs, but they often struggle with programs containing multiple interacting loops. This paper presents InvWeaver, a neuro-symbolic framework for synthesizing invariants for such programs. The key idea is to expose inter-loop dependencies and propagate proof obligations through a combination of loop-level abstraction, obligation-guided inference, and weakest-precondition-based refinement. We evaluate InvWeaver on a comprehensive benchmark suite, including a newly curated dataset derived from classic algorithms. Experimental results show that InvWeaver substantially outperforms existing invariant inference methods, solving 72 out of 82 multi-loop benchmark problems and maintaining strong performance on single-loop tasks.

</details>


### [161] [PatchOptic for Shared-State LLM Workflows with Projected Views and Verified Structured Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05483)
*Zhaoyu Bai,Jiaqi Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PatchOptic 提出了一种受光学（Optics）启发的接口，用于共享状态的 LLM 工作流。它通过投影读取和验证结构化补丁，在本地更新与全局有效性之间建立合约，解决渐进式披露中写操作缺乏全局约束的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 工作流通过渐进式披露管理共享状态，读取端已有成熟方案（grep、RAG、AST 查询等），但缺少本地更新写入全局状态时的有效性合约——即无法保证局部修改在全局层面是合法、一致的。

Method: 作者借鉴函数式编程中光学（Optics）的双向访问器概念，设计 PatchOptic 接口。每个工作流步骤声明三要素：投影读取视图（projected read view）、授权写入区域（authorized write region）和补丁来源区域（patch-source region）。运行时强制执行这些声明，同时生成路径级足迹，支持委托、子工作流组合，以及同阶段内独立步骤重排序的静态证书。

Result: 在 PatchBench（46 个跨领域用例）上评估表明：投影读取减少了信息泄露和 token 成本，同时保持了强 actor 下的输出质量；运行时验证在提交前阻止了工作流合约违规；补丁读取强制执行可拒绝使用了隐藏来源的受损补丁。

Conclusion: PatchOptic 为共享状态 LLM 工作流提供了缺失的读写合约层，将渐进式披露从仅管理读取扩展到可验证的写入，并通过路径级足迹实现可组合性和静态分析。

Abstract: Agentic workflows often operate over shared, structured state. Because LLM context windows are limited, each model invocation is typically shown only the state fragment needed for the current workflow step, a pattern commonly known as progressive disclosure. Modern systems construct such model-facing views using grep-like keyword search, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), abstract-syntax-tree (AST) queries, and task-specific agent skills. These methods make the read side manageable, but they do not define when a locally proposed rewrite is valid after it is applied back to the full state. The missing piece is a contract between local updates and global validity. We introduce PatchOptic, an optic-inspired interface for shared-state LLM workflows. Optics are compositional bidirectional accessors that describe how views of structured data are read and updated. PatchOptic borrows this view/update intuition and realizes it through projected reads and verified structured patches. Each workflow step declares a projected read view, an authorized write region, and a patch-source region. Beyond runtime enforcement, the same declaration yields a path-level footprint that supports delegation, sub-workflow composition, and static certificates for reordering independent steps within the same phase. We evaluate this design with PatchBench, a benchmark with 46 cases across domains. The results show that projected reads reduce reported leakage and token cost while preserving accepted-output quality under the strong actor. Runtime verification blocks declared workflow-contract violations before commit, and patch-read enforcement rejects compromised patch artifacts that use hidden sources.

</details>


### [162] [$\mathbfλ$-VAE: Variance Equalization for Posterior Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05531)
*Girum Demisse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 后验坍塌源于梯度不平衡和信息缺口两个独立但耦合的原因；本文提出λ-VAE，通过对重参数化步骤中采样噪声进行逐维指数缩放，同时KL惩罚保持原始方差，实现方差均衡，有效解决坍塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE普遍存在后验坍塌问题（近似后验退化为先验，隐编码失去信息性），但学界对其发生原因缺乏统一解释。本文旨在识别坍塌的根本原因，并给出统一的病理分析与解决方案。

Method: 1. 识别并形式化两个独立但耦合的坍塌原因：梯度不平衡（解码器重建信号衰减速度快于KL正则化压力）和信息缺口（随机采样丢弃编码器大部分表示，削弱解码器敏感性）。2. 证明信息缺口在代数上等价于聚合后验与先验的失配，统一两种病理。3. 提出λ-VAE：修改重参数化步骤，对采样噪声按逐维指数缩放，而KL惩罚保留原始后验方差，形成不对称性，使训练稳定吸引子远离坍塌态。4. 基于净信息增益目标推导各维度的闭式最优指数，仅用单一超参数控制重建-生成权衡。

Result: 在Binary MNIST、Binary Omniglot、CIFAR-10、CelebA-64标准基准上验证：坍塌维度持续减少，信息容量增益最高达2.8倍nats，重建质量提升最高达+0.33 BPD。

Conclusion: 后验坍塌由梯度不平衡和信息缺口共同导致，两者共享同一坍塌轨迹；λ-VAE通过方差均衡机制将训练推向非退化的稳定均衡，统一解决了这两种病理，在多个基准上取得了显著的信息容量和重建质量提升。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) frequently suffer from posterior collapse, a failure mode in which the approximate posterior converges to the prior, rendering the latent code uninformative. Despite extensive research, a unified account of why collapse occurs has remained an open question. We identify and formalize two logically independent but coupled causes. \emph{Gradient imbalance} occurs when the decoder's reconstruction signal vanishes faster than the $\mathbb{KL}$ regularization pressure as the posterior widens. \emph{Information gap} occurs when the stochastic sampling step discards a substantial fraction of the encoder's computed representation, attenuating decoder sensitivity and making collapse inexpensive. Both causes share the same collapse trajectory, and we show that the information gap is algebraically equivalent to mismatch between the aggregate posterior and the prior, unifying two pathologies. Subsequently, we introduce $λ$-VAE, which resolves both causes through a single modification to the reparameterization step: the sampling noise is scaled by per-dimension exponent, while the $\mathbb{KL}$ penalty retains the original posterior variance. This asymmetry shifts the stable training attractor away from the degenerate collapsed state, driving all latent dimensions toward the same equilibrium -- a mechanism we term \emph{variance equalization}. A closed-form optimal exponent per dimension follows from a net information gain objective, with a single hyperparameter controlling the reconstruction-generation tradeoff. We validate on standard benchmarks (Binary MNIST, Binary Omniglot, CIFAR-10, CelebA-64), showing consistent reductions in collapsed dimensions, information capacity gains of up to $2.8\times$ nats, and reconstruction quality improvements of up to $+0.33$ BPD.

</details>


### [163] [Self-Review Reinforcement Learning (SRRL) with Cross-Episode Memory and Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05541)
*Muhammad Zain Amin,Kibele Sebnem Yildirim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为自审查强化学习（SRRL）的训练框架，通过在强化学习每个episode中嵌入显式的自我审查步骤，使模型在首次响应失败时能够分析错误原因并生成改进后的二次尝试。该框架利用策略梯度优化自我审查，并通过选择性蒸馏将改进内化到基础策略中，配合跨episode记忆机制复用成功的自我审查。在GSM8K基准上，使用GRPO优化器对Qwen 3-4B和OLMo-3-7B两个模型进行评估，SRRL在最终奖励性能和学习效率上均持续优于标准RLVR基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，环境通常提供稀疏或延迟的反馈信号，导致大语言模型难以精确定位推理过程中哪些具体行为导致了成功或失败，从而难以从这些信号中有效学习。现有的推理时反思方法（如Reflexion）未能将反思能力内化到策略中。因此，需要一种能够在训练过程中有效利用稀疏反馈、并将改进持久化到模型行为中的方法。

Method: 提出SRRL框架，核心机制包括：(1) 在每个RL episode中，当模型首次响应失败时，生成一个显式的自我审查（self-review）来识别错误原因；(2) 基于该审查结果条件生成改进的第二次尝试；(3) 使用策略梯度（GRPO优化器）直接优化自我审查过程；(4) 通过选择性蒸馏（selective distillation）将成功的改进内化到基础策略中；(5) 引入跨episode记忆机制，存储成功的自我审查，供训练中遇到相似任务时复用。

Result: 在GSM8K数学推理基准上，使用GRPO优化器对Qwen 3-4B和OLMo-3-7B两个语言模型进行实验。SRRL在最终奖励性能上持续优于标准RLVR基线，并且通过成功地将反馈转化为行为改进，实现了更高的学习效率。

Conclusion: SRRL框架通过将自我审查机制显式嵌入强化学习训练循环，并结合策略梯度优化和选择性蒸馏，有效解决了稀疏反馈下LLM难以定位和纠正推理错误的问题。其跨episode记忆机制进一步提升了学习效率，证明了将反思能力内化到模型策略中是提升RL训练效果的有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning is commonly used to train large language models using environmental feedback. In applied settings, the environment usually provides sparse or delayed feedback. This makes it difficult for the model to pinpoint which actions in its reasoning led to success or failure. So, learning effectively from these signals is hard because the model must determine how each failure should inform meaningful behavioral corrections in subsequent iterations. We introduce a training framework, Self-Review Reinforcement Learning, that embeds an explicit self-review step into each RL episode. When a first-pass response fails, the model generates a self-review to identify what went wrong, which conditions an improved second attempt. Unlike inference-time reflection approaches, such as Reflexion, the framework optimizes self-review with policy gradients and internalizes improvements into the base policy via selective distillation, ensuring they persist across future episodes. A cross-episode memory keeps successful self-reviews for reuse when encountering similar tasks in future episodes during training. We evaluate SRRL against a standard RLVR baseline using the GRPO optimizer across two language models, Qwen 3-4B and OLMo-3- 7B, on GSM8K benchmark. SRRL consistently outperforms the RLVR in final reward performance and achieves greater learning efficiency by successfully transforming feedback into behavioral improvement.

</details>


### [164] [Federated Physics-Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Distributed Stability Control in Smart Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05553)
*Omar Al-Refai,Ibrahim Shahbaz,Adam Ali Husseinat,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedPPO-PG框架，将暂态稳定控制建模为合作式多智能体强化学习问题，各发电机基于物理耦合邻居的频率偏差进行本地决策，在IEEE 39节点系统上实现100%稳定率、72.4%的稳定时间缩减及7-14倍控制功率降低，且推理延迟满足实时标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电网暂态稳定控制需要在故障后快速抑制发电机频率和转子角偏差以防止连锁故障，传统集中式方法存在单点故障风险、通信负担重、扩展性差等问题，亟需一种可分布式部署且满足实时性要求的控制方案。

Method: 提出FedPPO-PG框架：(1) 将暂态稳定控制重构为合作式多智能体强化学习问题，直接针对闭环稳定目标优化；(2) 每台发电机托管独立的本地actor，其输入增补来自Kron化简电纳矩阵识别的两个最強耦合电气邻居的频率偏差；(3) 采用引导式策略初始化，从经典分散控制器热启动所有actor；(4) 采用CTDE范式，集中式critic在训练期辅助优势估计，部署时各actor独立执行，无需中央协调器。

Result: 在IEEE 39节点基准系统的5种训练故障和3种未见故障场景下，FedPPO-PG在全部24次试验中实现100%稳定；平均稳定时间降低72.4%；控制功率相比集中式基线降低7-14倍；单actor推理延迟满足IEEE/IEC 60255-118-1-2018实时报告要求。

Conclusion: FedPPO-PG通过联邦多智能体PPO与物理引导的邻域结构，实现了高效、鲁棒且满足实时性约束的分布式暂态稳定控制，在稳定率、控制效率和时间性能上均显著优于集中式基线。

Abstract: Transient stability control in smart grids requires rapid post-fault damping of generator frequency and rotor angle deviations to prevent cascading failures. This paper proposes FedPPO-PG, a Federated Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization framework with Physics-Grounded neighborhoods, which reformulates transient stability control as a cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning problem optimized directly against closed-loop stability objectives. Each generator hosts an independent local actor augmented with the frequency deviations of its two most strongly coupled electrical neighbors, identified from the post-fault Kron-reduced susceptance matrix. A guided policy initialization phase warm-starts all actors from the classical decentralized controller, while a centralized critic guides advantage estimation under the centralized training--decentralized execution (CTDE) paradigm. Evaluated on a simulation of the IEEE 39-bus benchmark system across five training and three unseen fault contingencies, FedPPO-PG achieves 100% stabilization in all 24 trials, reduces mean stability time by 72.4%, and cuts the control power by 7-14 times compared to the centralized baseline. Each actor executes independently with no central coordinator at deployment, and the per-actor inference latency satisfies the IEEE/IEC 60255-118-1-2018 real-time reporting requirements.

</details>


### [165] [EquiFiLM: Charge-Conditioned Equivariant Force Fields via Feature-wise Linear Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05559)
*Samuel Sahel-Schackis,Ken-ichi Nomura,Aiichiro Nakano,Matthias F. Kling,Thomas Linker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EquiFiLM 提出一种轻量级扩展方法，通过逐层特征线性调制（FiLM），为任意等变基础机器学习力场添加连续外部条件（如电荷、电场、电子激发），以少量训练数据学习外部驱动下势能面的变化，同时严格保持E(3)等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础MLFF（如MACE-MP-0、UMA）假设基态物理，无法处理充电、外加电场或电子激发等外部驱动的电子态变化，限制其在光激发、电荷注入等非平衡过程中的应用。从头训练电荷感知基础模型需约10^8量级结构，成本过高。

Method: 提出EquiFiLM，在等变MLFF的每层标量通道中插入FiLM模块。FiLM通过学习外部条件的缩放和平移参数来调制标量特征，仅作用于标量通道，从而严格保持E(3)等变性。以MACE-MatPES为基础模型，在带电液态水上验证，得到E-MACE模型，仅需数千帧DFT标注数据即可训练。

Result: 在四个训练电荷上，E-MACE相比无EquiFiLM的基线：力RMSE降低3.1倍（21.3→6.96 meV/Å），单原子能量RMSE降低61倍（6.1→0.1 meV/atom），推理成本几乎无增加。七个留出的插值/外推电荷上，力RMSE保持在18-61 meV/Å，能量RMSE在0.7-5.4 meV/atom。模型在全部测试范围运行稳定分子动力学，并能预测电荷依赖的约化对分布函数的首壳层响应。

Conclusion: EquiFiLM提供了一种通用、轻量的方案，可将外部条件轴添加到任意等变基础MLFF中，以极小数据成本（数千帧 vs. ~10^8帧）实现对外部驱动过程的精确建模，且架构无关、条件无关，具有良好的可迁移性。

Abstract: Foundation machine learning force fields (MLFFs) such as MACE-MP-0 and UMA cover broad chemical space at near density functional theory (DFT) accuracy. However, they assume equilibrium ground-state physics and do not natively handle externally induced changes to the electronic state, such as charging, applied fields, or electronic excitation, which limits their use for driven processes such as photoexcitation and charge injection. We propose EquiFiLM, a lightweight extension that adds continuous external conditioning to any equivariant foundation MLFF via a per-layer Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) block, learning externally driven changes to the potential energy surface from minimal training data. The block modulates only scalar channels and preserves E(3)-equivariance exactly. We demonstrate the recipe on charged liquid water with the foundation model MACE-MatPES as the backbone, yielding E-MACE. On the four training charges, E-MACE delivers a $3.1\times$ reduction in force RMSE ($21.3$ to $6.96$ meV/$\mathring{A}$) and a $61\times$ reduction in per-atom energy RMSE ($6.1$ to $0.1$ meV/atom) over a baseline without EquiFiLM trained on the same data, at indistinguishable inference cost. Across seven held-out interpolation and extrapolation charges, force RMSE stays within $18-61$ meV/$\mathring{A}$ and energy RMSE within $0.7-5.4$ meV/atom. The model runs stable molecular dynamics across the full range tested and predicts the charge-dependent first-shell response of the reduced pair distribution function probed by ultrafast electron diffraction. Adding this conditioning axis to the foundation requires only a few thousand DFT-labeled frames, against the $\approx 10^8$ structures of a charge-aware foundation trained from scratch. The recipe is backbone- and conditioning-agnostic: it applies without architectural change to any equivariant MLFF with scalar interaction-layer channels.

</details>


### [166] [SafeImpute: Reliable Clinical Data Imputation via Conformal Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05613)
*Xinrui He,Mengting Ai,Junting Wang,Curtiss B. Cook,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeImpute 是一个面向稀疏、不规则临床纵向数据的可靠插补框架。它通过事件图结合双关系 GNN 与自适应融合进行插补，并利用共形推断和 Benjamini-Hochberg 程序控制不可接受错误的 FDR，在 Mayo Clinic、MIMIC-III 和 MIMIC-IV 数据集上同时实现了高精度插补和可靠的错误控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中实验室指标检测稀疏且不规律，导致大量缺失值。现有插补方法虽提升了平均精度，但缺乏对"哪些插补值足够可靠可用于高风险下游任务"的判断能力——即无法在统计意义上控制临床上不可接受的错误，这限制了插补结果在关键临床决策中的可信应用。

Method: SafeImpute 包含两大核心组件：(1) 插补模块：构建事件图（event graph），同时建模患者内部的时序轨迹和患者间的临床相似性，使用双关系图神经网络（two-relation GNN）与自适应融合机制学习插补，并以辅助的掩码重建目标进行正则化；(2) 可靠性保障模块：将代理风险分数转化为共形 p 值（conformal p-values），应用 Benjamini-Hochberg 程序，在用户指定的容忍度下控制已释放插补结果中不可接受错误的错误发现率（FDR）。

Result: 在 Mayo Clinic 内部数据及公开数据集 MIMIC-III 和 MIMIC-IV 上的实验表明，SafeImpute 在标准插补精度指标和 FDR 控制的选择性释放评估中均优于多种基线方法，在保证强大插补精度的同时实现了可靠的错误控制。

Conclusion: SafeImpute 成功将高精度插补与统计可靠性保障相结合，为临床缺失数据的可信使用提供了实用框架，使插补结果能在 FDR 控制下选择性释放，更适合高风险的临床下游应用。

Abstract: Clinical care often relies on key laboratory indicators, yet real-world patient visits are sparse and tests are ordered irregularly, leading to pervasive missingness. While many imputation methods improve average accuracy, they provide limited guidance on which imputed values are reliable enough for high-stakes downstream use. In this work, we study reliable clinical imputation, aiming to produce accurate imputations while selectively releasing the reliable results, with statistical control over clinically unacceptable errors. To achieve this goal, we propose SafeImpute, a reliable imputation framework for irregular and sparse clinical longitudinal records. SafeImpute constructs an event graph that captures both intra-patient temporal trajectories and inter-patient clinical similarity, and learns imputations with a two-relation GNN and adaptive fusion, regularized by an auxiliary masked reconstruction objective. For reliability guarantees, SafeImpute converts a proxy risk score into conformal p-values and applies the Benjamini--Hochberg procedure to control the false discovery rate (FDR) of unacceptable errors among released imputations at a user-specified tolerance. Experiments on our Mayo Clinic data, the public MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets show that SafeImpute achieves strong imputation accuracy while providing reliable error control, outperforming diverse baselines in both standard imputation evaluation and FDR-controlled selective-release evaluation.

</details>


### [167] [A Coin Flip Per Token: Bernoulli Sparse Steering of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05615)
*Nima Eshraghi,Lovedeep Gondara,Yuqing Huang,Sagarika Suresh,Leizer Teran,Jithin Pradeep,Xiaotong Xu,Fanny Chevalier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机化稀疏自动编码器（SAE）引导策略，证明仅需对50%的token进行干预即可恢复密集引导的大部分效果，且引导效果取决于序列上的累积信号剂量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE引导方法对每个生成token施加恒定扰动，存在两个问题：一是持续扰动可能损害生成文本的流畅性；二是密集干预是否必要从未被系统验证。作者旨在探究能否以稀疏、随机的干预策略实现同等的行为控制效果。

Method: 提出两种随机化引导策略：(1) 随机Token引导（STS），以概率p对每个token独立进行门控干预；(2) 随机块引导（SBS），仅在序列前导窗口进行一次门控。两种方法均不依赖奖励模型或学习到的门控策略，仅通过随机采样决定是否施加SAE引导信号。

Result: 在两种模型家族和两种行为任务上，仅引导50%的token即可恢复密集引导的大部分效果且保持流畅性；引导低至30%的token仍能超越基于提示词的控制。最优引导强度与干预比例呈反比关系，表明行为结果取决于序列上的累积信号剂量，SAE引导存在速率限制特性。

Conclusion: 密集干预并非必要，SAE介导的行为控制遵循累积剂量原则。随机稀疏引导可在保持控制效果的同时提升生成流畅性，这为高效、低侵入的LLM行为调控提供了新范式。

Abstract: Activation steering via sparse autoencoders (SAEs) enables behavioral control of large language models without task-specific fine-tuning, but standard methods apply the steering signal at every generated token, incurring constant per-token perturbation that risks degrading fluency. We ask: is dense intervention necessary? We introduce Stochastic Token Steering (STS), which gates each token independently with probability $p$, and Stochastic Block Steering (SBS), which gates a leading window once per sequence; neither requires a reward model or learned gating policy. Across two model families and two behavioral tasks, steering only 50% of the tokens recovers most of the dense-steering effect while preserving fluency, and steering as few as 30% surpasses prompt-based control. The optimal steering magnitude scales inversely with the intervention ratio, revealing that SAE-mediated control is rate-limited: the behavioral outcome depends on cumulative signal dosage across a sequence.

</details>


### [168] [Safe Bayesian Optimization with Counterfactual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05620)
*Katherine Avery,Bruno Castro da Silva,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种安全贝叶斯优化方法，利用共形预测为反事实基线结果构建有效的不确定性区间，确保在优化过程中约束违反率不超过用户指定水平，并支持协变量偏移自适应调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多决策场景中，新干预措施只有在不使结果低于既定阈值时才可接受。然而，安全约束所参照的基线策略结果是反事实的、不可观测的，必须通过估计获得，而这些估计存在不确定性。如何在反事实基线结果不确定的情况下，安全地优化目标函数，是核心挑战。

Method: (1) 使用共形预测（conformal prediction）为反事实基线结果构造有效的、有限样本覆盖保证的不确定性区间；(2) 将这些区间集成到安全贝叶斯优化框架中，确保约束违反率控制在用户指定水平以下；(3) 提出针对不同协变量偏移类型的共形估计自适应方法；(4) 提供安全性理论证明、实验验证及敏感性分析。

Result: 实验证明该方法能有效控制约束违反率在指定阈值内，在多种协变量偏移场景下表现稳健，敏感性分析验证了方法对关键假设的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过共形预测与安全贝叶斯优化的结合，可以在反事实基线结果不确定的情况下实现安全优化，保证约束违反率可控，为临床决策等高风险场景提供了可靠的方法论支撑。

Abstract: In many decision-making settings, new interventions are acceptable only if they do not reduce outcomes below some established threshold. For example, in clinical medicine, new treatments are often acceptable only if they do not worsen outcomes relative to an established standard of care. Safe Bayesian optimization maximizes an objective subject to safety constraints. In the setting that we consider here, safety is defined relative to a known baseline policy whose outcomes are counterfactual and therefore unobserved. Thus, the counterfactual outcomes of the baseline policy must be estimated and those (uncertain) estimates must be used to safely optimize the objective. We address this estimation problem by using conformal prediction to construct valid uncertainty intervals for counterfactual baseline outcomes, and we show how these intervals can be integrated into safe Bayesian optimization to ensure that constraint violations occur at or below a user-specified rate. We also show how to adapt these conformal estimates to different kinds of covariate shift. We provide a safety proof, experimental evidence, and a sensitivity analysis.

</details>


### [169] [Intuitionistic Fuzzy Graph Embedded Random Vector Functional Link with Multiview Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05635)
*Vrushank Ahire,Yogesh Kumar,M. A. Ganaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出直觉模糊图嵌入随机向量功能链接多视图学习模型(IFGRVFL-MV)，结合直觉模糊集处理不确定性、图嵌入保持几何结构、多视图学习利用互补特征，在UCI和KEEL数据集上取得优于现有模型的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RVFL模型面临两大挑战：(1)难以有效保持数据的几何关系/拓扑结构；(2)无法充分利用多特征视图中的互补信息。此外，对数据中的不确定性和离群点缺乏鲁棒处理机制。

Method: 提出IFGRVFL-MV模型，包含三个核心组件：(1)直觉模糊集(IFS)——为每个数据点同时分配隶属度和非隶属度，增强对离群点的鲁棒性；(2)图嵌入框架——捕获数据内在的几何结构和拓扑关系，提升泛化性能；(3)多视图学习——从多个特征空间中提取互补信息进行联合学习。三者统一集成到RVFL框架中。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL标准基准数据集上进行实验验证，IFGRVFL-MV在分类准确率上优于现有模型。

Conclusion: IFGRVFL-MV在不确定性和多视图环境下的分类任务中展现出显著优势，是该领域一个有前景的进展。

Abstract: Random Vector Functional Link (RVFL) networks are popular due to their fast training and universal approximation capabilities. However, RVFL models face challenges in preserving geometric relationships and utilizing multiple feature views effectively. To address these limitations we propose the Intuitionistic Fuzzy Graph Embedded Random Vector Functional Link with Multiview Learning (IFGRVFL-MV) model. The proposed approach comprises three key components: intuitionistic fuzzy sets for uncertainty handling, graph embedding to capture intrinsic geometric structures, and multiview learning to use complementary information from multiple feature spaces. The model assigns intuitionistic fuzzy membership and non-membership values to data points making it robust to outliers.
