TL;DR: 本文揭示了线性注意力在图去噪任务中的理论局限性——仅能学习训练分布上的平均谱滤波器，并提出谱注意力（Spectral Attention）及其实用变体图卷积注意力（GCA），通过利用输入图谱实现分布感知的去噪，在理论和实验上均验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于注意力的图去噪方法缺乏原理性理解，标准线性注意力在图去噪目标下是否是最优机制尚不明确。作者发现线性注意力存在根本局限：它只能学习训练分布上的平均谱滤波器，而实际中不同图的谱特性往往差异显著，这促使作者探索能够感知输入图谱的注意力机制。

Method: 首先从理论上证明线性注意力在图去噪中仅能学习平均谱滤波器，并提出谱注意力（Spectral Attention），直接利用输入图谱，其性能优势由分布的谱多样性决定。随后推导出图卷积注意力（GCA），一种置换等变的实用实现，通过图滤波后的查询（queries）和键（keys）实现谱去噪。在随机块模型（SBM）上，GCA可证明匹配理想谱注意力。此外，证明了softmax操作通过将噪声特征向量近似投影到干净特征空间提供额外去噪效果。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，用GCA替换线性注意力一致地提升图去噪和扩散性能，且增益与谱多样性强相关。在DiGress框架中，GCA无需计算昂贵的结构特征即可匹配标准图Transformer的性能；结合PEARL位置编码后，可避免显式特征分解计算，实现更快的推理速度且不损失质量。

Conclusion: 线性注意力在图去噪中存在理论上的根本局限，而基于谱感知的GCA通过利用输入图谱实现了分布感知的去噪，理论上更优且在实验中表现出一致改进。该方法为图扩散模型中的注意力机制设计提供了新的原理性指导。

Abstract: Denoising graphs is a fundamental problem in graph learning and the core operation of graph diffusion models. Attention-based architectures like graph transformers have recently shown promise in denoising graphs. However, our principled understanding of attention-based graph denoising remains limited, making it unclear whether standard attention is the right mechanism for this task. Here we show that, under a denoising objective, linear attention is suboptimal and can only learn an average spectral denoising filter over the training distribution. This creates a fundamental limitation as graphs often vary spectrally across the distribution. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Spectral Attention, which directly utilizes the input graph spectrum and provably outperforms linear attention by a margin governed by the spectral diversity of the distribution. We then derive Graph Convolutional Attention (GCA), a practical and permutation-equivariant realization of this idea that implements spectral denoising through graph-filtered queries and keys. For stochastic block models, GCA provably matches the idealized Spectral Attention mechanism. We further show that the softmax operation, that follows the attention, provides additional denoising by approximately projecting noisy eigenvectors onto the clean eigenspace. Empirically, replacing linear attention with GCA consistently improves graph denoising and diffusion on synthetic and real datasets, with gains strongly correlated with spectral diversity. In DiGress, GCA matches standard graph-transformer performance without computing expensive structural features, and when combined with the recently proposed PEARL positional encodings, avoids explicit eigendecomposition computations resulting in faster inference without degrading quality. The code can be found here: github.com/shervinkhalafi/graph_conv_att

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [High-Dimensional Variance Estimation for the Generalized Regression Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05570)
*Kalil Bouhadra,Mehdi Dagdoug*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维设定下广义回归（GREG）估计量的方差估计问题，推导了常用方差估计量（包括基于Taylor线性化的方法）的高维渐近偏差，并证明在适当的协变量分布假设下，交叉验证方差估计量是渐近无偏的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代调查抽样中，辅助协变量的数量常与样本量同阶，形成高维设定。虽然这提供了更大的建模灵活性，但也给推断带来挑战。现有常用的方差估计方法（如Taylor线性化）在经典低维设定下表现良好，但在高维情形下的理论性质尚不明确，因此需要研究其渐近行为并提出改进方案。

Method: 本文采用渐近理论分析框架，针对广义回归（GREG）估计量，推导了常用方差估计量在高维设定下的渐近偏差解析表达式（包括Taylor线性化类方法）。在此基础上，本文提出采用交叉验证策略构造方差估计量，并在对协变量施加适当分布假设的条件下，证明该估计量自然满足渐近无偏性。

Result: （1）推导了常用方差估计量在高维渐近框架下的偏差理论结果，揭示了其在高维情形下存在系统性偏差；（2）证明了在适当的协变量分布假设下，基于交叉验证的方差估计量是渐近无偏的。

Conclusion: 在高维调查抽样中，传统方差估计方法存在不可忽视的渐近偏差，而交叉验证方差估计量在合理假设下能有效解决该问题，为高维模型辅助估计提供了可靠的推断工具。

Abstract: In survey sampling, the goal is to estimate finite population parameters such as totals, means, and proportions. At the estimation stage, it is common to have access to auxiliary information in the form of covariates known either in aggregate form or for each population unit. These covariates are often used, through models relating them to the variable of interest, to improve efficiency; this approach is known as model-assisted estimation. Modern applications increasingly involve settings where a large number of covariates are observed, sometimes of the same order as the sample size. While this setting offers greater modeling flexibility, it also creates important challenges for inference. In this article, we study variance estimation for the generalized regression (GREG) estimator in high-dimensional regimes. We derive new theoretical results that characterize the high-dimensional asymptotic bias of commonly used variance estimators, including those based on Taylor linearization. Furthermore, under suitable distributional assumptions on the covariates, we show that a cross-validated variance estimator is naturally asymptotically unbiased.

</details>


### [206] [On the Computation of Normalized Power Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05579)
*Fang Chen,Kaifeng Lu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MCMC采样的策略，利用未归一化幂先验下幂参数的边缘分布与归一化常数之间的函数恒等式，在通用贝叶斯软件中近似归一化幂先验，无需显式计算边际似然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化幂先验（normalized power prior）在贝叶斯推断中为融入历史数据提供了原则性框架，但其常规应用因需要计算难以处理的归一化常数函数 $C(a_0)$ 而受到阻碍。现有方法依赖模型特定的边际似然计算、网格数值积分或辅助采样方案，难以在标准贝叶斯软件中直接实现。

Method: 作者利用一个被低估的函数恒等式：在未归一化幂先验下，幂参数的边际分布与归一化幂先验的归一化常数成正比。基于此关系，提出一种采样策略，利用通用MCMC算法的输出近似归一化幂先验。该方法可完全在PROC MCMC、BUGS、JAGS、Stan、NIMBLE等通用贝叶斯软件包内实现，无需显式边际似然计算。

Result: 通过若干示例展示了该方法的实用性，验证了其在应用场景中促进归一化幂先验常规使用的潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法为归一化幂先验的常规应用扫除了计算障碍，使研究者能在标准贝叶斯软件中便捷地融入历史数据，有望推动该方法在各类应用场景中的广泛使用。

Abstract: The normalized power prior provides a principled framework for incorporating historical data into Bayesian inference while preserving coherence, but its routine application has been hindered by the need to evaluate an intractable normalizing constant function $C(a_{0})$. Existing approaches typically rely on model-specific marginal likelihood calculations, numerical integration over grids, or auxiliary sampling schemes implemented outside standard Bayesian software. In this paper, we present a computational perspective that exploits a simple and underutilized functional identity: under the unnormalized power prior, the marginal distribution of the power parameter is proportional to the normalizing constant of the normalized power prior. Leveraging this relationship, we propose a sampling-based strategy to approximate the normalized power prior using output from generic Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) algorithms. The resulting approximation can be implemented entirely within all general Bayesian software packages (such as PROC MCMC, BUGS, JAGS, Stan, or NIMBLE), without requiring explicit marginal likelihood evaluation. Several illustrative examples demonstrate the practicality of the approach and highlight its potential to facilitate the routine use of normalized power priors in applied settings.

</details>


### [207] [Sensitivity and Early Detection of Bayesian Causal Impact Models for Marketing Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05646)
*Jorge Pellegrini*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模拟的框架，用于评估贝叶斯因果影响分析在营销系统运行监控中的检测灵敏度。通过向弃购营销旅程的日流量数据注入受控的性能退化，评估不同效应量、置信水平和检测时间窗口下的告警激活概率。研究发现，基于比例的标准在长监控窗口下失效，而基于连续负累积影响的标准表现更稳定、更具操作意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销系统频繁经历运营变更，需要及时检测其对性能的负面影响。现有的贝叶斯因果影响模型主要用于事后效应估计，其在早期运行监控中的检测能力尚未得到充分探索。因此需要一种量化检测灵敏度的实用方法论。

Method: 基于模拟的评估框架：1）使用弃购营销旅程的日流量数据作为基准；2）向观测结果重复注入受控的性能退化（扰动）；3）在不同效应量、置信水平和检测时间窗口下，评估两种告警标准的告警激活概率——一种是基于观测值低于预测下界的比例，另一种是基于连续多天出现负累积影响。

Result: 检测性能强烈依赖于效应量、置信水平和评估时间窗口三者之间的交互作用。基于比例的标准随监控窗口延长而失效；基于连续负累积影响的持续性标准则提供更稳定且更具操作意义的检测行为。

Conclusion: 该框架将因果影响分析从事后效应估计扩展到事前检测灵敏度量化，为动态营销环境下的监控决策提供了实用方法论。持续性告警标准比比例标准更适合长期运行监控。

Abstract: Marketing systems frequently undergo operational changes that may affect performance, making timely detection of adverse effects essential for decision making. While Bayesian Causal Impact models are widely used to estimate the causal effects of interventions, their ability to support early operational monitoring remains less explored. This paper proposes a simulation based framework to evaluate the sensitivity and detection capabilities of Bayesian causal impact analysis under controlled performance degradations. Using daily traffic data from an abandoned cart marketing journey, we repeatedly perturb the observed outcomes and assess alarm activation probabilities across different effect magnitudes, confidence levels, and detection horizons. Two alarm criteria are analyzed: one based on the proportion of observations falling below the predictive lower bound and another based on consecutive days of negative cumulative impact. Results show that detection performance depends strongly on the interaction between effect size, confidence level, and evaluation horizon. In particular, proportion based criteria become less effective as the monitoring horizon increases, whereas persistence based criteria provide more stable and operationally meaningful detection behavior. The proposed framework extends causal impact analysis beyond retrospective effect estimation, offering a practical methodology for quantifying detection sensitivity and supporting monitoring decisions in dynamic marketing environments.

</details>


### [208] [Exact computation of posterior distribution of mixture weights in hierarchical Bayesian models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05692)
*Georgy Meshcheryakov*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究分层混合模型中混合权重 $\boldsymbol{w}$ 的精确边缘化问题，为两分量情形给出 $O(n^2)$ 动态规划及 $O(n \log^2 n)$ 的 FFT 变体，并证明权重后验是有限个 Beta 分布的混合；三分量及以上情形给出精确联合动态规划。方法在小样本场景优势显著：后验区间校准良好，比采样方法快两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层混合模型（以 Dirichlet 或 Beta-Liouville 为先验）广泛用于异质性数据建模，但混合权重的后验推断通常依赖 MCMC 采样或近似方法（EM、高斯/Laplace 近似），存在计算开销大、边界崩溃、覆盖不足等问题。作者旨在通过精确边缘化克服这些局限，尤其针对小样本场景。

Method: （1）两分量情形：利用对称性和生成函数，设计 $O(n^2)$ 动态规划及基于 FFT 的 $O(n \log^2 n)$ 加速算法，计算边缘似然；证明后验权重为有限 Beta 混合分布，从而获得精确的后验摘要、可信区间和局部错误发现率。（2）$K \ge 3$ 分量情形：给出精确联合动态规划。均无需任何采样。

Result: 在真实多水平荟萃分析、白血病基因表达通路失调分析及已知真相的白血病基因面板基准中，精确方法给出的信号比例区间校准良好，而 EM 方法坍缩至边界无法给出区间，高斯/Laplace 近似覆盖错误；速度上比等效精度的采样方法快两个数量级。在前列腺癌大数据基准中，与 locfdr 在基因排序上一致，并额外给出了零比例的后验区间。

Conclusion: 提出的精确边缘化方法在分层混合模型推断中兼具统计精度与计算效率，尤其在小样本条件下显著优于 EM 和近似方法，为模型选择、多重检验校正等任务提供了可靠且快速的后验推断工具。

Abstract: Hierarchical mixture models are a powerful tool for modeling data generated from heterogeneous sources, particularly when the mixing proportion $\boldsymbol{w}$ itself is treated as a random variable with a Dirichlet or Beta-Liouville prior. Such models are widely employed in scenarios where uncertainty in class membership or data-generating processes must be probabilistically quantified. This paper studies the exact marginalization of the mixture weight. For the two-component case we give an $O(n^2)$ dynamic program -- and an $O(n \log^2 n)$ FFT variant -- for the marginal likelihood, and show that the exact posterior of the weight is a finite mixture of Beta distributions, delivering closed-form posterior summaries, credible intervals and per-observation local false-discovery rates without any sampling. For $K \ge 3$ components we give an exact joint dynamic program. The gain is largest in the small-sample regime the method is built for: on a real multilevel meta-analysis, a pathway-level dysregulation analysis of leukemia gene expression, and a leukemia-derived gene-panel benchmark with known ground truth, the exact interval for the signal proportion is calibrated where EM gives no interval at all (collapsing to a boundary) and Gaussian/Laplace approximations mis-cover, and it is two orders of magnitude faster than the sampler that would match it. On the large prostate-cancer benchmark, where every method has ample data, it agrees with locfdr on the gene ranking while adding a posterior interval for the null proportion.

</details>


### [209] [Income inequality estimation with gamma mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05763)
*Roberto Vila,Helton Saulo,Felipe Quintino*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限伽马混合分布下第m阶基尼指数的估计问题，推导了第m阶基尼指数的闭式表达式及其非参数U统计量估计量的期望和偏差，建立了渐近性质，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟和实际收入数据进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基尼指数是衡量不平等程度的重要指标，而有限混合分布（尤其是伽马混合）在收入建模中应用广泛。现有文献对单一伽马总体的基尼指数已有研究，但针对伽马混合模型下第m阶基尼指数的估计理论尚不完善，需要填补这一空白。

Method: （1）推导了有限伽马混合分布下第m阶基尼指数的闭式表达式；（2）推导了其非参数U统计量估计量的期望与偏差的闭式表达；（3）在同速率参数假设下，建立了估计量的渐近性质（渐近偏差下界、渐近无偏性、强一致性和渐近正态性）；（4）通过蒙特卡洛模拟研究不同速率参数下的估计量表现，并与偏差校正估计量和参数估计量进行对比；（5）使用实际收入数据集进行实证分析。

Result: （1）获得了第m阶基尼指数及其U统计量估计量期望和偏差的闭式表达；（2）在同速率参数条件下，证明了估计量具有渐近无偏性、强一致性和渐近正态性，并给出了渐近偏差下界；（3）蒙特卡洛模拟表明，即使在不同速率参数下该估计量仍表现良好，偏差校正估计量和参数估计量在某些场景下具有更优性能；（4）收入数据的实证分析验证了所提方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文将单一伽马总体下第m阶基尼指数的理论结果成功推广至有限伽马混合模型，填补了该领域的理论空白。理论结果依赖于同速率参数假设，但模拟表明估计量在更一般设定下仍具鲁棒性。该方法为基于伽马混合模型的收入不平等测度提供了有效的统计工具。

Abstract: This paper studies the estimation of the $m$th Gini index under finite mixtures of gamma distributions. We derive closed-form expressions for the $m$th Gini index and for the expectation and bias of its non-parametric U-statistic estimator, extending previous results for both single gamma populations and gamma mixture models. We further establish the asymptotic properties of the estimator for gamma mixtures sharing a common rate parameter, including an asymptotic lower bound for the bias, asymptotic unbiasedness, strong consistency, and asymptotic normality. Although these theoretical results require a common rate parameter, a Monte Carlo study also investigates the estimator under mixtures with different rates and compares its performance with bias-corrected and parametric estimators. Finally, the proposed methodology is illustrated through the analysis of an income dataset.

</details>


### [210] [Optimal Designs with Robust Inference for Binary Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05768)
*David Azriel,Abba M. Krieger,Adam Kapelner*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在Neyman非参数模型下研究二元结局的随机实验，推导出差中差估计量的精确方差，刻画了最小化该方差的实验设计，证明满足协变量平衡条件的任意平衡设计渐近最优，并基于CMH统计量提出适用于任意平衡设计的保守方差估计器，在局部备择假设下渐近紧致，从而构建渐近有效置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元结局（如发病率、成功率）的随机实验在实践中极为常见，但现有文献缺乏在Neyman非参数模型下对最优实验设计的精确刻画，同时由于方差依赖于未知的成功概率，无偏方差估计不可行，迫切需要既能在有限样本下保持保守性又能在渐近意义上紧致的推断框架。

Method: 在Neyman非参数模型（协变量固定、潜在结局随机）下，严格推导出差中差估计量的精确方差表达式；证明任意满足协变量平衡条件的平衡设计是渐近最优的，并验证一大类区组设计在温和光滑性假设下满足该条件；推广Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel统计量使其适用于任意平衡设计，并扩展Robins方差估计器用于区组设计；在局部备择假设下建立CMH估计器渐近紧致的条件。

Result: 理论结果表明，满足协变量平衡和充分随机性的区组设计及其他设计，在配备基于CMH的推断时表现优良；该框架同时实现方差最优、有限样本保守性和渐近紧致性。

Conclusion: 本文构建了一个针对二元发生率结局的实验设计与推断统一框架，该框架在方差意义上最优、有限样本下保守且渐近紧致，为实践者提供了兼具理论保证和实用性的完整解决方案。

Abstract: We study randomized experiments with binary outcomes under Neyman's nonparametric model, where covariate measurements are fixed but potential outcomes are random. In this setting we derive the exact variance of the difference-in-means estimator and characterize designs that minimize it. We show that any balanced design satisfying a covariate-balance condition is asymptotically optimal, and we prove that a broad class of blocking designs satisfies this condition under mild smoothness assumptions. Because the variance depends on unknown success probabilities, unbiased variance estimation is impossible. We therefore develop two conservative estimators: a generalization of the Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel (CMH) statistic applicable to any balanced design, and an extension of Robins' variance estimator for blocking designs. We establish conditions under which the CMH-based estimator is asymptotically tight under local alternatives, thereby yielding asymptotically valid confidence intervals. Our theoretical and simulations results show that blocking and other designs that achieve covariate balance and sufficient degree of randomness, perform well when they are equipped with the CMH-based inference. Thus, we provide an experimental design and inference framework for incidence outcomes that is simultaneously variance-optimal, conservative in finite samples and asymptotically tight.

</details>


### [211] [AI-Augmented Statistical Network Estimation with Proxy Gene Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05774)
*Yan Chen,Weijing Tang,Jin-Hong Du*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: PLANE 是一种自适应加权联合模型，利用外部基础模型生成的代理基因嵌入来改善部分观测的目标基因网络中的潜在结构恢复，并通过块状 Gram 归一化梯度下降分析推导出数据自适应的最优权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因网络通常仅在受限目标集上观测到，而现代生物医学基础模型在更大的基因全域上提供了代理嵌入。如何利用外部嵌入提升部分观测网络的潜在结构恢复是核心动机。

Method: 提出 PLANE 模型，通过共享潜在位置将目标网络与代理嵌入结合；使用零阶最优性分析控制加权重建损失；进一步通过块状 Gram 归一化梯度下降分析，证明对齐的曲率加权误差的确定性收缩，并推导出目标块误差界，从而获得数据自适应的最优网络嵌入权重。

Result: 理论和实验表明，信息性代理嵌入能够改善潜在因子恢复、网络重建以及超出观测目标网络的插补效果；模拟和单细胞扰动分析均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PLANE 提供了一种原则性的方式将外部代理嵌入与部分观测网络融合，通过理论分析揭示了网络与嵌入信息之间的权衡关系，并给出了最优权重的自适应选择方案。

Abstract: Gene--gene networks are often observed only on a restricted target set, while modern biomedical foundation models provide proxy gene embeddings over substantially larger gene universes. To leverage externally learned representations to improve latent-structure recovery in partially observed target networks, we propose \emph{Proxy-Latent Assisted Network Estimation} (PLANE), an adaptively weighted joint network--embedding latent variable model. PLANE combines the two sources of information through the common latent positions of the target network and proxy embeddings. Under mild rank conditions, the target network enables the identification of latent positions and loading of all nodes up to an orthogonal rotation. We show that zero-order optimality analyses sharply control the weighted reconstruction loss, but are insufficient to identify the optimal weighting. To understand the network and embedding information trade-off for latent-factor recovery, we analyze blockwise Gram-normalized gradient descent and prove deterministic contraction of aligned, curvature-weighted errors up to an explicit statistical tolerance. We then specialize the weighted statistical error bound to derive the target-block error bound, yielding an optimal, data-adaptive choice of the network embedding weights. Simulations and single-cell perturbation analyses show that informative proxy embeddings improve latent recovery, network reconstruction, and imputation beyond the observed target network.

</details>


### [212] [Estimating Causal Effects from Data Generated by Stochastic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05792)
*Susan Athey,Guido Imbens,Zoe Ji*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用观测数据估计内容特征因果效应的新方法，要求推荐算法包含一定随机性，并额外记录未曝光项目及其曝光概率比。该方法即使在存在未观测混杂因素（同时影响用户偏好和所考虑项目对）的情况下，也能识别因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推荐系统和聊天机器人中，内容提供者常想知道内容特征（如风格、长度、情感等）是否因果性地提升了用户参与度。但由于内容是个性化定制的、内容库庞大且内容在多个维度上变化，传统观测研究方法难以估计内容特征的因果效应。研究者需要一种在现实数据条件下依然有效的因果推断方法。

Method: 该方法利用推荐算法中的随机性成分，并要求记录两条额外数据：(i) 至少一个本可曝光但实际未曝光给用户的候选项（未曝光项）；(ii) 未曝光项与已曝光项被展示的概率比的估计值。基于这些数据，构建了一个新的估计量，在存在未观测混杂因素（同时影响用户偏好和项目对的选择）的情况下，仍能识别内容特征的因果效应。

Result: 论文证明，在该数据设置下，内容特征的因果效应是可识别的，即使在存在未观测混杂因素的条件下。所提出的估计量在所用数据类型和构造方式上均不同于先前方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用观测数据在个性化推荐场景中估计内容特征因果效应提供了新的方法论工具，扩展了因果推断在推荐系统领域的适用范围，尤其适用于内容库庞大或内容为每个用户唯一生成的场景。

Abstract: Recommendation systems and chatbots present content to users, typically using stochastic algorithms that select the content based on user characteristics or context. Examples of content include chat responses, videos, or items available for purchase. Scientists and application developers are often interested in whether characteristics of content increase outcomes such as user engagement. Estimates of such causal effects may guide content providers to generate content that emphasize desirable features. However, in settings with a large content library or where content is generated uniquely for a given user, it can be difficult to use observational data to learn the causal effect of content features, because the content a user sees is tailored to that user, and because content varies in many dimensions. This paper proposes a new method for estimating the impact of content features using observational data, when the algorithm that determines user exposure incorporates some randomization, and when two additional data elements are logged for each user: $(i)$ the identity of at least one item that could have been exposed to the user, but was not (the unexposed item); $(ii)$ an estimate of the ratio of the probability that the unexposed item would have been shown to the probability that the exposed item was shown. We show that causal effects of features are identified in this setting, even in the presence of unobserved confounders that affect both user preferences and the identity of the considered pair of items (exposed and unexposed). Our estimator differs from prior approaches in terms of what data is used and how the estimator is constructed.

</details>


### [213] [Nested Simulation Methods for Sobol' Index Estimation: Bias Correction, Budget Allocation, and Latin Hypercube Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05809)
*Jingtao Zhang,Xi Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从嵌套模拟的角度重新审视Sobol'指数估计，将经典pick-freeze估计量统一解释为具有固定内层样本量的嵌套模拟估计量，并提出两种基于jackknife的改进估计量；其中分裂jackknife估计量在粗蒙特卡洛下达到规范MSE速率，而标准嵌套模拟与无偏jackknife估计量则受限于较慢的嵌套模拟速率；文章还分析了拉丁超立方抽样的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件期望方差估计是随机模拟中的经典问题，广泛用于全局敏感性分析与Sobol'指数估计。现有pick-freeze估计量与嵌套模拟估计量各自独立发展，缺乏统一框架进行比较，且不同估计量的偏差-方差行为及最优计算预算分配策略尚不清晰。

Method: (1) 建立统一框架：将经典pick-freeze估计量重新解释为固定内层样本量的嵌套模拟估计量；(2) 提出两种jackknife扩展：无偏jackknife估计量和利用独立预样本估计均值的分裂jackknife估计量；(3) 推导各估计量在CMC下的MSE速率及最优外层/内层计算预算分配；(4) 分析LHS对各估计量性能的影响，特别关注jackknife偏差减少在LHS下的退化条件。

Result: 在CMC下，分裂jackknife估计量达到规范MSE速率O(1/N)，优于标准嵌套模拟和无偏jackknife估计量的较慢嵌套模拟速率；LHS可提升标准嵌套模拟估计量的性能，但会削弱jackknife估计量的偏差减少效果，除非内层样本量随总预算同步增长；数值实验验证了理论结果，并为CMC和LHS下的估计量选择提供了实践指导。

Conclusion: 通过嵌套模拟视角统一pick-freeze与嵌套模拟估计量，为Sobol'指数估计提供了清晰的理论比较基础；分裂jackknife估计量在CMC下具有最优MSE速率；LHS虽有利于标准估计量，但对jackknife方法需谨慎使用，内层样本量需随预算增长才能保持偏差减少优势。

Abstract: Estimating the variance of a conditional expectation is a recurring problem in stochastic simulation, with applications in global sensitivity analysis and Sobol' index estimation. This paper revisits Sobol' index estimation through the lens of nested simulation and develops a unified comparison of classical pick-freeze estimators and nested simulation estimators under a common computational budget. We show that several standard pick-freeze estimators can be interpreted as nested simulation estimators with fixed inner-level sample sizes, enabling direct performance comparisons and clarifying their bias-variance behavior. Building on this perspective, we analyze the standard nested simulation estimator for the Sobol' index numerator and propose two jackknife-based extensions: an unbiased jackknife estimator and a split jackknife estimator that uses an independent preliminary sample to estimate the mean. Under crude Monte Carlo (CMC), the split jackknife estimator attains the canonical mean squared error (MSE) rate, whereas the standard nested simulation and unbiased jackknife estimators attain the slower nested simulation rate. We also characterize the associated allocations of outer- and inner-level simulation effort. Finally, we study the impact of Latin hypercube sampling (LHS), showing that it can improve the standard nested simulation estimator while undermining bias reduction in jackknife-based estimators unless the inner-level sample size grows with the total budget. Numerical experiments corroborate the theory and provide practical guidance on estimator selection for Sobol' index estimation under CMC and LHS.

</details>


### [214] [Fast Stochastic Nearest Neighbor Pairwise Composite Likelihood for Massive Spatial Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06142)
*Moreno Bevilacqua,Francisco Cuevas-Pacheco,Christian Caamaño-Carrillo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机加速的近邻配对复合似然方法，通过对确定性近邻候选图中的配对进行随机子采样（伯努利稀疏化或固定预算稀疏化），在大幅降低计算成本的同时保持统计效率。模拟和实际应用表明，每个观测保留两对是一个稳健的统计-计算折中方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于近邻配对加权复合似然是空间随机场全似然推断的可扩展替代方案，但当需要较大近邻邻域时计算成本仍然较高。作者希望在不牺牲太多统计效率的前提下进一步加速协方差拟合。

Method: 提出随机加速策略：首先构建确定性的近邻候选图，然后仅对其中随机子集的配对贡献进行估计。考虑两种稀疏化设计：(1) Bernoulli稀疏化，在期望意义上控制保留配对数量；(2) 固定预算稀疏化，通过逐目标无放回抽样严格满足精确预算。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在Matérn协方差模型下，每个观测保留两个配对即可实现稳健的统计-计算平衡。与Vecchia型高斯近似相比，随机近邻配对估计器在需要大幅减少协方差拟合时间且可接受适度效率损失时具有速度优势。这种折中对均值、尺度和基台参数尤为有利，效率损失主要集中在平滑度参数估计上。在250万WorldClim观测数据的应用中，预测精度与Vecchia基准几乎不可区分，而协方差拟合时间显著缩短。

Conclusion: 随机近邻配对复合似然方法是一种有效的空间统计计算加速策略，以最小的预测精度损失换取显著的计算时间节省，尤其适用于大规模空间数据集。

Abstract: Weighted pairwise composite likelihoods based on nearest-neighbor (NN) pairs provide a scalable alternative to full likelihood inference for spatial random fields, but can remain expensive when moderately large NN neighborhoods are needed. We propose a stochastic acceleration that constructs the deterministic NN candidate graph and evaluates only a randomized subset of its pairwise contributions.
  We consider two thinning designs: Bernoulli thinning, which controls the retained-pair budget in expectation, and fixed-budget thinning, which enforces an exact budget through target-wise sampling without replacement. Simulation studies for Matérn covariance models suggest that, in the settings considered, retaining two pairs per observation provides a stable statistical--computational compromise. The stochastic NN pairwise estimators provide a faster alternative to a Vecchia-type Gaussian approximation when substantial reductions in covariance-fitting time are desired and a modest loss of efficiency is acceptable. This trade-off is especially favorable for the mean, scale, and sill parameters, while the main efficiency loss is concentrated on smoothness estimation.
  In an application to July average temperature over the western--central United States, based on 2.5 million WorldClim observations, the proposed estimators achieve predictive accuracy essentially indistinguishable from the Vecchia benchmark, with substantially shorter covariance-fitting time.

</details>


### [215] [Error Propagation in Spectral Functionals of Shrinkage Covariance Estimators: Perturbation Bounds and Calibrated Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06373)
*Ahmad Koman*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为滚动协方差估计中常用的市场结构指标（主导特征空间投影子移动量和标量谱泛函如吸收比率、领先特征值份额）建立了统计校准理论。针对特征空间，推导了重叠窗口下投影子差异的一阶零分布，并证明其可迁移至旋转等变收缩估计量；提出了基于Davis-Kahan界限和自助法的校准检验及最小可检测旋转的设计规则。针对标量泛函，证明了一阶免疫椭圆峰度仅对尺度不变泛函成立，从而可用单一估计标量校准投影子零分布及椭圆族下的区间。在高维情形下，提出了保持迹的尖峰去偏估计量以消除收缩带来的吸收比率偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滚动协方差估计产生的特征空间投影子移动量和标量谱泛函（如吸收比率、领先特征值份额）常被用作市场结构变化的指标。然而，这些指标在估计噪声下会自然波动，收缩估计会改变该噪声的分布规律，因此直接将波动解读为结构变化需要经过统计校准。目前缺乏针对这类重叠窗口下谱统计量的严格分布理论和校准方法。

Method: 本文分两部分：(1) 对主导特征空间，推导重叠窗口间投影子Frobenius距离的一阶零分布，证明该分布在旋转等变收缩估计量下保持不变；利用分布自由的Davis-Kahan界限判断特征空间是否可识别；构建基于自助法的校准检验，并给出最小可检测旋转的近似设计规则。(2) 对标量谱泛函，证明一阶免疫椭圆峰度性质仅对尺度不变泛函成立；利用该性质，用一个估计标量即可校准投影子零分布以及吸收比率和领先份额在椭圆族下的置信区间。针对高维情形下收缩导致的吸收比率偏差，提出保持迹的尖峰去偏（spike-debiased）估计量。

Result: 推导了重叠窗口投影子差异的一阶零分布及其对旋转等变收缩估计量的不变性；建立了基于Davis-Kahan界限和自助法的校准检验框架；证明了一阶免疫椭圆峰度对尺度不变标量泛函的充要性；提出的尖峰去偏估计量可消除高维收缩对吸收比率的偏差。理论结果在已知总体协方差下通过模拟验证，并在未知总体下的权益面板数据附录中展示了诊断应用。

Conclusion: 滚动协方差谱统计量作为市场结构诊断工具，必须经过严格的统计校准才能可靠使用。本文为特征空间移动量和标量谱泛函分别建立了完整的校准理论框架，填补了重叠窗口推断和收缩估计下谱统计量分布理论的空白，并提供了实用的检验和去偏工具。

Abstract: Rolling covariance estimates feed two objects that are routinely treated as market structure. The first is the dominant eigenspace, monitored through the projector movement $\widehat D_{K,t}=\|\widehat P_{K,t}-\widehat P_{K,t-1}\|_F$; the second comprises scalar spectral functionals such as the absorption ratio and the leading-eigenvalue share. Both fluctuate under estimation noise, and shrinkage changes the law of that noise, so reading their movements as structural change requires calibration. For the eigenspace, we derive a first-order null law for $\widehat D_{K,t}$ between overlapping windows that share most of their data and show that it transfers without change to rotation-equivariant shrinkage estimators. A distribution-free Davis-Kahan band gauges whether the eigenspace is identified, an estimator-aware bootstrap provides the calibrated test, and a companion power analysis gives an approximate design rule for the smallest detectable rotation. For the scalar functionals, we show that first-order immunity to elliptical kurtosis holds for scale-invariant functionals and only for them, so that one estimated scalar calibrates the projector null and the absorption-ratio and leading-share intervals across the elliptical family. In high dimensions, where shrinkage cleaning biases the absorption ratio, we give a trace-preserving spike-debiased estimator that removes the bias. The results are verified by simulation under a known population covariance; an equity-panel appendix shows the procedures as diagnostics when the population is unknown.

</details>


### [216] [An Experimental Design Approach to Evaluating Agentic AI's Autonomous Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06413)
*Hao He,Xueying Liu,Chris J. Kuhlman,Xinwei Deng*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实验设计与分析框架，将LLM编码代理视为随机模型发现算子，系统评估其在开放数据建模中的发现过程、变异性和关键影响因素，并通过网络词形成游戏测试台进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM编码代理在开放数据建模中的行为具有随机性和自适应性，单次基准测试无法充分表征其自主模型发现过程。因此需要一种系统性的实验设计和分析框架，以量化该过程的可变性并识别重要影响因素。

Method: 将LLM编码代理形式化为随机模型发现算子，映射任务数据与优化目标到拟合模型。以Codex和Claude Code为研究对象，控制推理努力程度、任务类型、优化指标和训练数据组成等实验因素。对每个代理-任务-指标组合，对输出质量、美元成本、墙钟时间和过程复杂度进行回归建模与推断，并提出效用对齐的典型分解方法，刻画推理努力效应的主导方向及其与性能-成本效用方向的一致性。

Result: 在网络词形成游戏测试台上验证了该框架，揭示了推理努力程度在成本和过程复杂度方面的若干洞察性发现。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效系统评估LLM编码代理的模型发现行为，量化其变异性和关键影响因素，为理解和优化代理的推理努力与成本、复杂度之间的权衡提供了方法论支持。

Abstract: Large language model coding agents increasingly perform open-ended data modeling and analysis. These agents are stochastic and adaptive, and therefore their autonomous model discovery behavior cannot be adequately characterized by a single benchmark run. In this work, we propose an experimental design and analysis framework for systematically evaluating this discovery process, quantifying its variability, and identifying important factors. The proposed framework treats these agents as stochastic model-discovery operators, which map task-specific discovery data and an optimization target to a fitted model. Specifically, we investigate two such operators, Codex and Claude Code, under controlled experimental factors including agent's reasoning effort, task, optimization metric, and composition of training data. For each agent-task-metric combination, regression models and inference are conducted for multiple responses such as output quality, dollar cost, wall-clock time, and process complexity. Furthermore, we develop a utility-aligned canonical decomposition to characterize the dominant direction of the reasoning-effort effect and to assess whether that direction aligns with a performance-cost utility direction. The proposed framework is demonstrated on a testbed of networked word-forming games with insightful findings on reasoning effort with respect to cost and process complexity.

</details>


### [217] [Embracing Spillover: Spatial Effects in Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06506)
*Samuel I. Watson,Penny Hancock,Jack Fraser-Govil,Kieran DeWalt,Thomas A. Smith*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个空间实验分析框架，将因果估计量（直接效应、间接效应、总效应、剂量-反应效应）表示为溢出核的线性泛函。在固定暴露集规模的聚类随机化下，溢出核的形状可识别，但其水平不可识别——与截距和直接效应完全混杂。任何估计量都必须依赖一个锚定假设来固定该水平。作者推导了锚定估计量偏差的精确分解，并提出"水平杠杆"这一标量来刻画估计量对未识别水平的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间实验中的干预效应会跨实验单元溢出，但现有方法在识别和处理溢出效应时存在根本性的识别问题：在聚类随机化设计中，溢出核的水平（level）与直接效应和截距项完全混淆，导致任何估计量都依赖于不可检验的锚定假设。亟需一个统一框架来理解这一识别困境，并系统性地评估不同识别策略的偏差-方差权衡。

Method: 作者将因果估计量（直接、间接、总效应及剂量-反应效应）统一刻画为溢出核（spillover kernel）的线性泛函。在聚类随机化且暴露集规模固定的条件下，证明数据的矩条件仅能识别核的形状而无法识别其水平。引入"水平杠杆"（level leverage）概念，量化每个估计量对未识别水平的暴露程度，并推导出锚定估计量偏差的精确分解：水平项 + 核误设带来的形状误差 + 由实际几何布局吸收的漏损项。将聚类试验分析、伯努利随机化、有界衰减假设、紧支撑假设等现有方法统一为锚定选择的不同实例，并提出设计增强（design augmentation）的分类法及"何时增强、何时锚定"的决策准则。

Result: （1）在固定暴露集规模的聚类随机化下，溢出核的水平本质不可识别，与截距和直接效应完全混淆，无论样本量多大、结果模型如何； （2）导出偏差的精确三部分分解，各项均可在数据收集前从设计阶段计算； （3）单一标量"水平杠杆"统一刻画了水平偏差大小、方差成本及估计量对锚定假设的敏感度； （4）经典聚类试验分析是隐式锚定的特例，其污染偏差可写为闭式解； （5）给出了设计增强的分类体系和锚定/增强的决策准则。

Conclusion: 在空间实验中，溢出核的水平识别是一个根本性的设计问题而非估计问题。研究者必须在"锚定"（接受水平偏差但保持设计简单）与"增强设计"（通过额外设计要素购买水平识别）之间做出明确选择。水平杠杆为这一决策提供了定量化的指导工具。该框架为空间实验的设计与分析提供了统一的视角和可操作的实践指南。

Abstract: Interventions delivered in space generate effects that spill over between experimental units. We develop a framework for spatial experiments in which causal estimands, inclduing direct, indirect, total, and dose--response effects, are linear functionals of a spillover kernel. Under cluster randomisation with a fixed exposure set size, the data identify the shape of the kernel but not its level: the level is aliased with the intercept and direct effect, at any sample size and under any outcome model. Every estimator therefore rests on an anchoring assumption that fixes the level. We derive an exact decomposition of the bias of any anchored estimator into a level term, a shape error from kernel misspecification, and a leakage term absorbed by the realised geometry, each computable from the design before data collection. A single scalar, the level leverage, gives each estimand's exposure to the unidentified level, the exact level bias, and the variance cost of estimating the level instead. The conventional cluster-trial analysis is the special case of an implicit anchor, with contamination bias in closed form. Existing approaches, including identification through Bernoulli randomisation, elicited bounds on interference decay, and assumed compact support, are, within the linear exposure mapping, anchoring choices in this framework. We give a taxonomy of design augmentations that purchase the level and a criterion for when to augment and when to anchor.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Prompt-to-Paper: Agentic AI System for Bioinformatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05456)
*Ramsha Kamran,Maheera Amjad,Zartasha Mustansar,Arsalan Shaukat,Salma Sherbaz,Muhammad U. S. Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Prompt-to-Paper是一个多智能体框架，通过确定性检索增强生成、自主编码代理执行真实实验、八维自动质量评分器与质量驱动改进循环，解决AI生成论文中引用不可验证、实验结果虚构、缺乏标准化评估三大缺陷。在五个生物信息学案例中，系统生成了零无效引用的提交格式PDF，改进循环将质量平均提升+17.96分（百分制），人工评审平均7.0/10分，每篇成本约0.31美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动论文生成系统存在三个关键缺陷：(1) 生成的声明缺乏可验证文献的确定性支撑，(2) 实验结果常被虚构而非真实执行，(3) 缺乏标准化的多维度评估框架来衡量AI生成论文是否达到真实发表所需的质量与严谨性。Prompt-to-Paper正是为填补这一评估空白而提出。

Method: 系统包含三项集成创新：(1) 确定性检索增强生成管道，含章节感知相关性评分与滚雪球式引文扩展，将每条声明锚定在60-100篇可验证论文语料库上；(2) 自主编码代理，执行真实计算生物学实验，将合成输出替换为真实数值结果；(3) 八维自动质量评分器，以已发表论文的近似参考统计量为基准，并加入显式幻觉惩罚，提供标准化、可复现的质量评估。质量驱动改进循环使用上下文丰富的修订器，每次迭代路由至三种研究者动作之一，每十次迭代触发一次深度研究循环以重新运行实验并从更强输出重新生成论文。

Result: 在五个生物信息学案例研究中，所有案例均生成了符合投稿格式的PDF，且零超出范围引用。改进循环将论文质量在0-100量表上平均提升+17.96分（最大+26.04）。作为部分外部检验，人工评审员对五篇论文的平均评分为7.0/10。每篇完整论文的生产成本约0.31美元。

Conclusion: Prompt-to-Paper展示了通过确定性RAG、真实代码执行与标准化质量评估相结合，能够以极低成本生成具备可验证引用和真实实验结果的高质量AI论文，为自动化论文生成设立了新的基准框架。

Abstract: While recent advances in large language models have enabled end-to-end automated manuscript generation, existing systems suffer from three critical deficiencies: (i) generated claims are not deterministically grounded in verifiable literature, (ii) experimental results are frequently fabricated rather than executed, and (iii) there exists no standardized, multi-dimensional framework to assess whether AI-generated manuscripts meet the quality and rigor required for real-world publication. We present Prompt-to-Paper, a multi-agent framework that directly addresses this evaluation gap through three integrated innovations. First, a deterministic retrieval-augmented generation pipeline with section-aware relevance scoring and snowball citation expansion grounds every claim in a verifiable corpus of 60--100 papers. Second, an autonomous coding agent executes real computational biology experiments replacing synthetic outputs with genuine numerical results. Third, an eight-dimensional automated quality scorer, benchmarked with approximate reference statistics from published papers and augmented with explicit hallucination penalties, provides standardized, reproducible quality assessments. The quality-driven improvement loop uses a context-rich reviser that routes each iteration to one of three researcher actions and fires a deep research cycle every ten iterations to re-run experiments and re-manuscript from stronger outputs. We validate the system on five bioinformatics case studies; all five cases compiled submission-formatted PDFs with zero out-of-range citations. The improvement loop raises manuscript quality by an average of +17.96 points on a 0--100 scale (maximum +26.04. As partial external checks, a human reviewer scored the five manuscripts at an average of 7.0 out of 10. Complete manuscripts are produced at approximately 0.31 USD per paper.

</details>


### [219] [From Graphs to Gradients: Physics-Inspired Structural Attribution for Cyber-Physical IoT Systems and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05563)
*Spyridon Evangelatos,Christos Diou,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos,Evangelos Markakis,Panagiotis Sarigiannidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受统计力学启发的框架，通过无向、基于能量的表示来建模信息物理物联网系统中变量间的依赖关系，无需恢复有向因果图即可实现依赖感知的归因分析，在工业物联网安全测试中展现出优于现有图方法的归因准确性、鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释性方法主要关注输入与输出变量之间的相关性，而因果解释方法虽然通过干预性问题提供更稳健的洞察，但在大规模混合信息物理系统（具有反馈回路和部分可观测性）中恢复显式有向因果结构往往不切实际。因此需要一种无需完整有向因果图即可进行可靠依赖感知归因的新方法。

Method: 提出一种受统计力学启发的框架，采用无向、基于能量的表示来建模信息物理物联网系统中变量间的依赖关系。通过分析能量景观的变化如何反映单个组件的影响来进行依赖感知归因，并支持对混合交互中扰动效应进行推理，从而在不恢复有向因果图的前提下提供异常行为的可靠解释。

Result: 在具有混合连续和离散变量的工业物联网测试平台上进行仿真实验，结果表明该方法相比最先进的图方法，在归因准确性、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面均有更优表现。

Conclusion: 虽然该归因方法并非旨在完全恢复系统的生成动态，但它提供了有价值的依赖感知解释，可支持人工解读以及下游预测和诊断任务。除工业物联网安全外，该框架还适用于其他需要原则性结构解释的高维信息物理系统和社会技术系统。

Abstract: Interpretable explanation methods in Artificial Intelligence aim to uncover the underlying causes and their effects, enabling a deeper understanding of why a system behaves in a certain way under different inputs. Unlike traditional explainability methods, which mainly highlight correlations between input and output variables, causal explanation focuses on interventional questions. By doing so, it provides more robust insights, helping users understand automated decisions, especially in high-risk domains. Recovering an explicit directed causal structure, however, is often impractical in large-scale, hybrid cyber-physical systems with feedback loops and partial observability. This paper introduces a novel framework inspired by statistical mechanics that instead models variable dependencies through an undirected, energy-based representation of cyber-physical IoT systems. Our approach enables rigorous dependency-aware attribution by analysing how variations in the energy landscape reflect the influence of individual components, without recovering a directed causal graph. It also supports reasoning about perturbation effects across hybrid interactions, providing reliable explanations of abnormal behaviours. We empirically examined our framework through simulations on an industrial IoT testbed with hybrid continuous and discrete variables, demonstrating higher attribution accuracy, improved robustness and better scalability than state-of-the-art graph-based approaches. While the attributions are not intended to fully recover the system's generative dynamics, they provide valuable, dependency-aware explanations supporting both human interpretation and downstream predictive and diagnostic tasks. Although demonstrated in industrial IoT security, our framework also applies to other high-dimensional cyber-physical and socio-technical systems requiring principled, structural explanations.

</details>


### [220] [CSTutorBench: Benchmarking Small Language Models as Tutors for Block-Based Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05571)
*H. Chad Lane,Bryson Kageler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CSTutorBench，一个用于评估语言模型在VEX VR模块化编程环境中担任CS导师能力的基准，包含17个场景问题，通过人机协作的LLM-as-judge流程进行评分。评估11个模型后发现，模型在表面指标（词汇、语气）表现良好，但在避免答案泄露和参与学生调试历史方面存在不足，模型家族和指令微调方式比参数量更能预测辅导质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为AI导师的探索日益增多，但在K-12教育场景部署面临隐私、成本和对闭源模型依赖等问题。小语言模型提供了一种替代方案，然而为目标教育场景（如模块化编程）选择合适的模型仍面临挑战，因为该领域在模型训练数据中严重缺失。

Method: 构建了CSTutorBench基准，包含17个基于VEX VR模块化机器人编程环境的场景问题。评分依据基于成熟辅导与反馈研究的教学评估量规，采用human-in-the-loop的LLM-as-judge评估流水线。对11个不同规模的模型（4B-120B参数）进行评测，并引入基于教育提示工程研究的提示词修订策略。

Result: 所有模型在词汇和语气等表层标准上表现良好，但在深层教学行为上存在显著困难，尤其是在避免直接泄露答案和有效利用学生调试历史方面。模型家族和指令微调方式比单纯的参数量更能预测辅导质量。针对性提示词修订后，11个模型中有10个的评分得到提升。

Conclusion: 在教育场景部署中，为小语言模型选择提供上下文特定、基于教学理论支撑的基准至关重要。CSTutorBench揭示了当前模型在深层教学行为上的共同短板，并表明提示工程可有效改善模型的教学表现。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly explored as AI tutors, yet deploying them in K-12 settings raises concerns around privacy, cost, and reliance on proprietary models. Small language models (SLMs) offer a promising alternative, but selecting the right model for a specific educational context remains difficult, particularly when the target domain, such as block-based programming, is largely absent from model training data. We introduce CSTutorBench, a benchmark for evaluating language models as CS tutors in VEX VR, a block-based robotics environment. The benchmark comprises 17 scenario-based questions scored against a pedagogical rubric grounded in established tutoring and feedback research, with a human-in-the-loop LLM-as-judge pipeline for evaluation. Preliminary findings across 11 models (4B-120B parameters) reveal that models perform well on surface-level criteria such as vocabulary and tone but struggle with deeper pedagogical behaviors, particularly avoiding answer leakage and engaging with student debugging histories. In our sample, model family and instruction-tuning approach appear to be better predictors of tutoring quality than parameter count alone, though the small number of models limits the strength of this conclusion. A targeted prompt revision grounded in recent educational prompt engineering research improved scores for 10 of 11 models. These results underscore the value of context-specific, pedagogically grounded benchmarks for SLM selection in educational deployment.

</details>


### [221] [Foundation Models for Automatic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05573)
*J de Curtò,Victoria Guillén,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMForge 是一个多模型文本到CAD框架，集成JSON模式验证、分析特征评分、网格合成和多轮迭代优化，在97个工程问题基准上评估7个基础模型。IterTracer使用基于渲染的分析指标，IterVision使用VLM语义批评，后者在领先模型上实现100%水密网格生成，但在旋转对称几何体上存在视觉与语义评分分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM和VLM自动生成参数化3D CAD机械零件的可行性，填补基础模型在工程CAD自动生成领域缺乏统一评估标准的空白。

Method: 提出LLMForge多模型文本到CAD框架，结合JSON模式验证、分析特征评分、网格合成和多轮迭代优化。设计两种批评机制：IterTracer使用Phong着色光线追踪渲染器和分析视觉指标（轮廓IoU、孔可见性、边缘间隙、长宽比一致性）；IterVision用Qwen2.5-VL-72B作为VLM语义批评器，通过思维链视觉推理评估渲染视图。在涵盖四类几何家族（带孔和螺栓圆板、多特征盒子、法兰圆柱、L型支架）的97个设计问题上评估DeepSeek-V3.2、Qwen3-235B-A22B、Llama-3.3-70B、Gemma-3-27B、GLM-4.5、MiniMax-M2.1、INTELLECT七个模型。

Result: IterTracer下前四名模型整体均分在[0.885, 0.890]紧密聚集，网格成功率达98.97%，紧凑型指令微调模型可匹敌更大系统。IterVision的VLM批评使领先模型实现100%水密网格生成，但在圆柱等旋转对称几何上视觉与语义评分分歧最大。

Conclusion: 基础模型可自动生成工程级参数化CAD设计，分析反馈和VLM语义反馈各有优势，旋转对称几何仍是难点。对工业工作流程和可扩展自动化机械设计具有启示意义。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable the automatic generation of parametric 3D designs from natural-language specifications. This chapter presents an empirical study of foundation models for automatic Computer-Aided Design (CAD) generation of mechanical parts, using a unified evaluation pipeline and a curated benchmark of 97 engineering design problems. We introduce LLMForge, a multi-model text-to-CAD framework integrating JSON-schema validation, analytic feature scoring, mesh synthesis, and multi-round iterative refinement, studied under two critique regimes. IterTracer uses a Phong-shaded ray-trace renderer with analytic visual metrics (silhouette IoU, hole visibility, edge clearance, aspect-ratio conformance) for lightweight geometry-aware feedback across rounds. IterVision replaces the analytic scorer with a VLM semantic critic (Qwen2.5-VL-72B) that evaluates rendered views via chain-of-thought visual reasoning, assessing spatial coherence and design intent. On a benchmark spanning four canonical geometry families (plates with holes and bolt circles, multi-feature boxes, flanged cylinders, and L-brackets), we evaluate seven foundation models: DeepSeek-V3.2, Qwen3-235B-A22B, Llama-3.3-70B, Gemma-3-27B, GLM-4.5, MiniMax-M2.1, and INTELLECT. Under IterTracer, the four highest-ranked models form a tight cluster (overall mean in [0.885, 0.890]) with 98.97% mesh success, showing that compact instruction-tuned models can match substantially larger systems. VLM-based critique in IterVision yields 100% watertight mesh generation on the leading model while surfacing systematic difficulty on rotationally symmetric geometries such as cylinders, where visual and semantic scoring diverge most. We discuss benchmark design, failure modes, CAD-oriented prompting, and implications for industrial workflows and scalable automated mechanical design.

</details>


### [222] [Narrative World Model: Narratology-Grounded Writer Memory for Long-Form Fiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05577)
*Mohammad Saifullah,Thomas Kornmaier,Taaha Kazi,Vasu Sharma,Aditya Sanjiv Kanade,Aanand Kumar Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NWM 是一个面向长篇小说创作的记忆系统，采用叙事学基础的类型化时序状态图与查询条件混合检索，在叙事多跳问答上显著优于 Graphiti/Zep、GraphRAG 及平面检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇虚构作品的作者需要能够回答关于故事状态的多跳叙事问题（如谁知道秘密、何时知晓、铺垫是否回收、关系如何变化），但现有通用检索和智能体记忆系统只表征实体与事实，缺乏叙事结构建模能力，导致无法提供正确证据。

Method: 提出叙事世界模型（NWM），核心包含两部分：(1) 基于叙事学的类型化时序状态图，建模叙事结构而非仅建模实体-事实；(2) 查询条件混合检索机制。评估时固定使用 Opus 4.8 作为统一阅读器，仅基于各系统提供的章节安全证据作答，在可复现的公开语料库和验证过的多跳基准上进行测试。

Result: NWM 在两个语料库的多跳叙事问答上均显著优于最强基线 Graphiti/Zep（Rasmussen et al., 2025），且远超 GraphRAG 和平面检索。该优势源于表征层面（叙事学基础结构和查询条件检索），而非抽取器质量或图规模的人为因素——即便用 NWM 的抽取器重建基线，优势依然存在。

Conclusion: 面向叙事多跳问答的记忆系统，关键在于将叙事学结构纳入世界模型，而非仅提升实体-事实抽取能力。NWM 的类型化时序状态图与混合检索设计为此提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Long-form fiction writers need memory that answers multi-hop questions about evolving story state: who knows a secret and when they learned it, whether an event preceded the narration that revealed it, whether a setup paid off, and how a relationship shifted. General-purpose retrieval and agent-memory systems represent entities and facts but not the narratological structure these questions turn on, so they surface the wrong evidence or none at all. We introduce the Narrative World Model (NWM), a writer-memory system that pairs a narratology-grounded typed temporal-state graph with query-conditioned hybrid retrieval. To measure memory rather than the answerer, we read every system through a single held-constant Opus 4.8 reader over only that system's chapter-safe evidence, on a reproducible public corpus and a validated multi-hop benchmark, and we compare against the strongest existing temporal-knowledge-graph agent-memory framework, Graphiti/Zep (Rasmussen et al., 2025). NWM substantially and significantly outperforms this baseline on multi-hop narratological QA across both corpora, and far exceeds GraphRAG and flat retrieval. The advantage is representational rather than an artifact of extraction: it survives rebuilding the baseline with NWM's own extractor, and traces to its narratology-grounded structure and query-conditioned retrieval, not to graph size or extractor quality.

</details>


### [223] [FirstResearch: Auditable Question Formation for LLM Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05682)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FirstResearch 提出了一种基于"第一性原理"的研究问题构建框架，核心产物是一份结构化的"研究问题证书"，包含原始定义、假设、机制模型、可证伪假设、最小决定性测试等要素，使LLM生成的科研问题可被审计，实验表明其显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅助科学发现的系统（如AI co-scientist、Agent Laboratory等）在生成首个研究问题时缺乏可审计性——问题听起来合理，但科学家难以检查其背后的机制、可证伪条件和隐含假设。需要一种让研究问题可被检视的机制。

Method: 提出FirstResearch框架，核心是结构化的"Research Question Certificate"（研究问题证书），记录：原始定义、假设、机制模型、矛盾/张力、可证伪假设、最小决定性测试和失败更新规则。通过LLM代理按第一性原理推导生成证书，使研究问题在下游执行前可被审查。采用DeepSeek-blind-judge和Gemini-2.5-Flash独立评审进行评分，并与三类基线（AI co-scientist、Agent Laboratory、AI Scientist-v2风格提示）对比，同时进行消融实验。

Result: 在十个研究主题上，FirstResearch 在两个评审协议下均优于所有基线：Gemini独立评审中 FirstResearch 得分4.86/5，最强基线4.38/5，Pearson一致性0.865。消融实验中，仅保留证书核心组件得分4.90/5（DeepSeek）和4.88/5（Gemini），而移除证书后得分降至1/5以下。

Conclusion: 显式的推导约束是使LLM生成的科学问题更具可审计性的一种有前景的机制。虽然结果基于LLM评审而非人类专家，仍需进一步验证，但初步支持了"结构化的第一性原理推导能改善科研问题质量"这一窄声明。代码已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: LLM systems for scientific discovery increasingly assist with ideation, literature synthesis, experiment planning, and report generation, but the first research question they propose can remain difficult to audit: it may sound plausible without exposing the mechanism, falsifier, or assumption that a scientist should inspect. We introduce FirstResearch, a first-principles research-question formation framework for scientific LLM agents whose core artifact is a structured Research Question Certificate. The certificate records primitive definitions, assumptions, a mechanism model, a tension or contradiction, a falsifiable hypothesis, a minimal decisive test, and a failure update rule, making the proposed question inspectable before downstream execution. On ten LLM-agent research topics, FirstResearch outperforms controlled prompt-level baselines inspired by AI co-scientist, Agent Laboratory, and AI Scientist-v2 under a primary DeepSeek-blind-judge protocol. A Gemini-2.5-Flash independent-judge rescore of the same 40 baseline packages preserves the system-level ranking, with FirstResearch scoring 4.86/5 versus 4.38/5 for the strongest baseline and Pearson agreement of 0.865 on average score. A one-repeat ablation checkpoint further suggests that the certificate-centered core is the strongest component: certificate-only scoring reaches 4.90/5 under DeepSeek and 4.88/5 under Gemini, while removing certificates drops below 1/5 under both judges. These results are preliminary and use LLM judges rather than human domain experts, but they support a narrow scientific-discovery claim: explicit derivation constraints are a promising mechanism for making LLM-generated scientific questions more auditable. Code, prompts, saved outputs, and reproduction scripts are available at https://github.com/louiswang524/FirstResearch.

</details>


### [224] [Memory in the Loop: In-Process Retrieval as ExtendedWorking Memory for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05690)
*Yusuf Khan,Carlo Lipizzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证将语言智能体的记忆从循环外部（每回合查询一次）移入循环内部（每步读写），关键在于用进程内存储（~100μs）替代网络存储（数十至数百毫秒），将延迟压缩三个数量级，使"每步税"崩溃。基于延展心智论的均等原则，足够快的存储成为扩展工作记忆，而非仅被咨询的工具。实验证明：延迟与冗余行为单调正相关；端到端回忆从0/5提升至3.6-4.8/5；瓶颈转移至嵌入环节（~200-400ms），配以本地轻量嵌入器可将完整操作压至~40μs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言智能体的记忆位于推理循环之外，每回合最多查询一次，限制了其认知能力。将记忆移入循环内（每步读写）的障碍始终是延迟——网络存储的响应时间在数十到数百毫秒，使端到端延迟膨胀高达83倍。既有工作通过调度隐藏延迟或限制检索频率来管理成本，但未质疑该成本的根源。作者认为延迟是存储位置而非循环内模式的属性，因此值得重新审视这一架构选择。

Method: 核心方法是将存储从网络侧迁移到进程内，使查询延迟从~100ms降至~100μs（三个数量级）。理论框架基于延展心智论（extended-mind thesis）的均等原则（parity principle）：足够快且持续可用的外部存储构成扩展工作记忆。实验设计：(1) 固定每回合记忆-延迟预算，仅改变存储响应速度，测量冗余动作数量与延迟的关系，采用精确置换检验；(2) 在四类GPT-5级模型上有界窗口下进行端到端回忆任务评估；(3) 将进程内存储与小型本地嵌入器配对，测量完整的每步操作延迟。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 冗余动作与延迟单调正相关——进程内速度下12次中冗余0.0次，110ms云往返下冗余7.2次（p=0.0079）；(2) 端到端回忆：无循环内记忆时为0/5，有循环内记忆时提升至3.6-4.8/5；但"每轮复述"基线也能完美解决，代价是token成本随工作集增长；(3) 存储在244次写入中零丢失，所有遗漏均源于智能体的读取策略而非存储；(4) 瓶颈转移：每步主导成本变为嵌入（~200-400ms网络调用），配本地嵌入器后可压缩至~40μs。

Conclusion: 进程内存储消除了延迟障碍，使循环内记忆在工程上变得可行，将外部存储从被咨询的工具转变为扩展工作记忆。这从根本上改变了智能体架构的设计空间——延迟不再是每步检索的限制因素。然而，回忆性能的瓶颈转移到智能体的读取策略上，暗示未来研究应聚焦于优化读取策略和本地化嵌入，而非存储速度。

Abstract: Language agents run a loop - observe, reason, act - but the memory they reason over sits outside it: a store queried at most once per turn. We study the regime where memory moves inside the loop, read and written on every step. The obstacle has always been latency: networked stores answer in tens to hundreds of milliseconds, and in-loop retrieval can inflate end-to-end latency by up to 83x when retrieval is expensive. Prior work manages that cost rather than questioning it: serving-layer scheduling hides it, "memory-first" designs ration retrieval to once per turn. We argue latency is a property of where the store lives, not the in-loop pattern: an in-process store answers in ~100us, three orders of magnitude below the network regime, and at that speed the per-step tax collapses. By the extended-mind thesis's parity principle, a store fast enough to be constantly and directly available becomes extended working memory, not a tool the agent merely consults. The premise is causal: holding a fixed per-turn memory-latency budget and varying only the store's answer speed, redundant actions rise monotonically with latency - 0.0 of 12 at in-process speed, 7.2 of 12 at a 110ms cloud round trip (gpt-5-nano, gpt-5-mini; exact permutation p=0.0079). We demonstrate the regime end-to-end: across four GPT-5-class models under a bounded window, recall improves from 0/5 to 3.6-4.8/5 with in-loop memory, store ops at p50 80-165us - though an instructed restate-every-reply baseline also solves it perfectly, at a token cost that grows with the working set. The store never lost a fact in any run (244 of 244 writes kept); every miss traces to the agent's read policy, not the store. Our measurements also relocate the bottleneck: the dominant per-step cost is embedding (~200-400ms over the network); pairing the in-process store with a small local embedder returns the complete operation to a measured ~40us.

</details>


### [225] [Akashic: A Low-Overhead LLM Inference Service with MemAttention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05708)
*Yang Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Huayi Jin,Ruozhou He,Chenchen Hong,Zhiyong Wang,Yifei Liu,Yunfei Gu,Chentao Wu,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Akashic 是一种面向 LLM Agent 的低开销记忆系统，核心是 MemAttention 机制，将上下文组织为有界块并建模跨块语义关系，结合软硬件协同的内存放置策略，在保持证据的同时避免重复处理全量历史，显著提升任务精度、吞吐量和可持续请求率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 系统在多轮交互、工具调用和跨会话工作流中不断累积上下文，但每次请求都回放完整历史会导致 prefill 成本飙升、超出上下文窗口限制，且关键信息常被无关内容淹没，损害推理效率与输出质量。

Method: Akashic 包含两大设计：(1) MemAttention 机制，将上下文切分为有界块（bounded chunks），并建模跨块的语义关系以保留跨块证据，避免反复重写完整历史；(2) 软硬件协同的内存放置策略，将可能被共同检索的块物理上就近存放，减少检索碎片化和 I/O 开销。

Result: 在四个代表性工作负载和三种模型规模上，相比强基线记忆系统，Akashic 的任务准确率最高提升 10.2 个百分点，吞吐量最高提升 1.21 倍，可持续请求率最高提升 1.88 倍。

Conclusion: Akashic 通过分块记忆与语义关联建模，配合协同优化的内存布局，有效解决了长上下文 LLM Agent 的检索效率与证据保留之间的矛盾，在准确性和系统效率两个维度均实现了显著突破。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based agent systems continuously accumulate context across multi-turn interactions, tool invocations, and cross-session workflows. Replaying the full history for every request quickly becomes impractical: long contexts increase prefill cost, may exceed context limits, and often bury task-relevant evidence in irrelevant content, degrading both serving efficiency and output quality. We propose Akashic, a low-overhead memory system built around MemAttention, which organizes context into bounded chunks and models semantic relationships across chunks, preserving cross-chunk evidence without repeatedly rewriting the full history. Akashic further applies hardware-software co-designed memory placement to co-locate likely co-retrieved chunks, reducing retrieval fragmentation and I/O overhead. Across four representative workloads and three model sizes, Akashic improves task accuracy by up to 10.2 points, throughput by up to 1.21x, and sustainable request rate by up to 1.88x over strong prior memory baselines.

</details>


### [226] [ArtisanCAD: An Industrial-Level CAD Agent with Expert-Grounded Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05750)
*Yunhan Xu,Qifeng Wu,Xunjin Li,Yuanwei Bin,Qingsong Yao,Jianghang Gu,Guan Wang,Weihao Lv,Huiyu Yang,Wenfa Luo,Jiao Xiang,Yuntian Chen,Shiyi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAD-Ignite，一个面向工业级CAD的技能引导智能体。其核心是CAD-IR（CAD中间表示），一种可执行的过程表示，既能将专家CAD流程蒸馏为可复用的参数化技能，又能为模糊提示提供过程化支架，生成完整的可执行CAD操作。在Text2CAD基准上，CAD-IR将中间级提示的Chamfer距离从14.83降至9.88；在四个复杂汽车零部件上，成功实现专家CATIA录制到可复用技能的蒸馏与新变体生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有text-to-CAD方法在用户提示模糊、欠指定或仅描述高层设计意图时表现不佳，且很少利用工业工作流中天然存在的专家过程性知识（如CATIA操作录制、宏日志等）。工业CAD需要长程过程建模、鲁棒特征依赖、可编辑参数化几何以及生产级B-Rep执行，已有方法在这些方面存在明显不足。

Method: 核心是CAD-IR（CAD中间表示），一种可执行过程表示，编码参数、有序操作、MCP工具绑定、依赖关系、生成实体和验证规则。CAD-IR承担双重角色：(1) 作为将专家CAD流程蒸馏为可复用参数化技能的载体；(2) 提供过程化支架，将模糊或中间级提示转化为完整可执行CAD操作。系统通过CATIA-MCP后端执行过程，利用多视图视觉反馈进行迭代优化，最终生成生产级B-Rep模型。

Result: 在Text2CAD基准上，CAD-IR将中间级提示生成的Chamfer距离从14.83降至9.88，显著缩小模糊文本意图与可执行CAD构建之间的差距。在四个复杂汽车零部件的案例中，CAD-IR成功将专家CATIA录制蒸馏为可复用技能，使系统能针对新变体请求生成可编辑的CATIA原生B-Rep模型。

Conclusion: CAD-Ignite通过CAD-IR这一创新中间表示，有效弥合了模糊文本意图与精确可执行CAD操作之间的鸿沟，同时实现了专家知识的系统化蒸馏与复用，为工业级text-to-CAD提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Computer-aided design (CAD) for industrial components requires long-horizon procedural modeling, robust feature dependencies, editable parametric geometry, and production-grade B-Rep execution. Existing text-to-CAD methods have made promising progress in generating CAD programs from natural-language descriptions, but they still struggle when user prompts are ambiguous, underspecified, or only describe high-level design intent. They also rarely exploit expert procedural knowledge naturally available in industrial workflows, such as CATIA operation recordings, macro logs, drawing notes, and engineering descriptions. We present \algname, a skill-guided industrial CAD agent with expert-grounded knowledge distillation. The core of \algname is CAD intermediate representation (CAD-IR), an executable procedural representation that encodes parameters, ordered operations, MCP tool bindings, dependencies, generated entities, and verification rules. CAD-IR plays two key roles: it first serves as the carrier for distilling expert CAD procedures into reusable parameterized skills; then it provides a procedural scaffold that turns vague or intermediate-level prompts into complete executable CAD operations. \algname retrieves expert-derived skills, instantiates and revises CAD-IR, executes the resulting procedure through a dedicated CATIA-MCP backend, and uses multi-view visual feedback for iterative refinement, and finally generates production-ready B-Rep models. On the Text2CAD benchmark, CAD-IR improves generation from intermediate prompts by reducing mean Chamfer Distance from $14.83$ to $9.88$, showing its ability to bridge ambiguous textual intent and executable CAD construction. On four complex automotive components, CAD-IR enables expert CATIA recordings to be distilled into reusable skills, allowing \algname to generate editable CATIA-native B-Rep models for new variant requests.

</details>


### [227] [Synthetic Consumer Insight Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05761)
*Stephen L. France,Pia. A. Albinsson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用大语言模型（LLMs）生成合成消费者数据用于投射技术的可行性。通过对比人类与LLM在多项投射任务中的回答，发现两者在宏观主题上高度重叠，但在文体、语言结构和多样性生成方式上存在显著差异，并据此提出了使用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据驱动营销依赖大量消费者数据，但采集真实数据成本高、耗时长且难以规模化。作者希望探究LLMs能否作为一种高效替代方案，为投射技术（一种挖掘消费者联想、情感与需求的方法）生成合成数据。

Method: 研究者以城市旅游目的地感知为主题开展初步研究，收集人类回答作为基准。随后在多个投射任务中，系统性地测试了不同LLM、不同提示策略（prompting strategies）及不同温度参数（temperature settings）下生成的回答，采用语言测量、多样性与集中度指标、主题模型和关键词分析等方法，对人类与LLM回答进行多维度比较。

Result: 人类与LLM生成的数据在宏观主题和联想层面表现出显著重叠，但在写作风格、语言结构以及多样性生成机制上存在重要差异。模型选择与提示策略会显著影响回答质量。

Conclusion: LLM可用于生成合成消费者数据以辅助投射技术研究，但需注意其局限性。论文就如何优化LLM使用、如何根据模型与提示策略调校回答质量，以及如何识别合成数据生成的边界提出了具体建议。

Abstract: Modern data-driven marketing relies on large amounts of consumer data, yet collecting such data can be costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. This research examines whether large language models (LLMs) can be used to generate synthetic consumer data for projective techniques, a set of methods designed to elicit consumer associations, emotions, wants, and needs. We test LLM-generated responses across multiple projective tasks, LLMs, prompting strategies, and temperature settings, and compare them with human responses from a primary research study on perceptions of city tourism destinations. Human and LLM responses were analyzed using linguistic measures, diversity and concentration metrics, topic models, and top-term analyses. The results show substantial overlap between human and LLM responses in broad topics and associations, but also important differences in style, linguistic structure, and the way diversity is generated. Recommendations are given on how to best utilize LLMs for generating synthetic consumer data, how model and prompt choices shape response quality, and on recognizing the limitations of LLM synthetic consumer data generation.

</details>


### [228] [Beyond Static Evaluation: Building Simulation Environments for Scalable Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05773)
*Akshay Arora,Ishan Nigam,Ashutosh Aggarwal,Shefali Bansal,Krishna Singh,Sweta Kumari,Nikhil Mittal,Shariq Farhan,Siddarth Malreddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍AgenticAI-Supervisor，一个基于API和UI驱动的RL Gym环境，用于评估和优化LLM自主Agent。该平台通过可验证的执行结果、多维奖励塑造和内部状态验证来缓解奖励黑客问题，并以客户支持Agent为例展示了闭环反馈优化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM向自主Agent演进，传统的静态评估方法无法有效捕捉多步决策过程的性能。需要一种支持可扩展执行、能生成高保真轨迹并提供可靠奖励信号的动态评估框架。

Method: 提出AgenticAI-Supervisor平台：1）将环境创建与可扩展执行解耦的API/UI驱动RL Gym环境；2）基于可验证执行结果生成高保真轨迹；3）应用多维奖励塑造（multi-dimensional reward shaping）；4）通过严格的内部状态验证与测试来缓解奖励黑客（reward hacking）问题。

Result: 通过客户支持Agent案例研究，展示了平台的核心能力，证明了该框架能够实现一致的闭环反馈，用于模型优化。

Conclusion: AgenticAI-Supervisor为LLM Agent的评估与优化提供了一种可扩展、防奖励黑客的闭环方案。未来将扩展至计算机操作（Computer Use）、工具使用（Tool Use）、自动化"难倒"（stumping）和边缘案例生成等高级功能。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous agents, traditional static evaluation fails to capture multi-step decision-making. We introduce AgenticAI-Supervisor, an API and UI-driven RL Gym environment that decouples environment creation from scalable execution. By moving to verifiable execution outcomes, the platform generates high-fidelity traces and applies multi-dimensional reward shaping. Critically, our framework mitigates reward hacking through rigorous internal state validation and testing. This work provides a first look at our platform's core capabilities through a Customer Support Agent case study demonstrating a consistent closed-loop feedback for model optimization. Future work will focus on advanced features such as Computer Use, Tool Use, automated "stumping", and edge-case generation.

</details>


### [229] [Beyond the Leaderboard: A Synthesis of Tool-Use, Planning, and Reasoning Failures in Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05775)
*Wael Albayaydh,Rui Zhao,Ivan Flechais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对2023-2026年间27篇涉及LLM智能体的基准、分类和审计论文进行了综合分析，首次将工具使用、规划、长时域推理、多智能体协调、安全性及测量效度等维度的证据整合为统一的六类失效分类体系，揭示了LLM智能体失败随任务长度非线性复合、子任务强表现不必然转化为端到端成功、以及额外脚手架并不持续提升可靠性等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体的基准评估报告中的增益往往掩盖了跨不同评估工作中反复出现的失败模式。现有文献缺乏一个将这些分散的失效证据整合起来的统一框架，因而亟需构建一个跨工具使用、规划、长时域推理、多智能体协调、安全性和测量效度的综合分类体系。

Method: 采用迭代式文献综合方法：收集2023-2026年间27篇基准、分类和审计论文（覆盖19个不同基准），将独立报告的错误类别按智能体"推理到行动"流水线的不同阶段进行主题分组，提炼出六大失效簇，形成交叉统一的分类体系。

Result: 识别出六大失效簇：(1)工具调用与参数级错误；(2)规划与约束满足失败；(3)上下文累积导致的长时域退化；(4)多智能体协调失败；(5)对抗性或欠规范条件下的安全与安保失败；(6)测量效度问题。核心发现包括：失败随任务长度呈非线性复合增长；单子任务上的强劲表现不可靠地转化为端到端成功；额外脚手架并未持续提升可靠性。同时，单轮工具使用、短时域网页导航和窄范围编码任务方面已取得实质性进展。

Conclusion: LLM智能体在单轮工具使用和短时域任务上进展显著，但失败模式随任务复杂度非线性加剧，子任务级成功不能保证整体可靠性，且当前脚手架方案效果不稳定。该统一分类体系为后续智能体鲁棒性研究与评估提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly evaluated on their ability to use tools, plan multi-step tasks, coordinate with other agents, and operate over extended horizons. Reported benchmark gains often obscure recurring failure modes documented across otherwise unrelated evaluation efforts. This paper synthesizes 27 benchmark, taxonomy, and audit papers (2023-2026), spanning 19 distinct benchmarks, into a cross-cutting taxonomy of agent limitations. To our knowledge, this is the first synthesis that integrates evidence across tool use, planning, long-horizon reasoning, multi-agent coordination, safety, and measurement validity into a single, unified taxonomy of LLM agent limitations. We identify six failure clusters: (1) tool invocation and parameter-level errors, (2) planning and constraint-satisfaction failures, (3) long-horizon degradation from context accumulation, (4) multi-agent coordination failures, (5) safety and security failures under adversarial or underspecified conditions, and (6) measurement validity problems. The taxonomy was derived iteratively by grouping independently reported error categories into themes corresponding to distinct stages of the agent reasoning-to-action pipeline. Across the literature, we find that failures compound nonlinearly with task length, that strong performance on individual sub-tasks does not reliably translate into end-to-end success, and that additional scaffolding does not consistently improve reliability. At the same time, substantial progress has been demonstrated in single-turn tool use, short-horizon web navigation, and narrowly scoped coding tasks.

</details>


### [230] [Controlling Tool Use with Heading-Specific Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05790)
*Yuqi Chen,Vincent Siu,Yang Liu,Dawn Song,Chenguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了工具增强大语言模型中工具调用决策的内部表征。作者发现，从头锚点位置提取的导向向量可对工具调用行为产生双向因果控制，能有效抑制不必要的工具调用。然而，几何分析揭示这种因果效应并不对应清晰的线性结构：工具调用步骤呈现弥散的双峰对齐模式，且不同工具类型具有低重叠的内部特征签名。作者推测这些几何特性反映了工具的非参数化本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强的大语言模型倾向于不必要地调用外部工具。研究者希望探究：工具调用决策在模型内部是否具有可被提取和操控的稳定表征？这一问题的重要性在于，工具仅在推理时的上下文中存在，并未直接编码在模型权重中，因此其内部表征的存在性和性质均不显然。

Method: 1. 从模型隐层表示的头锚点（heading-anchors）位置提取导向向量（steering vectors）；2. 通过加/减导向向量实现对工具调用行为的双向因果操控；3. 在五个开源模型和三个领域上验证有效性；4. 进行几何分析，考察导向向量与工具调用步骤的对齐模式（双峰对齐）以及不同工具类型的特征重叠度。

Result: 1. 导向向量对工具调用行为具有双向因果控制能力，在参数推理已足够的领域中，抑制不必要工具调用的效果最佳；2. 工具调用步骤与抑制向量的对齐呈弥散的双峰分布，而非线性编码假说所预测的一致的负对齐；3. 不同工具类型激活的内部表征差异显著，跨工具特征重叠度低。

Conclusion: 工具调用决策虽可被导向向量因果操控，但其内部几何结构并不符合清晰的线性编码。作者假设这种几何不规则性反映了工具的非参数化本质，并将其与参数化概念的表征区分开来。因果有效性与几何不规则性之间的关系仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language models extend their capabilities beyond parametric knowledge through external tools, but tend to invoke them unnecessarily. We investigate whether tool-use decisions have any stable internal representation that can be extracted and manipulated, a question that is non-trivial given that tools exist entirely in context at inference time and have no direct encoding in model weights. We show that steering vectors extracted from heading-anchors positions exert bidirectional causal control over tool-invocation behavior across five open-source models and three domains, suppressing unnecessary tool use most effectively in domains where parametric reasoning suffices. However, geometric analysis reveals that this causal effectiveness does not correspond to clean linear structure: tool-invocation steps exhibit diffuse, bimodal alignment with the suppression vector rather than the consistent negative alignment a linear encoding account would predict, and different tool types recruit largely distinct internal signatures with low cross-tool feature overlap. We hypothesize these geometric properties are indicative of the non-parametric nature of tools, and distinguish tool-use steering vectors from those extracted for parametrically grounded concepts. The relationship between this geometric irregularity and the observed causal effectiveness remains an open question.

</details>


### [231] [From Passive Retrieval to Active Memory Navigation: Learning to Use Memory as a Structured Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05794)
*Yue Xu,Yutao Sun,Yihao Liu,Mengyu Zhou,Jiayi Qiao,Lu Ma,Kai Tang,Wenjie Wang,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NapMem 提出将长期用户记忆构建为结构化动作空间（记忆金字塔），而非被动检索上下文，通过记忆工具和强化学习训练智能体在回答前主动选择合适粒度的记忆，在多个记忆密集型任务上表现优异，同时保持了通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话系统的长期记忆大多采用被动检索接口，模型仅能消费预先检索到的证据，缺乏主动、灵活地按需探查不同粒度记忆的能力。这限制了记忆利用的效率和个性化对话的质量。

Method: NapMem 框架将用户历史组织为链接的多粒度记忆金字塔：原始对话、类型化记忆记录、话题轨迹和用户画像，通过溯源关系连接各层，并以记忆工具的形式暴露。智能体通过记忆工具强化学习（memory-tool RL）训练，根据查询和中间证据自主选择探查哪一层记忆，在回答前完成记忆导航与筛选。

Result: 在 PersonaMem-v2、LongMemEval 和 LoCoMo 三个基准上的实验表明，经过记忆工具 RL 训练的 NapMem 智能体在多种记忆密集型任务上具有竞争力，同时在非记忆任务上基本保持了通用推理和工具使用能力。消融实验还分析了存储、推理成本、工具使用行为，以及导航、记忆粒度和 RL 训练的影响。

Conclusion: 长期用户记忆系统不仅需要结构化存储，更需要一种学习策略来在合适的粒度上使用记忆。将结构化存储与学习型记忆调用策略相结合，是提升个性化对话智能体的有效路径。

Abstract: Long-term user memory is essential for personalized conversational agents, yet many memory systems still expose memory through passive retrieval interfaces, making the model a consumer of pre-selected evidence. We introduce NapMem, a framework for learning to use long-term user memory as a structured action space rather than passively retrieved context. NapMem organizes user history into a linked multi-granularity memory pyramid, where raw conversations, typed memory records, topic tracks, and user profiles are connected through provenance relations, and exposes these levels through memory tools. The agent is trained to select memory according to the query and intermediate evidence, allowing it to inspect different memory granularities before answering. Experiments on PersonaMem-v2, LongMemEval, and LoCoMo show that a NapMem agent trained with memory-tool reinforcement learning is competitive across diverse memory-intensive tasks, while evaluations on non-memory tasks suggest that the learned policy largely preserves general reasoning and tool-use abilities. Additional analyses examine storage, inference cost, tool-use behavior, and ablations over navigation, memory granularity, and RL training. Our results suggest that long-term user memory benefits from coupling structured storage with a learned policy for using memory at the appropriate granularity.

</details>


### [232] [TurnOPD: Making On-Policy Distillation Turn-Aware for Efficient Long-Horizon Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05804)
*Yuhang Zhou,Kai Zheng,Haoling Li,Dengyun Peng,Can Xu,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TurnOPD 提出基于回合级预算策略的高效在线蒸馏方法，通过自适应 rollout 深度预算和渐进式回合归一化损失预算，解决长周期智能体在线蒸馏中尾部回合资源浪费和浅层 token 主导损失的问题，在等量训练时间下取得更优的验证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在长周期智能体任务中存在两个核心低效问题：一是全周期 rollout 在尾部回合上浪费计算资源，因为这些回合提供的 KL 监督信号弱且噪声大；二是轨迹级 KL 目标使大部分损失集中在浅层 token 上，导致深层决策回合在初始行为对齐后训练不足。

Method: TurnOPD 引入两个预算控制器：（1）自适应 rollout 深度预算——利用探针式回合统计量动态决定 rollout 终止长度，避免在无效尾部回合上浪费资源；（2）渐进式回合归一化损失预算——逐步将 KL 损失的权重从 token 级别平滑转移到回合均衡的监督上，使深层决策回合获得充分训练。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 Multi-Hop Search 三个长周期智能体任务上，使用任务专用教师模型进行实验，TurnOPD 在同等 wall-clock 训练预算下取得了优于 vanilla OPD 的验证准确率，并推进了精度-时间前沿边界。

Conclusion: TurnOPD 通过回合级预算策略有效提升了长周期智能体在线蒸馏的效率，证明了在资源受限条件下，精细化的 rollout 深度控制和损失加权策略能够显著改善学生策略的学习效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student policy by matching a stronger teacher on the student's own trajectories, offering a promising framework for language agent training. However, its application to long-horizon agentic tasks remains insufficiently explored. We identify two key inefficiencies in vanilla agent OPD: (1) full-horizon rollouts often waste wall-clock resources on tail turns that provide weak and noisy KL supervision, and (2) trajectory-level KL objectives concentrate most of the loss on shallow tokens, leaving deeper decision turns under-trained once initial behaviors are aligned. To address these challenges, we propose TurnOPD, a turn-level budgeting strategy for efficient on-policy distillation of long-horizon agents. TurnOPD consists of two budget controllers: adaptive rollout-depth budgeting, which uses probe-based turn statistics to determine rollout length, and progressive turn-normalized loss budgeting, which gradually shifts KL weighting from token-level to turn-balanced supervision. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Multi-Hop Search with task-specialized teacher models show that TurnOPD achieves superior validation accuracy under equal wall-clock training budgets and advances the accuracy--time frontier beyond vanilla OPD.

</details>


### [233] [Onnes: A Physics-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Simulator for Cryogenic Fault Diagnosis in Quantum Computing Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05805)
*Praneeth Narisetty,Uday Kumar Reddy Kattamanchi,Shiva Nagendra Babu Kore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Onnes系统，一个基于物理的数字孪生稀释制冷机模拟器，结合多智能体LLM操作层，用于超导量子计算机低温故障诊断。零样本LLM智能体面板在检测上与监督ML分类器无显著差异，但在分类上落后；通过精心设计的对比少样本示例和自一致性投票，分类准确率从0.685提升至0.990，匹配监督分类器的0.985。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前稀释制冷机故障诊断仍以阈值报警为主，只能报告"出了故障"而无法指出"是什么故障"。需要一种能识别具体故障类型的智能诊断系统，同时探索LLM在物理系统故障诊断中的能力。

Method: 构建Onnes系统：(1) 物理驱动的数字孪生模拟器，耦合真实稀释制冷循环、从BlueFors日志学习的噪声与相关性指纹，以及六类物理故障（其中三类在温度上重叠但在流量和压力上可区分）；(2) 多智能体LLM操作层负责诊断；(3) 对比零样本LLM面板与监督ML分类器；(4) 引入精心设计的对比少样本示例和自一致性投票机制提升分类性能；(5) 连续监控实验和sim-to-real验证。

Result: 零样本LLM面板在1000轮评估中检测能力与监督分类器无显著差异，但分类准确率较低，错误集中在易混淆故障上。通过少样本示例和自一致性投票，分类准确率从0.685升至0.990，匹配监督分类器的0.985，且无需参数更新仅需6个标注示例。消融实验表明增益几乎完全来自示例。连续监控中智能体在一个轮询间隔内捕获所有发展中的故障，置信门抑制了误报。真实硬件验证：仅在真实BlueFors遥测数据上训练的检测器在真实硬件上误报率6.4%，对注入故障的召回率100%。

Conclusion: Onnes系统展示了物理数字孪生与LLM多智能体结合的低温故障诊断方案，少样本提示可大幅提升LLM分类性能至监督学习水平，为量子计算基础设施的智能运维提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dilution refrigerators are the enabling infrastructure of superconducting quantum computers, yet their fault diagnosis is still dominated by threshold alarms that report that something is wrong, not what. We present Onnes, a physics-grounded digital-twin simulator of a dilution refrigerator (a forward physics model with a learned real-fridge noise fingerprint) that drives a live multi-agent LLM operations layer, and use it for a controlled head-to-head between a zero-shot LLM agent panel and a supervised ML classifier on cryogenic fault diagnosis. The twin couples a real dilution-cooling floor, a noise-and-correlation fingerprint learned from real BlueFors logs, and six physics-grounded fault classes, three engineered to overlap on temperature but separate on flow and pressure. Across a 1000-turn evaluation the zero-shot panel shows no significant difference from the classifier on detection but trails on classification, its errors concentrating on the confusable faults. Curated contrastive few-shot demonstrations and self-consistency voting then raise classification accuracy from 0.685 to 0.990, matching the supervised classifier (0.985) with no parameter updates and six labeled demonstrations; an ablation attributes the gain almost entirely to the demonstrations. Run as a continuous monitor across a nine-run fault-by-seed sweep, the agent catches every developing fault within one poll interval, and a confidence gate suppresses pre-onset false alarms whose rate is backend-dependent. As a first sim-to-real check, a detector trained purely on real BlueFors telemetry posts a real-hardware false-alarm rate of 6.4% and 100% recall on physics faults injected onto real held-out windows. All numbers are drawn verbatim from released run logs.

</details>


### [234] [StateFuse: Deterministic Conflict-Preserving Memory for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05844)
*Sergey Volkov,Yang Li,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StateFuse 提出了一种基于 OpSet/CRDT 合并的冲突感知复制内存契约，通过不可变历史、显式冲突对象、精确与语义校正句柄、确定性谓词契约及投影时解析，在不引入新合并代数的前提下，确保矛盾可见、可审计，并在受控 agent 循环中实现更安全的弃权与校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent 系统的记忆层在多分支、重试和副本场景下积累冲突观察，但普遍采用覆盖规则隐性消解分歧，导致冲突不可见且难以检查与纠正。作者旨在提供一种冲突保留的内存契约，使矛盾持续可见且可审计。

Method: 基于标准 OpSet/CRDT 合并构建 agent 语义层：不可变历史记录、显式冲突对象、claim_id/claim_ref 双重校正句柄（精确匹配与语义匹配）、确定性谓词契约约束状态访问，以及投影时解析（不覆写已复制状态）。

Result: 在 MemoryAgentBench 的 282 题冲突切片上，各方法答案准确率持平，但 StateFuse 保持矛盾可见而折叠基线则否；在统一验证的 agent 循环中，保留歧义比早期折叠更安全地支持弃权与校正；校正句柄消融实验表明语义句柄在缺乏精确标识符时发挥作用。

Conclusion: StateFuse 的贡献窄而明确：最适合作为更安全的公共内存契约，用于矛盾浮现、安全弃权和可审计校正，而非追求通用准确率提升。

Abstract: Agent systems accumulate conflicting observations across branches, retries, and replicas, yet many practical memory layers still collapse disagreement behind overwrite rules that are difficult to inspect or correct. We present StateFuse, a conflict-aware replicated memory contract built on standard OpSet/CRDT merge. StateFuse does not introduce a new join algebra; it defines an agent-facing semantics layer with immutable history, explicit conflict objects, exact and semantic correction handles (claim_id / claim_ref), deterministic predicate contracts, and projection-time resolution that cannot rewrite replicated state.
  We evaluate StateFuse against flat multi-value, raw-log, provenance-style, and collapsed baselines under matched resolver and verification policies. On a 282-question official conflict-bearing MemoryAgentBench slice, the compared methods tie on answer accuracy, but conflict-preserving surfaces keep contradictions visible while collapsed surfaces do not. In a controlled agent loop with uniform verification, preserving ambiguity enables safer abstention and correction than early collapse. A correction-handle ablation further shows that semantic handles matter when exact prior identifiers are unavailable.
  The resulting claim is narrow: StateFuse is best supported as a safer public memory contract for contradiction surfacing, abstention, and auditable correction, not as a universal accuracy gain.

</details>


### [235] [Uncovering Latent Depression Severity for Binary Depression Detection via Advantage-weighting Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05901)
*Manning Gao,Tingyi Liu,Leheng Zhang,Haifeng Hu,Yuncheng Jiang,Sijie Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于细粒度多模态融合的抑郁症检测框架，核心创新是二元优势加权排序损失（Binary Advantage-weighting Ranking Loss），通过优势加权分离和优势加权紧凑性两个互补机制优化潜在空间分布，在D-vlog和LMVD数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于音视频的自动抑郁症检测面临两大核心挑战：一是重叠的特征分布难以有效解耦，二是鲁棒的决策边界难以建立。现有方法在处理困难样本对和保持类内紧凑性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出细粒度多模态框架，包含时序编码器（temporal encoder）和互Transformer（mutual transformer）用于深层跨模态融合。核心贡献为二元优势加权排序损失（Binary Advantage-weighting Ranking Loss），由两个互补机制组成：（1）优势加权分离（Advantage-weighted Separation）：通过计算成对预测差异矩阵挖掘困难样本对，并根据难度动态加权；（2）优势加权紧凑性（Advantage-weighted Compactness）：最小化类内方差，迫使特征围绕各自类中心聚集。

Result: 在D-vlog和LMVD两个数据集上进行广泛实验，所提模型通过优先处理困难样本对，成功重建了潜在的序数结构，取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 二元优势加权排序损失有效解决了特征分布重叠和决策边界模糊的问题，通过困难样本挖掘和类内紧凑性约束，使模型在抑郁症检测任务上达到当前最优水平，验证了该方法在潜在序数结构重建方面的有效性。

Abstract: Automatic depression detection using audio-visual data faces significant challenges, particularly in disentangling overlapping feature distributions and establishing robust decision boundaries. To address this, we propose a fine-grained multimodal framework featuring a temporal encoder and a mutual transformer to facilitate deep cross-modal fusion. Our core contribution is the Binary Advantage-weighting Ranking Loss, which optimizes the latent space distribution through two complementary mechanisms: Advantage-weighted Separation, which mines hard pairs by computing a pairwise prediction difference matrix and dynamically weighting them based on their difficulty; and Advantage-weighted Compactness, which minimizes intra-class variance to force features to cluster around their respective class centers. Extensive experiments on D-vlog and LMVD demonstrate that our model reconstructs the latent ordinal structure by prioritizing hard pairs, thereby achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [236] [PCBWorld: A Benchmark Environment for Engine-Grounded PCB Design Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05915)
*Hyungseok Song,Junseok Park,Won-Seok Choi,Seohui Bae,Han-Seul Jeong,Youngjoon Park,Soonyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PCBWorld 是一个基于 KiCad EDA 引擎的开源 PCB 布线环境，支持 RL 和 LLM 智能体通过引擎原生操作交互式完成布线，并利用 DRC 反馈保证设计规则合规。配套的 PCBWorld-Bench 提供合成与真实板级数据集及八项引擎校验指标。实验表明，该环境下训练的智能体优于网格动作 RL 和开环 LLM 基线，且仅在合成数据上训练的 RL 策略可零样本迁移至真实电路板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于学习的 PCB 布线方法仍落后于基于规则的布线器，现有学习环境通常将布线简化为网格动作空间，缺乏与真实 EDA 引擎的交互及设计规则检查（DRC）反馈，限制了 RL 和 LLM 智能体的实际布线能力。

Method: 作者构建了 PCBWorld 环境，直接对接 KiCad 引擎，智能体通过引擎原生操作（如放置走线、过孔等）进行交互式布线，并实时接收 DRC 反馈以确保合规。环境同时支持 RL 策略和工具调用型 LLM 智能体。PCBWorld-Bench 包含三类数据集：两种可控合成实例和 679 块真实开源电路板，采用八项引擎校验指标（如布线完成率、DRC 违规数等）统一评分。

Result: 在 PCBWorld 中训练的智能体一致优于网格动作空间的 RL 策略和开环 LLM 基线；仅在合成板上训练的 RL 策略实现了向真实电路板的零样本迁移，性能接近基于规则的布线器。

Conclusion: PCBWorld 的引擎接地、交互式方法为提升 RL 和 LLM 智能体的 PCB 布线能力提供了有前景的基础平台。
